8.34.9	ā tvā̍ mada̱cyutā̱ harī̍ śye̱nam pa̱kṣeva̍ vakṣataḥ | di̱vo a̱muṣya̱ śāsa̍to̱ diva̍ṁ ya̱ya di̍vāvaso ||
8.34.10	ā yā̍hy a̱rya ā pari̱ svāhā̱ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ | di̱vo a̱muṣya̱ śāsa̍to̱ diva̍ṁ ya̱ya di̍vāvaso ||
8.34.11	ā no̍ yā̱hy upa̍śruty u̱ktheṣu̍ raṇayā i̱ha | di̱vo a̱muṣya̱ śāsa̍to̱ diva̍ṁ ya̱ya di̍vāvaso ||
8.34.12	sarū̍pai̱r ā su no̍ gahi̱ sambhṛ̍tai̱ḥ sambhṛ̍tāśvaḥ | di̱vo a̱muṣya̱ śāsa̍to̱ diva̍ṁ ya̱ya di̍vāvaso ||
8.34.13	ā yā̍hi̱ parva̍tebhyaḥ samu̱drasyādhi̍ vi̱ṣṭapa̍ḥ | di̱vo a̱muṣya̱ śāsa̍to̱ diva̍ṁ ya̱ya di̍vāvaso ||
8.34.14	ā no̱ gavyā̱ny aśvyā̍ sa̱hasrā̍ śūra dardṛhi | di̱vo a̱muṣya̱ śāsa̍to̱ diva̍ṁ ya̱ya di̍vāvaso ||
8.34.15	ā na̍ḥ sahasra̱śo bha̍rā̱yutā̍ni śa̱tāni̍ ca | di̱vo a̱muṣya̱ śāsa̍to̱ diva̍ṁ ya̱ya di̍vāvaso ||
8.34.16	ā yad indra̍ś ca̱ dadva̍he sa̱hasra̱ṁ vasu̍rociṣaḥ | oji̍ṣṭha̱m aśvya̍m pa̱śum ||
8.34.17	ya ṛ̱jrā vāta̍raṁhaso 'ru̱ṣāso̍ raghu̱ṣyada̍ḥ | bhrāja̍nte̱ sūryā̍ iva ||
8.34.18	pārā̍vatasya rā̱tiṣu̍ dra̱vacca̍kreṣv ā̱śuṣu̍ | tiṣṭha̱ṁ vana̍sya̱ madhya̱ ā ||
8.35.1	a̱gninendre̍ṇa̱ varu̍ṇena̱ viṣṇu̍nādi̱tyai ru̱drair vasu̍bhiḥ sacā̱bhuvā̍ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍m pibatam aśvinā ||
8.35.2	viśvā̍bhir dhī̱bhir bhuva̍nena vājinā di̱vā pṛ̍thi̱vyādri̍bhiḥ sacā̱bhuvā̍ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍m pibatam aśvinā ||
8.35.3	viśvai̍r de̱vais tri̱bhir e̍kāda̱śair i̱hādbhir ma̱rudbhi̱r bhṛgu̍bhiḥ sacā̱bhuvā̍ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍m pibatam aśvinā ||
8.35.4	ju̱ṣethā̍ṁ ya̱jñam bodha̍ta̱ṁ hava̍sya me̱ viśve̱ha de̍vau̱ sava̱nāva̍ gacchatam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ ceṣa̍ṁ no voḻham aśvinā ||
8.35.5	stoma̍ṁ juṣethāṁ yuva̱śeva̍ ka̱nyanā̱ṁ viśve̱ha de̍vau̱ sava̱nāva̍ gacchatam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ ceṣa̍ṁ no voḻham aśvinā ||
8.35.6	giro̍ juṣethām adhva̱raṁ ju̍ṣethā̱ṁ viśve̱ha de̍vau̱ sava̱nāva̍ gacchatam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ ceṣa̍ṁ no voḻham aśvinā ||
8.35.7	hā̱ri̱dra̱veva̍ patatho̱ vaned upa̱ soma̍ṁ su̱tam ma̍hi̱ṣevāva̍ gacchathaḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ trir va̱rtir yā̍tam aśvinā ||
8.35.8	ha̱ṁsāv i̍va patatho adhva̱gāv i̍va̱ soma̍ṁ su̱tam ma̍hi̱ṣevāva̍ gacchathaḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ trir va̱rtir yā̍tam aśvinā ||
8.35.9	śye̱nāv i̍va patatho ha̱vyadā̍taye̱ soma̍ṁ su̱tam ma̍hi̱ṣevāva̍ gacchathaḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ trir va̱rtir yā̍tam aśvinā ||
8.35.10	piba̍taṁ ca tṛpṇu̱taṁ cā ca̍ gacchatam pra̱jāṁ ca̍ dha̱ttaṁ dravi̍ṇaṁ ca dhattam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ corja̍ṁ no dhattam aśvinā ||
8.35.11	jaya̍taṁ ca̱ pra stu̍taṁ ca̱ pra cā̍vatam pra̱jāṁ ca̍ dha̱ttaṁ dravi̍ṇaṁ ca dhattam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ corja̍ṁ no dhattam aśvinā ||
8.35.12	ha̱taṁ ca̱ śatrū̱n yata̍taṁ ca mi̱triṇa̍ḥ pra̱jāṁ ca̍ dha̱ttaṁ dravi̍ṇaṁ ca dhattam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ corja̍ṁ no dhattam aśvinā ||
8.35.13	mi̱trāvaru̍ṇavantā u̱ta dharma̍vantā ma̱rutva̍ntā jari̱tur ga̍cchatho̱ hava̍m | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa cādi̱tyair yā̍tam aśvinā ||
8.35.14	aṅgi̍rasvantā u̱ta viṣṇu̍vantā ma̱rutva̍ntā jari̱tur ga̍cchatho̱ hava̍m | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa cādi̱tyair yā̍tam aśvinā ||
8.35.15	ṛ̱bhu̱mantā̍ vṛṣaṇā̱ vāja̍vantā ma̱rutva̍ntā jari̱tur ga̍cchatho̱ hava̍m | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa cādi̱tyair yā̍tam aśvinā ||
8.35.16	brahma̍ jinvatam u̱ta ji̍nvata̱ṁ dhiyo̍ ha̱taṁ rakṣā̍ṁsi̱ sedha̍ta̱m amī̍vāḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍ṁ sunva̱to a̍śvinā ||
8.35.17	kṣa̱traṁ ji̍nvatam u̱ta ji̍nvata̱ṁ nṝn ha̱taṁ rakṣā̍ṁsi̱ sedha̍ta̱m amī̍vāḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍ṁ sunva̱to a̍śvinā ||
8.35.18	dhe̱nūr ji̍nvatam u̱ta ji̍nvata̱ṁ viśo̍ ha̱taṁ rakṣā̍ṁsi̱ sedha̍ta̱m amī̍vāḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍ṁ sunva̱to a̍śvinā ||
8.35.19	atre̍r iva śṛṇutam pū̱rvyastu̍tiṁ śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱to ma̍dacyutā | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ cāśvi̍nā ti̱roa̍hnyam ||
8.35.20	sargā̍m̐ iva sṛjataṁ suṣṭu̱tīr upa̍ śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱to ma̍dacyutā | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ cāśvi̍nā ti̱roa̍hnyam ||
8.35.21	ra̱śmīm̐r i̍va yacchatam adhva̱rām̐ upa̍ śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱to ma̍dacyutā | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ cāśvi̍nā ti̱roa̍hnyam ||
8.35.22	a̱rvāg ratha̱ṁ ni ya̍cchata̱m piba̍taṁ so̱myam madhu̍ | ā yā̍tam aśvi̱nā ga̍tam ava̱syur vā̍m a̱haṁ hu̍ve dha̱ttaṁ ratnā̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ ||
8.35.23	na̱mo̱vā̱ke prasthi̍te adhva̱re na̍rā vi̱vakṣa̍ṇasya pī̱taye̍ | ā yā̍tam aśvi̱nā ga̍tam ava̱syur vā̍m a̱haṁ hu̍ve dha̱ttaṁ ratnā̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ ||
8.35.24	svāhā̍kṛtasya tṛmpataṁ su̱tasya̍ devā̱v andha̍saḥ | ā yā̍tam aśvi̱nā ga̍tam ava̱syur vā̍m a̱haṁ hu̍ve dha̱ttaṁ ratnā̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ ||
8.36.1	a̱vi̱tāsi̍ sunva̱to vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣa̱ḥ pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||
8.36.2	prāva̍ sto̱tāra̍m maghava̱nn ava̱ tvām pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||
8.36.3	ū̱rjā de̱vām̐ ava̱sy oja̍sā̱ tvām pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||
8.36.4	ja̱ni̱tā di̱vo ja̍ni̱tā pṛ̍thi̱vyāḥ pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||
8.36.5	ja̱ni̱tāśvā̍nāṁ jani̱tā gavā̍m asi̱ pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||
8.36.6	atrī̍ṇā̱ṁ stoma̍m adrivo ma̱has kṛ̍dhi̱ pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||
8.36.7	śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱tas tathā̍ śṛṇu̱ yathāśṛ̍ṇo̱r atre̱ḥ karmā̍ṇi kṛṇva̱taḥ | pra tra̱sada̍syum āvitha̱ tvam eka̱ in nṛ̱ṣāhya̱ indra̱ brahmā̍ṇi va̱rdhaya̍n ||
8.37.1	predam brahma̍ vṛtra̱tūrye̍ṣv āvitha̱ pra su̍nva̱taḥ śa̍cīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||
8.37.2	se̱hā̱na u̍gra̱ pṛta̍nā a̱bhi druha̍ḥ śacīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||
8.37.3	e̱ka̱rāḻ a̱sya bhuva̍nasya rājasi śacīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||
8.37.4	sa̱sthāvā̍nā yavayasi̱ tvam eka̱ ic cha̍cīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||
8.37.5	kṣema̍sya ca pra̱yuja̍ś ca̱ tvam ī̍śiṣe śacīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||
8.37.6	kṣa̱trāya̍ tva̱m ava̍si̱ na tva̍m āvitha śacīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||
8.37.7	śyā̱vāśva̍sya̱ rebha̍ta̱s tathā̍ śṛṇu̱ yathāśṛ̍ṇo̱r atre̱ḥ karmā̍ṇi kṛṇva̱taḥ | pra tra̱sada̍syum āvitha̱ tvam eka̱ in nṛ̱ṣāhya̱ indra̍ kṣa̱trāṇi̍ va̱rdhaya̍n ||
8.38.1	ya̱jñasya̱ hi stha ṛ̱tvijā̱ sasnī̱ vāje̍ṣu̱ karma̍su | indrā̍gnī̱ tasya̍ bodhatam ||
8.38.2	to̱śāsā̍ ratha̱yāvā̍nā vṛtra̱haṇāpa̍rājitā | indrā̍gnī̱ tasya̍ bodhatam ||
8.38.3	i̱daṁ vā̍m madi̱ram madhv adhu̍kṣa̱nn adri̍bhi̱r nara̍ḥ | indrā̍gnī̱ tasya̍ bodhatam ||
8.38.4	ju̱ṣethā̍ṁ ya̱jñam i̱ṣṭaye̍ su̱taṁ soma̍ṁ sadhastutī | indrā̍gnī̱ ā ga̍taṁ narā ||
8.38.5	i̱mā ju̍ṣethā̱ṁ sava̍nā̱ yebhi̍r ha̱vyāny ū̱hathu̍ḥ | indrā̍gnī̱ ā ga̍taṁ narā ||
8.38.6	i̱māṁ gā̍ya̱trava̍rtaniṁ ju̱ṣethā̍ṁ suṣṭu̱tim mama̍ | indrā̍gnī̱ ā ga̍taṁ narā ||
8.38.7	prā̱ta̱ryāva̍bhi̱r ā ga̍taṁ de̱vebhi̍r jenyāvasū | indrā̍gnī̱ soma̍pītaye ||
8.38.8	śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱to 'trī̍ṇāṁ śṛṇuta̱ṁ hava̍m | indrā̍gnī̱ soma̍pītaye ||
8.38.9	e̱vā vā̍m ahva ū̱taye̱ yathāhu̍vanta̱ medhi̍rāḥ | indrā̍gnī̱ soma̍pītaye ||
8.38.10	āhaṁ sara̍svatīvator indrā̱gnyor avo̍ vṛṇe | yābhyā̍ṁ gāya̱tram ṛ̱cyate̍ ||
8.39.1	a̱gnim a̍stoṣy ṛ̱gmiya̍m a̱gnim ī̱ḻā ya̱jadhyai̍ | a̱gnir de̱vām̐ a̍naktu na u̱bhe hi vi̱dathe̍ ka̱vir a̱ntaś cara̍ti dū̱tya1̱̍ṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.2	ny a̍gne̱ navya̍sā̱ vaca̍s ta̱nūṣu̱ śaṁsa̍m eṣām | ny arā̍tī̱ rarā̍vṇā̱ṁ viśvā̍ a̱ryo arā̍tīr i̱to yu̍cchantv ā̱muro̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.3	agne̱ manmā̍ni̱ tubhya̱ṁ kaṁ ghṛ̱taṁ na ju̍hva ā̱sani̍ | sa de̱veṣu̱ pra ci̍kiddhi̱ tvaṁ hy asi̍ pū̱rvyaḥ śi̱vo dū̱to vi̱vasva̍to̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.4	tat-ta̍d a̱gnir vayo̍ dadhe̱ yathā̍-yathā kṛpa̱ṇyati̍ | ū̱rjāhu̍ti̱r vasū̍nā̱ṁ śaṁ ca̱ yoś ca̱ mayo̍ dadhe̱ viśva̍syai de̱vahū̍tyai̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.5	sa ci̍keta̱ sahī̍yasā̱gniś ci̱treṇa̱ karma̍ṇā | sa hotā̱ śaśva̍tīnā̱ṁ dakṣi̍ṇābhir a̱bhīvṛ̍ta i̱noti̍ ca pratī̱vya1̱̍ṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.6	a̱gnir jā̱tā de̱vānā̍m a̱gnir ve̍da̱ martā̍nām apī̱cya̍m | a̱gniḥ sa dra̍viṇo̱dā a̱gnir dvārā̱ vy ū̍rṇute̱ svā̍huto̱ navī̍yasā̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.7	a̱gnir de̱veṣu̱ saṁva̍su̱ḥ sa vi̱kṣu ya̱jñiyā̱sv ā | sa mu̱dā kāvyā̍ pu̱ru viśva̱m bhūme̍va puṣyati de̱vo de̱veṣu̍ ya̱jñiyo̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.8	yo a̱gniḥ sa̱ptamā̍nuṣaḥ śri̱to viśve̍ṣu̱ sindhu̍ṣu | tam āga̍nma tripa̱styam ma̍ndhā̱tur da̍syu̱hanta̍mam a̱gniṁ ya̱jñeṣu̍ pū̱rvyaṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.9	a̱gnis trīṇi̍ tri̱dhātū̱ny ā kṣe̍ti vi̱dathā̍ ka̱viḥ | sa trīm̐r e̍kāda̱śām̐ i̱ha yakṣa̍c ca pi̱praya̍c ca no̱ vipro̍ dū̱taḥ pari̍ṣkṛto̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.39.10	tvaṁ no̍ agna ā̱yuṣu̱ tvaṁ de̱veṣu̍ pūrvya̱ vasva̱ eka̍ irajyasi | tvām āpa̍ḥ pari̱sruta̱ḥ pari̍ yanti̱ svase̍tavo̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.1	indrā̍gnī yu̱vaṁ su na̱ḥ saha̍ntā̱ dāsa̍tho ra̱yim | yena̍ dṛ̱ḻhā sa̱matsv ā vī̱ḻu ci̍t sāhiṣī̱mahy a̱gnir vane̍va̱ vāta̱ in nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.2	na̱hi vā̍ṁ va̱vrayā̍ma̱he 'thendra̱m id ya̍jāmahe̱ śavi̍ṣṭhaṁ nṛ̱ṇāṁ nara̍m | sa na̍ḥ ka̱dā ci̱d arva̍tā̱ gama̱d ā vāja̍sātaye̱ gama̱d ā me̱dhasā̍taye̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.3	tā hi madhya̱m bharā̍ṇām indrā̱gnī a̍dhikṣi̱taḥ | tā u̍ kavitva̱nā ka̱vī pṛ̱cchyamā̍nā sakhīya̱te saṁ dhī̱tam a̍śnutaṁ narā̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.4	a̱bhy a̍rca nabhāka̱vad i̍ndrā̱gnī ya̱jasā̍ gi̱rā | yayo̱r viśva̍m i̱daṁ jaga̍d i̱yaṁ dyauḥ pṛ̍thi̱vī ma̱hy u1̱̍pasthe̍ bibhṛ̱to vasu̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.5	pra brahmā̍ṇi nabhāka̱vad i̍ndrā̱gnibhyā̍m irajyata | yā sa̱ptabu̍dhnam arṇa̱vaṁ ji̱hmabā̍ram aporṇu̱ta indra̱ īśā̍na̱ oja̍sā̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.6	api̍ vṛśca purāṇa̱vad vra̱tate̍r iva guṣpi̱tam ojo̍ dā̱sasya̍ dambhaya | va̱yaṁ tad a̍sya̱ sambhṛ̍ta̱ṁ vasv indre̍ṇa̱ vi bha̍jemahi̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.7	yad i̍ndrā̱gnī janā̍ i̱me vi̱hvaya̍nte̱ tanā̍ gi̱rā | a̱smāke̍bhi̱r nṛbhi̍r va̱yaṁ sā̍sa̱hyāma̍ pṛtanya̱to va̍nu̱yāma̍ vanuṣya̱to nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.8	yā nu śve̱tāv a̱vo di̱va u̱ccarā̍ta̱ upa̱ dyubhi̍ḥ | i̱ndrā̱gnyor anu̍ vra̱tam uhā̍nā yanti̱ sindha̍vo̱ yān sī̍m ba̱ndhād amu̍ñcatā̱ṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.9	pū̱rvīṣ ṭa̍ i̱ndropa̍mātayaḥ pū̱rvīr u̱ta praśa̍staya̱ḥ sūno̍ hi̱nvasya̍ harivaḥ | vasvo̍ vī̱rasyā̱pṛco̱ yā nu sādha̍nta no̱ dhiyo̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.10	taṁ śi̍śītā suvṛ̱ktibhi̍s tve̱ṣaṁ satvā̍nam ṛ̱gmiya̍m | u̱to nu ci̱d ya oja̍sā̱ śuṣṇa̍syā̱ṇḍāni̱ bheda̍ti̱ jeṣa̱t sva̍rvatīr a̱po nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.11	taṁ śi̍śītā svadhva̱raṁ sa̱tyaṁ satvā̍nam ṛ̱tviya̍m | u̱to nu ci̱d ya oha̍ta ā̱ṇḍā śuṣṇa̍sya̱ bheda̱ty ajai̱ḥ sva̍rvatīr a̱po nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.40.12	e̱vendrā̱gnibhyā̍m pitṛ̱van navī̍yo mandhātṛ̱vad a̍ṅgira̱svad a̍vāci | tri̱dhātu̍nā̱ śarma̍ṇā pātam a̱smān va̱yaṁ syā̍ma̱ pata̍yo rayī̱ṇām ||
8.41.1	a̱smā ū̱ ṣu prabhū̍taye̱ varu̍ṇāya ma̱rudbhyo 'rcā̍ vi̱duṣṭa̍rebhyaḥ | yo dhī̱tā mānu̍ṣāṇām pa̱śvo gā i̍va̱ rakṣa̍ti̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.2	tam ū̱ ṣu sa̍ma̱nā gi̱rā pi̍tṝ̱ṇāṁ ca̱ manma̍bhiḥ | nā̱bhā̱kasya̱ praśa̍stibhi̱r yaḥ sindhū̍nā̱m upo̍da̱ye sa̱ptasva̍sā̱ sa ma̍dhya̱mo nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.3	sa kṣapa̱ḥ pari̍ ṣasvaje̱ ny u1̱̍sro mā̱yayā̍ dadhe̱ sa viśva̱m pari̍ darśa̱taḥ | tasya̱ venī̱r anu̍ vra̱tam u̱ṣas ti̱sro a̍vardhaya̱n nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.4	yaḥ ka̱kubho̍ nidhāra̱yaḥ pṛ̍thi̱vyām adhi̍ darśa̱taḥ | sa mātā̍ pū̱rvyam pa̱daṁ tad varu̍ṇasya̱ saptya̱ṁ sa hi go̱pā i̱veryo̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.5	yo dha̱rtā bhuva̍nānā̱ṁ ya u̱srāṇā̍m apī̱cyā̱3̱̍ veda̱ nāmā̍ni̱ guhyā̍ | sa ka̱viḥ kāvyā̍ pu̱ru rū̱paṁ dyaur i̍va puṣyati̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.6	yasmi̱n viśvā̍ni̱ kāvyā̍ ca̱kre nābhi̍r iva śri̱tā | tri̱taṁ jū̱tī sa̍paryata vra̱je gāvo̱ na sa̱ṁyuje̍ yu̱je aśvā̍m̐ ayukṣata̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.7	ya ā̱sv atka̍ ā̱śaye̱ viśvā̍ jā̱tāny e̍ṣām | pari̱ dhāmā̍ni̱ marmṛ̍śa̱d varu̍ṇasya pu̱ro gaye̱ viśve̍ de̱vā anu̍ vra̱taṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.8	sa sa̍mu̱dro a̍pī̱cya̍s tu̱ro dyām i̍va rohati̱ ni yad ā̍su̱ yaju̍r da̱dhe | sa mā̱yā a̱rcinā̍ pa̱dāstṛ̍ṇā̱n nāka̱m āru̍ha̱n nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.9	yasya̍ śve̱tā vi̍cakṣa̱ṇā ti̱sro bhūmī̍r adhikṣi̱taḥ | trir utta̍rāṇi pa̱pratu̱r varu̍ṇasya dhru̱vaṁ sada̱ḥ sa sa̍ptā̱nām i̍rajyati̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.41.10	yaḥ śve̱tām̐ adhi̍nirṇijaś ca̱kre kṛ̱ṣṇām̐ anu̍ vra̱tā | sa dhāma̍ pū̱rvyam ma̍me̱ yaḥ ska̱mbhena̱ vi roda̍sī a̱jo na dyām adhā̍raya̱n nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.42.1	asta̍bhnā̱d dyām asu̍ro vi̱śvave̍dā̱ ami̍mīta vari̱māṇa̍m pṛthi̱vyāḥ | āsī̍da̱d viśvā̱ bhuva̍nāni sa̱mrāḍ viśvet tāni̱ varu̍ṇasya vra̱tāni̍ ||
8.42.2	e̱vā va̍ndasva̱ varu̍ṇam bṛ̱hanta̍ṁ nama̱syā dhīra̍m a̱mṛta̍sya go̱pām | sa na̱ḥ śarma̍ tri̱varū̍tha̱ṁ vi ya̍ṁsat pā̱taṁ no̍ dyāvāpṛthivī u̱pasthe̍ ||
8.42.3	i̱māṁ dhiya̱ṁ śikṣa̍māṇasya deva̱ kratu̱ṁ dakṣa̍ṁ varuṇa̱ saṁ śi̍śādhi | yayāti̱ viśvā̍ duri̱tā tare̍ma su̱tarmā̍ṇa̱m adhi̱ nāva̍ṁ ruhema ||
8.42.4	ā vā̱ṁ grāvā̍ṇo aśvinā dhī̱bhir viprā̍ acucyavuḥ | nāsa̍tyā̱ soma̍pītaye̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.42.5	yathā̍ vā̱m atri̍r aśvinā gī̱rbhir vipro̱ ajo̍havīt | nāsa̍tyā̱ soma̍pītaye̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.42.6	e̱vā vā̍m ahva ū̱taye̱ yathāhu̍vanta̱ medhi̍rāḥ | nāsa̍tyā̱ soma̍pītaye̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||
8.43.1	i̱me vipra̍sya ve̱dhaso̱ 'gner astṛ̍tayajvanaḥ | gira̱ḥ stomā̍sa īrate ||
8.43.2	asmai̍ te prati̱harya̍te̱ jāta̍vedo̱ vica̍rṣaṇe | agne̱ janā̍mi suṣṭu̱tim ||
8.43.3	ā̱ro̱kā i̍va̱ ghed aha̍ ti̱gmā a̍gne̱ tava̱ tviṣa̍ḥ | da̱dbhir vanā̍ni bapsati ||
8.43.4	hara̍yo dhū̱make̍tavo̱ vāta̍jūtā̱ upa̱ dyavi̍ | yata̍nte̱ vṛtha̍g a̱gnaya̍ḥ ||
8.43.5	e̱te tye vṛtha̍g a̱gnaya̍ i̱ddhāsa̱ḥ sam a̍dṛkṣata | u̱ṣasā̍m iva ke̱tava̍ḥ ||
8.43.6	kṛ̱ṣṇā rajā̍ṁsi patsu̱taḥ pra̱yāṇe̍ jā̱tave̍dasaḥ | a̱gnir yad rodha̍ti̱ kṣami̍ ||
8.43.7	dhā̱siṁ kṛ̍ṇvā̱na oṣa̍dhī̱r bapsa̍d a̱gnir na vā̍yati | puna̱r yan taru̍ṇī̱r api̍ ||
8.43.8	ji̱hvābhi̱r aha̱ nanna̍mad a̱rciṣā̍ jañjaṇā̱bhava̍n | a̱gnir vane̍ṣu rocate ||
8.43.9	a̱psv a̍gne̱ sadhi̱ṣ ṭava̱ sauṣa̍dhī̱r anu̍ rudhyase | garbhe̱ sañ jā̍yase̱ puna̍ḥ ||
8.43.10	ud a̍gne̱ tava̱ tad ghṛ̱tād a̱rcī ro̍cata̱ āhu̍tam | niṁsā̍naṁ ju̱hvo̱3̱̍ mukhe̍ ||
8.43.11	u̱kṣānnā̍ya va̱śānnā̍ya̱ soma̍pṛṣṭhāya ve̱dhase̍ | stomai̍r vidhemā̱gnaye̍ ||
8.43.12	u̱ta tvā̱ nama̍sā va̱yaṁ hota̱r vare̍ṇyakrato | agne̍ sa̱midbhi̍r īmahe ||
8.43.13	u̱ta tvā̍ bhṛgu̱vac chu̍ce manu̱ṣvad a̍gna āhuta | a̱ṅgi̱ra̱svad dha̍vāmahe ||
8.43.14	tvaṁ hy a̍gne a̱gninā̱ vipro̱ vipre̍ṇa̱ san sa̱tā | sakhā̱ sakhyā̍ sami̱dhyase̍ ||
8.43.15	sa tvaṁ viprā̍ya dā̱śuṣe̍ ra̱yiṁ de̍hi saha̱sriṇa̍m | agne̍ vī̱rava̍tī̱m iṣa̍m ||
8.43.16	agne̱ bhrāta̱ḥ saha̍skṛta̱ rohi̍daśva̱ śuci̍vrata | i̱maṁ stoma̍ṁ juṣasva me ||
8.43.17	u̱ta tvā̍gne̱ mama̱ stuto̍ vā̱śrāya̍ prati̱harya̍te | go̱ṣṭhaṁ gāva̍ ivāśata ||
8.43.18	tubhya̱ṁ tā a̍ṅgirastama̱ viśvā̍ḥ sukṣi̱taya̱ḥ pṛtha̍k | agne̱ kāmā̍ya yemire ||
8.43.19	a̱gniṁ dhī̱bhir ma̍nī̱ṣiṇo̱ medhi̍rāso vipa̱ścita̍ḥ | a̱dma̱sadyā̍ya hinvire ||
8.43.20	taṁ tvām ajme̍ṣu vā̱jina̍ṁ tanvā̱nā a̍gne adhva̱ram | vahni̱ṁ hotā̍ram īḻate ||
8.43.21	pu̱ru̱trā hi sa̱dṛṅṅ asi̱ viśo̱ viśvā̱ anu̍ pra̱bhuḥ | sa̱matsu̍ tvā havāmahe ||
8.43.22	tam ī̍ḻiṣva̱ ya āhu̍to̱ 'gnir vi̱bhrāja̍te ghṛ̱taiḥ | i̱maṁ na̍ḥ śṛṇava̱d dhava̍m ||
8.43.23	taṁ tvā̍ va̱yaṁ ha̍vāmahe śṛ̱ṇvanta̍ṁ jā̱tave̍dasam | agne̱ ghnanta̱m apa̱ dviṣa̍ḥ ||
8.43.24	vi̱śāṁ rājā̍na̱m adbhu̍ta̱m adhya̍kṣa̱ṁ dharma̍ṇām i̱mam | a̱gnim ī̍ḻe̱ sa u̍ śravat ||
8.43.25	a̱gniṁ vi̱śvāyu̍vepasa̱m marya̱ṁ na vā̱jina̍ṁ hi̱tam | sapti̱ṁ na vā̍jayāmasi ||
8.43.26	ghnan mṛ̱dhrāṇy apa̱ dviṣo̱ daha̱n rakṣā̍ṁsi vi̱śvahā̍ | agne̍ ti̱gmena̍ dīdihi ||
8.43.27	yaṁ tvā̱ janā̍sa indha̱te ma̍nu̱ṣvad a̍ṅgirastama | agne̱ sa bo̍dhi me̱ vaca̍ḥ ||
8.43.28	yad a̍gne divi̱jā asy a̍psu̱jā vā̍ sahaskṛta | taṁ tvā̍ gī̱rbhir ha̍vāmahe ||
8.43.29	tubhya̱ṁ ghet te janā̍ i̱me viśvā̍ḥ sukṣi̱taya̱ḥ pṛtha̍k | dhā̱siṁ hi̍nva̱nty atta̍ve ||
8.43.30	te ghed a̍gne svā̱dhyo 'hā̱ viśvā̍ nṛ̱cakṣa̍saḥ | tara̍ntaḥ syāma du̱rgahā̍ ||
8.43.31	a̱gnim ma̱ndram pu̍rupri̱yaṁ śī̱ram pā̍va̱kaśo̍ciṣam | hṛ̱dbhir ma̱ndrebhi̍r īmahe ||
8.43.32	sa tvam a̍gne vi̱bhāva̍suḥ sṛ̱jan sūryo̱ na ra̱śmibhi̍ḥ | śardha̱n tamā̍ṁsi jighnase ||
8.43.33	tat te̍ sahasva īmahe dā̱traṁ yan nopa̱dasya̍ti | tvad a̍gne̱ vārya̱ṁ vasu̍ ||
8.44.1	sa̱midhā̱gniṁ du̍vasyata ghṛ̱tair bo̍dhaya̱tāti̍thim | āsmi̍n ha̱vyā ju̍hotana ||
8.44.2	agne̱ stoma̍ṁ juṣasva me̱ vardha̍svā̱nena̱ manma̍nā | prati̍ sū̱ktāni̍ harya naḥ ||
8.44.3	a̱gniṁ dū̱tam pu̱ro da̍dhe havya̱vāha̱m upa̍ bruve | de̱vām̐ ā sā̍dayād i̱ha ||
8.44.4	ut te̍ bṛ̱hanto̍ a̱rcaya̍ḥ samidhā̱nasya̍ dīdivaḥ | agne̍ śu̱krāsa̍ īrate ||
8.44.5	upa̍ tvā ju̱hvo̱3̱̍ mama̍ ghṛ̱tācī̍r yantu haryata | agne̍ ha̱vyā ju̍ṣasva naḥ ||
8.44.6	ma̱ndraṁ hotā̍ram ṛ̱tvija̍ṁ ci̱trabhā̍nuṁ vi̱bhāva̍sum | a̱gnim ī̍ḻe̱ sa u̍ śravat ||
8.44.7	pra̱tnaṁ hotā̍ra̱m īḍya̱ṁ juṣṭa̍m a̱gniṁ ka̱vikra̍tum | a̱dhva̱rāṇā̍m abhi̱śriya̍m ||
8.44.8	ju̱ṣā̱ṇo a̍ṅgirastame̱mā ha̱vyāny ā̍nu̱ṣak | agne̍ ya̱jñaṁ na̍ya ṛtu̱thā ||
8.44.9	sa̱mi̱dhā̱na u̍ santya̱ śukra̍śoca i̱hā va̍ha | ci̱ki̱tvān daivya̱ṁ jana̍m ||
8.44.10	vipra̱ṁ hotā̍ram a̱druha̍ṁ dhū̱make̍tuṁ vi̱bhāva̍sum | ya̱jñānā̍ṁ ke̱tum ī̍mahe ||
8.44.11	agne̱ ni pā̍hi na̱s tvam prati̍ ṣma deva̱ rīṣa̍taḥ | bhi̱ndhi dveṣa̍ḥ sahaskṛta ||
8.44.12	a̱gniḥ pra̱tnena̱ manma̍nā̱ śumbhā̍nas ta̱nva1̱̍ṁ svām | ka̱vir vipre̍ṇa vāvṛdhe ||
8.44.13	ū̱rjo napā̍ta̱m ā hu̍ve̱ 'gnim pā̍va̱kaśo̍ciṣam | a̱smin ya̱jñe sva̍dhva̱re ||
8.44.14	sa no̍ mitramaha̱s tvam agne̍ śu̱kreṇa̍ śo̱ciṣā̍ | de̱vair ā sa̍tsi ba̱rhiṣi̍ ||
8.44.15	yo a̱gniṁ ta̱nvo̱3̱̍ dame̍ de̱vam marta̍ḥ sapa̱ryati̍ | tasmā̱ id dī̍daya̱d vasu̍ ||
8.44.16	a̱gnir mū̱rdhā di̱vaḥ ka̱kut pati̍ḥ pṛthi̱vyā a̱yam | a̱pāṁ retā̍ṁsi jinvati ||
8.44.17	ud a̍gne̱ śuca̍ya̱s tava̍ śu̱krā bhrāja̍nta īrate | tava̱ jyotī̍ṁṣy a̱rcaya̍ḥ ||
8.44.18	īśi̍ṣe̱ vārya̍sya̱ hi dā̱trasyā̍gne̱ sva̍rpatiḥ | sto̱tā syā̱ṁ tava̱ śarma̍ṇi ||
8.44.19	tvām a̍gne manī̱ṣiṇa̱s tvāṁ hi̍nvanti̱ citti̍bhiḥ | tvāṁ va̍rdhantu no̱ gira̍ḥ ||
8.44.20	ada̍bdhasya sva̱dhāva̍to dū̱tasya̱ rebha̍ta̱ḥ sadā̍ | a̱gneḥ sa̱khyaṁ vṛ̍ṇīmahe ||
8.44.21	a̱gniḥ śuci̍vratatama̱ḥ śuci̱r vipra̱ḥ śuci̍ḥ ka̱viḥ | śucī̍ rocata̱ āhu̍taḥ ||
8.44.22	u̱ta tvā̍ dhī̱tayo̱ mama̱ giro̍ vardhantu vi̱śvahā̍ | agne̍ sa̱khyasya̍ bodhi naḥ ||
8.44.23	yad a̍gne̱ syām a̱haṁ tvaṁ tvaṁ vā̍ ghā̱ syā a̱ham | syuṣ ṭe̍ sa̱tyā i̱hāśiṣa̍ḥ ||
8.44.24	vasu̱r vasu̍pati̱r hi ka̱m asy a̍gne vi̱bhāva̍suḥ | syāma̍ te suma̱tāv api̍ ||
8.44.25	agne̍ dhṛ̱tavra̍tāya te samu̱drāye̍va̱ sindha̍vaḥ | giro̍ vā̱śrāsa̍ īrate ||
8.44.26	yuvā̍naṁ vi̱śpati̍ṁ ka̱viṁ vi̱śvāda̍m puru̱vepa̍sam | a̱gniṁ śu̍mbhāmi̱ manma̍bhiḥ ||
8.44.27	ya̱jñānā̍ṁ ra̱thye̍ va̱yaṁ ti̱gmaja̍mbhāya vī̱ḻave̍ | stomai̍r iṣemā̱gnaye̍ ||
8.44.28	a̱yam a̍gne̱ tve api̍ jari̱tā bhū̍tu santya | tasmai̍ pāvaka mṛḻaya ||
8.44.29	dhīro̱ hy asy a̍dma̱sad vipro̱ na jāgṛ̍vi̱ḥ sadā̍ | agne̍ dī̱daya̍si̱ dyavi̍ ||
8.44.30	pu̱rāgne̍ duri̱tebhya̍ḥ pu̱rā mṛ̱dhrebhya̍ḥ kave | pra ṇa̱ āyu̍r vaso tira ||
8.45.1	ā ghā̱ ye a̱gnim i̍ndha̱te stṛ̱ṇanti̍ ba̱rhir ā̍nu̱ṣak | yeṣā̱m indro̱ yuvā̱ sakhā̍ ||
8.45.2	bṛ̱hann id i̱dhma e̍ṣā̱m bhūri̍ śa̱stam pṛ̱thuḥ svaru̍ḥ | yeṣā̱m indro̱ yuvā̱ sakhā̍ ||
8.45.3	ayu̍ddha̱ id yu̱dhā vṛta̱ṁ śūra̱ āja̍ti̱ satva̍bhiḥ | yeṣā̱m indro̱ yuvā̱ sakhā̍ ||
8.45.4	ā bu̱ndaṁ vṛ̍tra̱hā da̍de jā̱taḥ pṛ̍ccha̱d vi mā̱tara̍m | ka u̱grāḥ ke ha̍ śṛṇvire ||
8.45.5	prati̍ tvā śava̱sī va̍dad gi̱rāv apso̱ na yo̍dhiṣat | yas te̍ śatru̱tvam ā̍ca̱ke ||
8.45.6	u̱ta tvam ma̍ghavañ chṛṇu̱ yas te̱ vaṣṭi̍ va̱vakṣi̱ tat | yad vī̱ḻayā̍si vī̱ḻu tat ||
8.45.7	yad ā̱jiṁ yāty ā̍ji̱kṛd indra̍ḥ svaśva̱yur upa̍ | ra̱thīta̍mo ra̱thīnā̍m ||
8.45.8	vi ṣu viśvā̍ abhi̱yujo̱ vajri̱n viṣva̱g yathā̍ vṛha | bhavā̍ naḥ su̱śrava̍stamaḥ ||
8.45.9	a̱smāka̱ṁ su ratha̍m pu̱ra indra̍ḥ kṛṇotu sā̱taye̍ | na yaṁ dhūrva̍nti dhū̱rtaya̍ḥ ||
8.45.10	vṛ̱jyāma̍ te̱ pari̱ dviṣo 'ra̍ṁ te śakra dā̱vane̍ | ga̱memed i̍ndra̱ goma̍taḥ ||
8.45.11	śanai̍ś ci̱d yanto̍ adri̱vo 'śvā̍vantaḥ śata̱gvina̍ḥ | vi̱vakṣa̍ṇā ane̱hasa̍ḥ ||
8.45.12	ū̱rdhvā hi te̍ di̱ve-di̍ve sa̱hasrā̍ sū̱nṛtā̍ śa̱tā | ja̱ri̱tṛbhyo̍ vi̱maṁha̍te ||
8.45.13	vi̱dmā hi tvā̍ dhanaṁja̱yam indra̍ dṛ̱ḻhā ci̍d āru̱jam | ā̱dā̱riṇa̱ṁ yathā̱ gaya̍m ||
8.45.14	ka̱ku̱haṁ ci̍t tvā kave̱ manda̍ntu dhṛṣṇa̱v inda̍vaḥ | ā tvā̍ pa̱ṇiṁ yad īma̍he ||
8.45.15	yas te̍ re̱vām̐ adā̍śuriḥ prama̱marṣa̍ ma̱ghatta̍ye | tasya̍ no̱ veda̱ ā bha̍ra ||
8.45.16	i̱ma u̍ tvā̱ vi ca̍kṣate̱ sakhā̍ya indra so̱mina̍ḥ | pu̱ṣṭāva̍nto̱ yathā̍ pa̱śum ||
8.45.17	u̱ta tvāba̍dhiraṁ va̱yaṁ śrutka̍rṇa̱ṁ santa̍m ū̱taye̍ | dū̱rād i̱ha ha̍vāmahe ||
8.45.18	yac chu̍śrū̱yā i̱maṁ hava̍ṁ du̱rmarṣa̍ṁ cakriyā u̱ta | bhave̍r ā̱pir no̱ anta̍maḥ ||
8.45.19	yac ci̱d dhi te̱ api̱ vyathi̍r jaga̱nvāṁso̱ ama̍nmahi | go̱dā id i̍ndra bodhi naḥ ||
8.45.20	ā tvā̍ ra̱mbhaṁ na jivra̍yo rara̱bhmā śa̍vasas pate | u̱śmasi̍ tvā sa̱dhastha̱ ā ||
8.45.21	sto̱tram indrā̍ya gāyata purunṛ̱mṇāya̱ satva̍ne | naki̱r yaṁ vṛ̍ṇva̱te yu̱dhi ||
8.45.22	a̱bhi tvā̍ vṛṣabhā su̱te su̱taṁ sṛ̍jāmi pī̱taye̍ | tṛ̱mpā vy a̍śnuhī̱ mada̍m ||
8.45.23	mā tvā̍ mū̱rā a̍vi̱ṣyavo̱ mopa̱hasvā̍na̱ ā da̍bhan | mākī̍m brahma̱dviṣo̍ vanaḥ ||
8.45.24	i̱ha tvā̱ gopa̍rīṇasā ma̱he ma̍ndantu̱ rādha̍se | saro̍ gau̱ro yathā̍ piba ||
8.45.25	yā vṛ̍tra̱hā pa̍rā̱vati̱ sanā̱ navā̍ ca cucyu̱ve | tā sa̱ṁsatsu̱ pra vo̍cata ||
8.45.26	api̍bat ka̱druva̍ḥ su̱tam indra̍ḥ sa̱hasra̍bāhve | atrā̍dediṣṭa̱ pauṁsya̍m ||
8.45.27	sa̱tyaṁ tat tu̱rvaśe̱ yadau̱ vidā̍no ahnavā̱yyam | vy ā̍naṭ tu̱rvaṇe̱ śami̍ ||
8.45.28	ta̱raṇi̍ṁ vo̱ janā̍nāṁ tra̱daṁ vāja̍sya̱ goma̍taḥ | sa̱mā̱nam u̱ pra śa̍ṁsiṣam ||
8.45.29	ṛ̱bhu̱kṣaṇa̱ṁ na varta̍va u̱ktheṣu̍ tugryā̱vṛdha̍m | indra̱ṁ some̱ sacā̍ su̱te ||
8.45.30	yaḥ kṛ̱ntad id vi yo̱nyaṁ tri̱śokā̍ya gi̱rim pṛ̱thum | gobhyo̍ gā̱tuṁ nire̍tave ||
8.45.31	yad da̍dhi̱ṣe ma̍na̱syasi̍ mandā̱naḥ pred iya̍kṣasi | mā tat ka̍r indra mṛ̱ḻaya̍ ||
8.45.32	da̱bhraṁ ci̱d dhi tvāva̍taḥ kṛ̱taṁ śṛ̱ṇve adhi̱ kṣami̍ | jigā̍tv indra te̱ mana̍ḥ ||
8.45.33	taved u̱ tāḥ su̍kī̱rtayo 'sa̍nn u̱ta praśa̍stayaḥ | yad i̍ndra mṛ̱ḻayā̍si naḥ ||
8.45.34	mā na̱ eka̍smi̱nn āga̍si̱ mā dvayo̍r u̱ta tri̱ṣu | vadhī̱r mā śū̍ra̱ bhūri̍ṣu ||
8.45.35	bi̱bhayā̱ hi tvāva̍ta u̱grād a̍bhiprabha̱ṅgiṇa̍ḥ | da̱smād a̱ham ṛ̍tī̱ṣaha̍ḥ ||
8.45.36	mā sakhyu̱ḥ śūna̱m ā vi̍de̱ mā pu̱trasya̍ prabhūvaso | ā̱vṛtva̍d bhūtu te̱ mana̍ḥ ||
8.45.37	ko nu ma̍ryā̱ ami̍thita̱ḥ sakhā̱ sakhā̍yam abravīt | ja̱hā ko a̱smad ī̍ṣate ||
8.45.38	e̱vāre̍ vṛṣabhā su̱te 'si̍nva̱n bhūry ā̍vayaḥ | śva̱ghnīva̍ ni̱vatā̱ cara̍n ||
8.45.39	ā ta̍ e̱tā va̍co̱yujā̱ harī̍ gṛbhṇe su̱madra̍thā | yad ī̍m bra̱hmabhya̱ id dada̍ḥ ||
8.45.40	bhi̱ndhi viśvā̱ apa̱ dviṣa̱ḥ pari̱ bādho̍ ja̱hī mṛdha̍ḥ | vasu̍ spā̱rhaṁ tad ā bha̍ra ||
8.45.41	yad vī̱ḻāv i̍ndra̱ yat sthi̱re yat parśā̍ne̱ parā̍bhṛtam | vasu̍ spā̱rhaṁ tad ā bha̍ra ||
8.45.42	yasya̍ te vi̱śvamā̍nuṣo̱ bhūre̍r da̱ttasya̱ veda̍ti | vasu̍ spā̱rhaṁ tad ā bha̍ra ||
8.46.1	tvāva̍taḥ purūvaso va̱yam i̍ndra praṇetaḥ | smasi̍ sthātar harīṇām ||
8.46.2	tvāṁ hi sa̱tyam a̍drivo vi̱dma dā̱tāra̍m i̱ṣām | vi̱dma dā̱tāra̍ṁ rayī̱ṇām ||
8.46.3	ā yasya̍ te mahi̱māna̱ṁ śata̍mūte̱ śata̍krato | gī̱rbhir gṛ̱ṇanti̍ kā̱rava̍ḥ ||
8.46.4	su̱nī̱tho ghā̱ sa martyo̱ yam ma̱ruto̱ yam a̍rya̱mā | mi̱traḥ pānty a̱druha̍ḥ ||
8.46.5	dadhā̍no̱ goma̱d aśva̍vat su̱vīrya̍m ādi̱tyajū̍ta edhate | sadā̍ rā̱yā pu̍ru̱spṛhā̍ ||
8.46.6	tam indra̱ṁ dāna̍m īmahe śavasā̱nam abhī̍rvam | īśā̍naṁ rā̱ya ī̍mahe ||
8.46.7	tasmi̱n hi santy ū̱tayo̱ viśvā̱ abhī̍rava̱ḥ sacā̍ | tam ā va̍hantu̱ sapta̍yaḥ purū̱vasu̱m madā̍ya̱ hara̍yaḥ su̱tam ||
8.46.8	yas te̱ mado̱ vare̍ṇyo̱ ya i̍ndra vṛtra̱hanta̍maḥ | ya ā̍da̱diḥ sva1̱̍r nṛbhi̱r yaḥ pṛta̍nāsu du̱ṣṭara̍ḥ ||
8.46.9	yo du̱ṣṭaro̍ viśvavāra śra̱vāyyo̱ vāje̱ṣv asti̍ taru̱tā | sa na̍ḥ śaviṣṭha̱ sava̱nā va̍so gahi ga̱mema̱ goma̍ti vra̱je ||
8.46.10	ga̱vyo ṣu ṇo̱ yathā̍ pu̱rāśva̱yota ra̍tha̱yā | va̱ri̱va̱sya ma̍hāmaha ||
8.46.11	na̱hi te̍ śūra̱ rādha̱so 'nta̍ṁ vi̱ndāmi̍ sa̱trā | da̱śa̱syā no̍ maghava̱n nū ci̍d adrivo̱ dhiyo̱ vāje̍bhir āvitha ||
8.46.12	ya ṛ̱ṣvaḥ śrā̍va̱yatsa̍khā̱ viśvet sa ve̍da̱ jani̍mā puruṣṭu̱taḥ | taṁ viśve̱ mānu̍ṣā yu̱gendra̍ṁ havante tavi̱ṣaṁ ya̱tasru̍caḥ ||
8.46.13	sa no̱ vāje̍ṣv avi̱tā pu̍rū̱vasu̍ḥ puraḥsthā̱tā ma̱ghavā̍ vṛtra̱hā bhu̍vat ||
8.46.14	a̱bhi vo̍ vī̱ram andha̍so̱ made̍ṣu gāya gi̱rā ma̱hā vice̍tasam | indra̱ṁ nāma̱ śrutya̍ṁ śā̱kina̱ṁ vaco̱ yathā̍ ||
8.46.15	da̱dī rekṇa̍s ta̱nve̍ da̱dir vasu̍ da̱dir vāje̍ṣu puruhūta vā̱jina̍m | nū̱nam atha̍ ||
8.46.16	viśve̍ṣām ira̱jyanta̱ṁ vasū̍nāṁ sāsa̱hvāṁsa̍ṁ cid a̱sya varpa̍saḥ | kṛ̱pa̱ya̱to nū̱nam aty atha̍ ||
8.46.17	ma̱haḥ su vo̱ ara̍m iṣe̱ stavā̍mahe mī̱ḻhuṣe̍ araṁga̱māya̱ jagma̍ye | ya̱jñebhi̍r gī̱rbhir vi̱śvama̍nuṣām ma̱rutā̍m iyakṣasi̱ gāye̍ tvā̱ nama̍sā gi̱rā ||
8.46.18	ye pā̱taya̍nte̱ ajma̍bhir girī̱ṇāṁ snubhi̍r eṣām | ya̱jñam ma̍hi̱ṣvaṇī̍nāṁ su̱mnaṁ tu̍vi̱ṣvaṇī̍nā̱m prādhva̱re ||
8.46.19	pra̱bha̱ṅgaṁ du̍rmatī̱nām indra̍ śavi̱ṣṭhā bha̍ra | ra̱yim a̱smabhya̱ṁ yujya̍ṁ codayanmate̱ jyeṣṭha̍ṁ codayanmate ||
8.46.20	sani̍ta̱ḥ susa̍nita̱r ugra̱ citra̱ ceti̍ṣṭha̱ sūnṛ̍ta | prā̱sahā̍ samrā̱ṭ sahu̍ri̱ṁ saha̍ntam bhu̱jyuṁ vāje̍ṣu̱ pūrvya̍m ||
8.46.21	ā sa e̍tu̱ ya īva̱d ām̐ ade̍vaḥ pū̱rtam ā̍da̱de | yathā̍ ci̱d vaśo̍ a̱śvyaḥ pṛ̍thu̱śrava̍si kānī̱te̱3̱̍ 'syā vyuṣy ā̍da̱de ||
8.46.22	ṣa̱ṣṭiṁ sa̱hasrāśvya̍syā̱yutā̍sana̱m uṣṭrā̍nāṁ viṁśa̱tiṁ śa̱tā | daśa̱ śyāvī̍nāṁ śa̱tā daśa̱ trya̍ruṣīṇā̱ṁ daśa̱ gavā̍ṁ sa̱hasrā̍ ||
8.46.23	daśa̍ śyā̱vā ṛ̱dhadra̍yo vī̱tavā̍rāsa ā̱śava̍ḥ | ma̱thrā ne̱miṁ ni vā̍vṛtuḥ ||
8.46.24	dānā̍saḥ pṛthu̱śrava̍saḥ kānī̱tasya̍ su̱rādha̍saḥ | ratha̍ṁ hira̱ṇyaya̱ṁ dada̱n maṁhi̍ṣṭhaḥ sū̱rir a̍bhū̱d varṣi̍ṣṭham akṛta̱ śrava̍ḥ ||
8.46.25	ā no̍ vāyo ma̱he tane̍ yā̱hi ma̱khāya̱ pāja̍se | va̱yaṁ hi te̍ cakṛ̱mā bhūri̍ dā̱vane̍ sa̱dyaś ci̱n mahi̍ dā̱vane̍ ||
8.46.26	yo aśve̍bhi̱r vaha̍te̱ vasta̍ u̱srās triḥ sa̱pta sa̍ptatī̱nām | e̱bhiḥ some̍bhiḥ soma̱sudbhi̍ḥ somapā dā̱nāya̍ śukrapūtapāḥ ||
8.46.27	yo ma̍ i̱maṁ ci̍d u̱ tmanāma̍ndac ci̱traṁ dā̱vane̍ | a̱ra̱ṭve akṣe̱ nahu̍ṣe su̱kṛtva̍ni su̱kṛtta̍rāya su̱kratu̍ḥ ||
8.46.28	u̱ca̱thye̱3̱̍ vapu̍ṣi̱ yaḥ sva̱rāḻ u̱ta vā̍yo ghṛta̱snāḥ | aśve̍ṣita̱ṁ raje̍ṣita̱ṁ śune̍ṣita̱m prājma̱ tad i̱daṁ nu tat ||
8.46.29	adha̍ pri̱yam i̍ṣi̱rāya̍ ṣa̱ṣṭiṁ sa̱hasrā̍sanam | aśvā̍nā̱m in na vṛṣṇā̍m ||
8.46.30	gāvo̱ na yū̱tham upa̍ yanti̱ vadhra̍ya̱ upa̱ mā ya̍nti̱ vadhra̍yaḥ ||
8.46.31	adha̱ yac cāra̍the ga̱ṇe śa̱tam uṣṭrā̱m̐ aci̍kradat | adha̱ śvitne̍ṣu viṁśa̱tiṁ śa̱tā ||
8.46.32	śa̱taṁ dā̱se ba̍lbū̱the vipra̱s taru̍kṣa̱ ā da̍de | te te̍ vāyav i̱me janā̱ mada̱ntīndra̍gopā̱ mada̍nti de̱vago̍pāḥ ||
8.46.33	adha̱ syā yoṣa̍ṇā ma̱hī pra̍tī̱cī vaśa̍m a̱śvyam | adhi̍rukmā̱ vi nī̍yate ||
8.47.1	mahi̍ vo maha̱tām avo̱ varu̍ṇa̱ mitra̍ dā̱śuṣe̍ | yam ā̍dityā a̱bhi dru̱ho rakṣa̍thā̱ nem a̱ghaṁ na̍śad ane̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.2	vi̱dā de̍vā a̱ghānā̱m ādi̍tyāso a̱pākṛ̍tim | pa̱kṣā vayo̱ yatho̱pari̱ vy a1̱̍sme śarma̍ yacchatāne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.3	vy a1̱̍sme adhi̱ śarma̱ tat pa̱kṣā vayo̱ na ya̍ntana | viśvā̍ni viśvavedaso varū̱thyā̍ manāmahe 'ne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.4	yasmā̱ arā̍sata̱ kṣaya̍ṁ jī̱vātu̍ṁ ca̱ prace̍tasaḥ | mano̱r viśva̍sya̱ ghed i̱ma ā̍di̱tyā rā̱ya ī̍śate 'ne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.5	pari̍ ṇo vṛṇajann a̱ghā du̱rgāṇi̍ ra̱thyo̍ yathā | syāmed indra̍sya̱ śarma̍ṇy ādi̱tyānā̍m u̱tāva̍sy ane̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.6	pa̱ri̱hvṛ̱ted a̱nā jano̍ yu̱ṣmāda̍ttasya vāyati | devā̱ ada̍bhram āśa vo̱ yam ā̍dityā̱ ahe̍tanāne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.7	na taṁ ti̱gmaṁ ca̱na tyajo̱ na drā̍sad a̱bhi taṁ gu̱ru | yasmā̍ u̱ śarma̍ sa̱pratha̱ ādi̍tyāso̱ arā̍dhvam ane̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.8	yu̱ṣme de̍vā̱ api̍ ṣmasi̱ yudhya̍nta iva̱ varma̍su | yū̱yam ma̱ho na̱ ena̍so yū̱yam arbhā̍d uruṣyatāne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.9	adi̍tir na uruṣya̱tv adi̍ti̱ḥ śarma̍ yacchatu | mā̱tā mi̱trasya̍ re̱vato̍ 'rya̱mṇo varu̍ṇasya cāne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.10	yad de̍vā̱ḥ śarma̍ śara̱ṇaṁ yad bha̱draṁ yad a̍nātu̱ram | tri̱dhātu̱ yad va̍rū̱thya1̱̍ṁ tad a̱smāsu̱ vi ya̍ntanāne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.11	ādi̍tyā̱ ava̱ hi khyatādhi̱ kūlā̍d iva̱ spaśa̍ḥ | su̱tī̱rtham arva̍to ya̱thānu̍ no neṣathā su̱gam a̍ne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.12	neha bha̱draṁ ra̍kṣa̱svine̱ nāva̱yai nopa̱yā u̱ta | gave̍ ca bha̱draṁ dhe̱nave̍ vī̱rāya̍ ca śravasya̱te̍ 'ne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.13	yad ā̱vir yad a̍pī̱cya1̱̍ṁ devā̍so̱ asti̍ duṣkṛ̱tam | tri̱te tad viśva̍m ā̱ptya ā̱re a̱smad da̍dhātanāne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.14	yac ca̱ goṣu̍ du̱ṣṣvapnya̱ṁ yac cā̱sme du̍hitar divaḥ | tri̱tāya̱ tad vi̍bhāvary ā̱ptyāya̱ parā̍ vahāne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.15	ni̱ṣkaṁ vā̍ ghā kṛ̱ṇava̍te̱ sraja̍ṁ vā duhitar divaḥ | tri̱te du̱ṣṣvapnya̱ṁ sarva̍m ā̱ptye pari̍ dadmasy ane̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.16	tada̍nnāya̱ tada̍pase̱ tam bhā̱gam u̍pase̱duṣe̍ | tri̱tāya̍ ca dvi̱tāya̱ coṣo̍ du̱ṣṣvapnya̍ṁ vahāne̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.17	yathā̍ ka̱lāṁ yathā̍ śa̱phaṁ yatha̍ ṛ̱ṇaṁ sa̱ṁnayā̍masi | e̱vā du̱ṣṣvapnya̱ṁ sarva̍m ā̱ptye saṁ na̍yāmasy ane̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.47.18	ajai̍ṣmā̱dyāsa̍nāma̱ cābhū̱mānā̍gaso va̱yam | uṣo̱ yasmā̍d du̱ṣṣvapnyā̱d abhai̱ṣmāpa̱ tad u̍cchatv ane̱haso̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ suū̱tayo̍ va ū̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.48.1	svā̱dor a̍bhakṣi̱ vaya̍saḥ sume̱dhāḥ svā̱dhyo̍ varivo̱vitta̍rasya | viśve̱ yaṁ de̱vā u̱ta martyā̍so̱ madhu̍ bru̱vanto̍ a̱bhi sa̱ṁcara̍nti ||
8.48.2	a̱ntaś ca̱ prāgā̱ adi̍tir bhavāsy avayā̱tā hara̍so̱ daivya̍sya | inda̱v indra̍sya sa̱khyaṁ ju̍ṣā̱ṇaḥ śrauṣṭī̍va̱ dhura̱m anu̍ rā̱ya ṛ̍dhyāḥ ||
8.48.3	apā̍ma̱ soma̍m a̱mṛtā̍ abhū̱māga̍nma̱ jyoti̱r avi̍dāma de̱vān | kiṁ nū̱nam a̱smān kṛ̍ṇava̱d arā̍ti̱ḥ kim u̍ dhū̱rtir a̍mṛta̱ martya̍sya ||
8.48.4	śaṁ no̍ bhava hṛ̱da ā pī̱ta i̍ndo pi̱teva̍ soma sū̱nave̍ su̱śeva̍ḥ | sakhe̍va̱ sakhya̍ uruśaṁsa̱ dhīra̱ḥ pra ṇa̱ āyu̍r jī̱vase̍ soma tārīḥ ||
8.48.5	i̱me mā̍ pī̱tā ya̱śasa̍ uru̱ṣyavo̱ ratha̱ṁ na gāva̱ḥ sam a̍nāha̱ parva̍su | te mā̍ rakṣantu vi̱srasa̍ś ca̱ritrā̍d u̱ta mā̱ srāmā̍d yavaya̱ntv inda̍vaḥ ||
8.48.6	a̱gniṁ na mā̍ mathi̱taṁ saṁ di̍dīpa̱ḥ pra ca̍kṣaya kṛṇu̱hi vasya̍so naḥ | athā̱ hi te̱ mada̱ ā so̍ma̱ manye̍ re̱vām̐ i̍va̱ pra ca̍rā pu̱ṣṭim accha̍ ||
8.48.7	i̱ṣi̱reṇa̍ te̱ mana̍sā su̱tasya̍ bhakṣī̱mahi̱ pitrya̍syeva rā̱yaḥ | soma̍ rāja̱n pra ṇa̱ āyū̍ṁṣi tārī̱r ahā̍nīva̱ sūryo̍ vāsa̱rāṇi̍ ||
8.48.8	soma̍ rājan mṛ̱ḻayā̍ naḥ sva̱sti tava̍ smasi vra̱tyā̱3̱̍s tasya̍ viddhi | ala̍rti̱ dakṣa̍ u̱ta ma̱nyur i̍ndo̱ mā no̍ a̱ryo a̍nukā̱mam parā̍ dāḥ ||
8.48.9	tvaṁ hi na̍s ta̱nva̍ḥ soma go̱pā gātre̍-gātre niṣa̱satthā̍ nṛ̱cakṣā̍ḥ | yat te̍ va̱yam pra̍mi̱nāma̍ vra̱tāni̱ sa no̍ mṛḻa suṣa̱khā de̍va̱ vasya̍ḥ ||
8.48.10	ṛ̱dū̱dare̍ṇa̱ sakhyā̍ saceya̱ yo mā̱ na riṣye̍d dharyaśva pī̱taḥ | a̱yaṁ yaḥ somo̱ ny adhā̍yy a̱sme tasmā̱ indra̍m pra̱tira̍m e̱my āyu̍ḥ ||
8.48.11	apa̱ tyā a̍sthu̱r ani̍rā̱ amī̍vā̱ nir a̍trasa̱n tami̍ṣīcī̱r abhai̍ṣuḥ | ā somo̍ a̱smām̐ a̍ruha̱d vihā̍yā̱ aga̍nma̱ yatra̍ prati̱ranta̱ āyu̍ḥ ||
8.48.12	yo na̱ indu̍ḥ pitaro hṛ̱tsu pī̱to 'ma̍rtyo̱ martyā̍m̐ āvi̱veśa̍ | tasmai̱ somā̍ya ha̱viṣā̍ vidhema mṛḻī̱ke a̍sya suma̱tau syā̍ma ||
8.48.13	tvaṁ so̍ma pi̱tṛbhi̍ḥ saṁvidā̱no 'nu̱ dyāvā̍pṛthi̱vī ā ta̍tantha | tasmai̍ ta indo ha̱viṣā̍ vidhema va̱yaṁ syā̍ma̱ pata̍yo rayī̱ṇām ||
8.48.14	trātā̍ro devā̱ adhi̍ vocatā no̱ mā no̍ ni̱drā ī̍śata̱ mota jalpi̍ḥ | va̱yaṁ soma̍sya vi̱śvaha̍ pri̱yāsa̍ḥ su̱vīrā̍so vi̱datha̱m ā va̍dema ||
8.48.15	tvaṁ na̍ḥ soma vi̱śvato̍ vayo̱dhās tvaṁ sva̱rvid ā vi̍śā nṛ̱cakṣā̍ḥ | tvaṁ na̍ inda ū̱tibhi̍ḥ sa̱joṣā̍ḥ pā̱hi pa̱ścātā̍d u̱ta vā̍ pu̱rastā̍t ||
8.49.1	a̱bhi pra va̍ḥ su̱rādha̍sa̱m indra̍m arca̱ yathā̍ vi̱de | yo ja̍ri̱tṛbhyo̍ ma̱ghavā̍ purū̱vasu̍ḥ sa̱hasre̍ṇeva̱ śikṣa̍ti ||
8.49.2	śa̱tānī̍keva̱ pra ji̍gāti dhṛṣṇu̱yā hanti̍ vṛ̱trāṇi̍ dā̱śuṣe̍ | gi̱rer i̍va̱ pra rasā̍ asya pinvire̱ datrā̍ṇi puru̱bhoja̍saḥ ||
8.49.3	ā tvā̍ su̱tāsa̱ inda̍vo̱ madā̱ ya i̍ndra girvaṇaḥ | āpo̱ na va̍jri̱nn anv o̱kya1̱̍ṁ sara̍ḥ pṛ̱ṇanti̍ śūra̱ rādha̍se ||
8.49.4	a̱ne̱hasa̍m pra̱tara̍ṇaṁ vi̱vakṣa̍ṇa̱m madhva̱ḥ svādi̍ṣṭham īm piba | ā yathā̍ mandasā̱naḥ ki̱rāsi̍ na̱ḥ pra kṣu̱dreva̱ tmanā̍ dhṛ̱ṣat ||
8.49.5	ā na̱ḥ stoma̱m upa̍ dra̱vad dhi̍yā̱no aśvo̱ na sotṛ̍bhiḥ | yaṁ te̍ svadhāvan sva̱daya̍nti dhe̱nava̱ indra̱ kaṇve̍ṣu rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.49.6	u̱graṁ na vī̱raṁ nama̱sopa̍ sedima̱ vibhū̍ti̱m akṣi̍tāvasum | u̱drīva̍ vajrinn ava̱to na si̍ñca̱te kṣara̍ntīndra dhī̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.49.7	yad dha̍ nū̱naṁ yad vā̍ ya̱jñe yad vā̍ pṛthi̱vyām adhi̍ | ato̍ no ya̱jñam ā̱śubhi̍r mahemata u̱gra u̱grebhi̱r ā ga̍hi ||
8.49.8	a̱ji̱rāso̱ hara̍yo̱ ye ta̍ ā̱śavo̱ vātā̍ iva prasa̱kṣiṇa̍ḥ | yebhi̱r apa̍tya̱m manu̍ṣaḥ pa̱rīya̍se̱ yebhi̱r viśva̱ṁ sva̍r dṛ̱śe ||
8.49.9	e̱tāva̍tas ta īmaha̱ indra̍ su̱mnasya̱ goma̍taḥ | yathā̱ prāvo̍ maghava̱n medhyā̍tithi̱ṁ yathā̱ nīpā̍tithi̱ṁ dhane̍ ||
8.49.10	yathā̱ kaṇve̍ maghavan tra̱sada̍syavi̱ yathā̍ pa̱kthe daśa̍vraje | yathā̱ gośa̍rye̱ asa̍nor ṛ̱jiśva̱nīndra̱ goma̱d dhira̍ṇyavat ||
8.50.1	pra su śru̱taṁ su̱rādha̍sa̱m arcā̍ śa̱kram a̱bhiṣṭa̍ye | yaḥ su̍nva̱te stu̍va̱te kāmya̱ṁ vasu̍ sa̱hasre̍ṇeva̱ maṁha̍te ||
8.50.2	śa̱tānī̍kā he̱tayo̍ asya du̱ṣṭarā̱ indra̍sya sa̱miṣo̍ ma̱hīḥ | gi̱rir na bhu̱jmā ma̱ghava̍tsu pinvate̱ yad ī̍ṁ su̱tā ama̍ndiṣuḥ ||
8.50.3	yad ī̍ṁ su̱tāsa̱ inda̍vo̱ 'bhi pri̱yam ama̍ndiṣuḥ | āpo̱ na dhā̍yi̱ sava̍nam ma̱ ā va̍so̱ dughā̍ i̱vopa̍ dā̱śuṣe̍ ||
8.50.4	a̱ne̱hasa̍ṁ vo̱ hava̍mānam ū̱taye̱ madhva̍ḥ kṣaranti dhī̱taya̍ḥ | ā tvā̍ vaso̱ hava̍mānāsa̱ inda̍va̱ upa̍ sto̱treṣu̍ dadhire ||
8.50.5	ā na̱ḥ some̍ svadhva̱ra i̍yā̱no atyo̱ na to̍śate | yaṁ te̍ svadāva̱n svada̍nti gū̱rtaya̍ḥ pau̱re cha̍ndayase̱ hava̍m ||
8.50.6	pra vī̱ram u̱graṁ vivi̍ciṁ dhana̱spṛta̱ṁ vibhū̍ti̱ṁ rādha̍so ma̱haḥ | u̱drīva̍ vajrinn ava̱to va̍sutva̱nā sadā̍ pīpetha dā̱śuṣe̍ ||
8.50.7	yad dha̍ nū̱nam pa̍rā̱vati̱ yad vā̍ pṛthi̱vyāṁ di̱vi | yu̱jā̱na i̍ndra̱ hari̍bhir mahemata ṛ̱ṣva ṛ̱ṣvebhi̱r ā ga̍hi ||
8.50.8	ra̱thi̱rāso̱ hara̍yo̱ ye te̍ a̱sridha̱ ojo̱ vāta̍sya̱ pipra̍ti | yebhi̱r ni dasyu̱m manu̍ṣo ni̱ghoṣa̍yo̱ yebhi̱ḥ sva̍ḥ pa̱rīya̍se ||
8.50.9	e̱tāva̍tas te vaso vi̱dyāma̍ śūra̱ navya̍saḥ | yathā̱ prāva̱ eta̍śa̱ṁ kṛtvye̱ dhane̱ yathā̱ vaśa̱ṁ daśa̍vraje ||
8.50.10	yathā̱ kaṇve̍ maghava̱n medhe̍ adhva̱re dī̱rghanī̍the̱ damū̍nasi | yathā̱ gośa̍rye̱ asi̍ṣāso adrivo̱ mayi̍ go̱traṁ ha̍ri̱śriya̍m ||
8.51.1	yathā̱ manau̱ sāṁva̍raṇau̱ soma̍m i̱ndrāpi̍baḥ su̱tam | nīpā̍tithau maghava̱n medhyā̍tithau̱ puṣṭi̍gau̱ śruṣṭi̍gau̱ sacā̍ ||
8.51.2	pā̱rṣa̱dvā̱ṇaḥ praska̍ṇva̱ṁ sam a̍sādaya̱c chayā̍na̱ṁ jivri̱m uddhi̍tam | sa̱hasrā̍ṇy asiṣāsa̱d gavā̱m ṛṣi̱s tvoto̱ dasya̍ve̱ vṛka̍ḥ ||
8.51.3	ya u̱kthebhi̱r na vi̱ndhate̍ ci̱kid ya ṛ̍ṣi̱coda̍naḥ | indra̱ṁ tam acchā̍ vada̱ navya̍syā ma̱ty ari̍ṣyanta̱ṁ na bhoja̍se ||
8.51.4	yasmā̍ a̱rkaṁ sa̱ptaśī̍rṣāṇam ānṛ̱cus tri̱dhātu̍m utta̱me pa̱de | sa tv i1̱̍mā viśvā̱ bhuva̍nāni cikrada̱d ād ij ja̍niṣṭa̱ pauṁsya̍m ||
8.51.5	yo no̍ dā̱tā vasū̍nā̱m indra̱ṁ taṁ hū̍mahe va̱yam | vi̱dmā hy a̍sya suma̱tiṁ navī̍yasīṁ ga̱mema̱ goma̍ti vra̱je ||
8.51.6	yasmai̱ tvaṁ va̍so dā̱nāya̱ śikṣa̍si̱ sa rā̱yas poṣa̍m aśnute | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yam ma̍ghavann indra girvaṇaḥ su̱tāva̍nto havāmahe ||
8.51.7	ka̱dā ca̱na sta̱rīr a̍si̱ nendra̍ saścasi dā̱śuṣe̍ | upo̱pen nu ma̍ghava̱n bhūya̱ in nu te̱ dāna̍ṁ de̱vasya̍ pṛcyate ||
8.51.8	pra yo na̍na̱kṣe a̱bhy oja̍sā̱ krivi̍ṁ va̱dhaiḥ śuṣṇa̍ṁ nigho̱ṣaya̍n | ya̱ded asta̍mbhīt pra̱thaya̍nn a̱mūṁ diva̱m ād ij ja̍niṣṭa̱ pārthi̍vaḥ ||
8.51.9	yasyā̱yaṁ viśva̱ āryo̱ dāsa̍ḥ śevadhi̱pā a̱riḥ | ti̱raś ci̍d a̱rye ruśa̍me̱ parī̍ravi̱ tubhyet so a̍jyate ra̱yiḥ ||
8.51.10	tu̱ra̱ṇyavo̱ madhu̍mantaṁ ghṛta̱ścuta̱ṁ viprā̍so a̱rkam ā̍nṛcuḥ | a̱sme ra̱yiḥ pa̍prathe̱ vṛṣṇya̱ṁ śavo̱ 'sme su̍vā̱nāsa̱ inda̍vaḥ ||
8.52.1	yathā̱ manau̱ viva̍svati̱ soma̍ṁ śa̱krāpi̍baḥ su̱tam | yathā̍ tri̱te chanda̍ indra̱ jujo̍ṣasy ā̱yau mā̍dayase̱ sacā̍ ||
8.52.2	pṛṣa̍dhre̱ medhye̍ māta̱riśva̱nīndra̍ suvā̱ne ama̍ndathāḥ | yathā̱ soma̱ṁ daśa̍śipre̱ daśo̍ṇye̱ syūma̍raśmā̱v ṛjū̍nasi ||
8.52.3	ya u̱kthā keva̍lā da̱dhe yaḥ soma̍ṁ dhṛṣi̱tāpi̍bat | yasmai̱ viṣṇu̱s trīṇi̍ pa̱dā vi̍cakra̱ma upa̍ mi̱trasya̱ dharma̍bhiḥ ||
8.52.4	yasya̱ tvam i̍ndra̱ stome̍ṣu cā̱kano̱ vāje̍ vājiñ chatakrato | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yaṁ su̱dughā̍m iva go̱duho̍ juhū̱masi̍ śrava̱syava̍ḥ ||
8.52.5	yo no̍ dā̱tā sa na̍ḥ pi̱tā ma̱hām̐ u̱gra ī̍śāna̱kṛt | ayā̍mann u̱gro ma̱ghavā̍ purū̱vasu̱r gor aśva̍sya̱ pra dā̍tu naḥ ||
8.52.6	yasmai̱ tvaṁ va̍so dā̱nāya̱ maṁha̍se̱ sa rā̱yas poṣa̍m invati | va̱sū̱yavo̱ vasu̍patiṁ śa̱takra̍tu̱ṁ stomai̱r indra̍ṁ havāmahe ||
8.52.7	ka̱dā ca̱na pra yu̍cchasy u̱bhe ni pā̍si̱ janma̍nī | turī̍yāditya̱ hava̍naṁ ta indri̱yam ā ta̍sthāv a̱mṛta̍ṁ di̱vi ||
8.52.8	yasmai̱ tvam ma̍ghavann indra girvaṇa̱ḥ śikṣo̱ śikṣa̍si dā̱śuṣe̍ | a̱smāka̱ṁ gira̍ u̱ta su̍ṣṭu̱tiṁ va̍so kaṇva̱vac chṛ̍ṇudhī̱ hava̍m ||
8.52.9	astā̍vi̱ manma̍ pū̱rvyam brahmendrā̍ya vocata | pū̱rvīr ṛ̱tasya̍ bṛha̱tīr a̍nūṣata sto̱tur me̱dhā a̍sṛkṣata ||
8.52.10	sam indro̱ rāyo̍ bṛha̱tīr a̍dhūnuta̱ saṁ kṣo̱ṇī sam u̱ sūrya̍m | saṁ śu̱krāsa̱ḥ śuca̍ya̱ḥ saṁ gavā̍śira̱ḥ somā̱ indra̍m amandiṣuḥ ||
8.53.1	u̱pa̱maṁ tvā̍ ma̱ghonā̱ṁ jyeṣṭha̍ṁ ca vṛṣa̱bhāṇā̍m | pū̱rbhitta̍mam maghavann indra go̱vida̱m īśā̍naṁ rā̱ya ī̍mahe ||
8.53.2	ya ā̱yuṁ kutsa̍m atithi̱gvam arda̍yo vāvṛdhā̱no di̱ve-di̍ve | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yaṁ harya̍śvaṁ śa̱takra̍tuṁ vāja̱yanto̍ havāmahe ||
8.53.3	ā no̱ viśve̍ṣā̱ṁ rasa̱m madhva̍ḥ siñca̱ntv adra̍yaḥ | ye pa̍rā̱vati̍ sunvi̱re jane̱ṣv ā ye a̍rvā̱vatīnda̍vaḥ ||
8.53.4	viśvā̱ dveṣā̍ṁsi ja̱hi cāva̱ cā kṛ̍dhi̱ viśve̍ sanva̱ntv ā vasu̍ | śīṣṭe̍ṣu cit te madi̱rāso̍ a̱ṁśavo̱ yatrā̱ soma̍sya tṛ̱mpasi̍ ||
8.53.5	indra̱ nedī̍ya̱ ed i̍hi mi̱tame̍dhābhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | ā śa̍ṁtama̱ śaṁta̍mābhir a̱bhiṣṭi̍bhi̱r ā svā̍pe svā̱pibhi̍ḥ ||
8.53.6	ā̱ji̱tura̱ṁ satpa̍tiṁ vi̱śvaca̍rṣaṇiṁ kṛ̱dhi pra̱jāsv ābha̍gam | pra sū ti̍rā̱ śacī̍bhi̱r ye ta̍ u̱kthina̱ḥ kratu̍m puna̱ta ā̍nu̱ṣak ||
8.53.7	yas te̱ sādhi̱ṣṭho 'va̍se̱ te syā̍ma̱ bhare̍ṣu te | va̱yaṁ hotrā̍bhir u̱ta de̱vahū̍tibhiḥ sasa̱vāṁso̍ manāmahe ||
8.53.8	a̱haṁ hi te̍ harivo̱ brahma̍ vāja̱yur ā̱jiṁ yāmi̱ sado̱tibhi̍ḥ | tvām id e̱va tam ame̱ sam a̍śva̱yur ga̱vyur agre̍ mathī̱nām ||
8.54.1	e̱tat ta̍ indra vī̱rya̍ṁ gī̱rbhir gṛ̱ṇanti̍ kā̱rava̍ḥ | te stobha̍nta̱ ūrja̍m āvan ghṛta̱ścuta̍m pau̱rāso̍ nakṣan dhī̱tibhi̍ḥ ||
8.54.2	nakṣa̍nta̱ indra̱m ava̍se sukṛ̱tyayā̱ yeṣā̍ṁ su̱teṣu̱ manda̍se | yathā̍ saṁva̱rte ama̍do̱ yathā̍ kṛ̱śa e̱vāsme i̍ndra matsva ||
8.54.3	ā no̱ viśve̍ sa̱joṣa̍so̱ devā̍so̱ ganta̱nopa̍ naḥ | vasa̍vo ru̱drā ava̍se na̱ ā ga̍mañ chṛ̱ṇvantu̍ ma̱ruto̱ hava̍m ||
8.54.4	pū̱ṣā viṣṇu̱r hava̍nam me̱ sara̍sva̱ty ava̍ntu sa̱pta sindha̍vaḥ | āpo̱ vāta̱ḥ parva̍tāso̱ vana̱spati̍ḥ śṛ̱ṇotu̍ pṛthi̱vī hava̍m ||
8.54.5	yad i̍ndra̱ rādho̱ asti̍ te̱ māgho̍nam maghavattama | tena̍ no bodhi sadha̱mādyo̍ vṛ̱dhe bhago̍ dā̱nāya̍ vṛtrahan ||
8.54.6	āji̍pate nṛpate̱ tvam id dhi no̱ vāja̱ ā va̍kṣi sukrato | vī̱tī hotrā̍bhir u̱ta de̱vavī̍tibhiḥ sasa̱vāṁso̱ vi śṛ̍ṇvire ||
8.54.7	santi̱ hy a1̱̍rya ā̱śiṣa̱ indra̱ āyu̱r janā̍nām | a̱smān na̍kṣasva maghava̱nn upāva̍se dhu̱kṣasva̍ pi̱pyuṣī̱m iṣa̍m ||
8.54.8	va̱yaṁ ta̍ indra̱ stome̍bhir vidhema̱ tvam a̱smāka̍ṁ śatakrato | mahi̍ sthū̱raṁ śa̍śa̱yaṁ rādho̱ ahra̍ya̱m praska̍ṇvāya̱ ni to̍śaya ||
8.55.1	bhūrīd indra̍sya vī̱rya1̱̍ṁ vy akhya̍m a̱bhy āya̍ti | rādha̍s te dasyave vṛka ||
8.55.2	śa̱taṁ śve̱tāsa̍ u̱kṣaṇo̍ di̱vi tāro̱ na ro̍cante | ma̱hnā diva̱ṁ na ta̍stabhuḥ ||
8.55.3	śa̱taṁ ve̱ṇūñ cha̱taṁ śuna̍ḥ śa̱taṁ carmā̍ṇi mlā̱tāni̍ | śa̱tam me̍ balbajastu̱kā aru̍ṣīṇā̱ṁ catu̍ḥśatam ||
8.55.4	su̱de̱vāḥ stha̍ kāṇvāyanā̱ vayo̍-vayo vica̱ranta̍ḥ | aśvā̍so̱ na ca̍ṅkramata ||
8.55.5	ād it sā̱ptasya̍ carkira̱nn ānū̍nasya̱ mahi̱ śrava̍ḥ | śyāvī̍r atidhva̱san pa̱thaś cakṣu̍ṣā ca̱na sa̱ṁnaśe̍ ||
8.56.1	prati̍ te dasyave vṛka̱ rādho̍ ada̱rśy ahra̍yam | dyaur na pra̍thi̱nā śava̍ḥ ||
8.56.2	daśa̱ mahya̍m pautakra̱taḥ sa̱hasrā̱ dasya̍ve̱ vṛka̍ḥ | nityā̍d rā̱yo a̍maṁhata ||
8.56.3	śa̱tam me̍ garda̱bhānā̍ṁ śa̱tam ūrṇā̍vatīnām | śa̱taṁ dā̱sām̐ ati̱ sraja̍ḥ ||
8.56.4	tatro̱ api̱ prāṇī̍yata pū̱takra̍tāyai̱ vya̍ktā | aśvā̍nā̱m in na yū̱thyā̍m ||
8.56.5	ace̍ty a̱gniś ci̍ki̱tur ha̍vya̱vāṭ sa su̱madra̍thaḥ | a̱gniḥ śu̱kreṇa̍ śo̱ciṣā̍ bṛ̱hat sūro̍ arocata di̱vi sūryo̍ arocata ||
8.57.1	yu̱vaṁ de̍vā̱ kratu̍nā pū̱rvyeṇa̍ yu̱ktā rathe̍na tavi̱ṣaṁ ya̍jatrā | āga̍cchataṁ nāsatyā̱ śacī̍bhir i̱daṁ tṛ̱tīya̱ṁ sava̍nam pibāthaḥ ||
8.57.2	yu̱vāṁ de̱vās traya̍ ekāda̱śāsa̍ḥ sa̱tyāḥ sa̱tyasya̍ dadṛśe pu̱rastā̍t | a̱smāka̍ṁ ya̱jñaṁ sava̍naṁ juṣā̱ṇā pā̱taṁ soma̍m aśvinā̱ dīdya̍gnī ||
8.57.3	pa̱nāyya̱ṁ tad a̍śvinā kṛ̱taṁ vā̍ṁ vṛṣa̱bho di̱vo raja̍saḥ pṛthi̱vyāḥ | sa̱hasra̱ṁ śaṁsā̍ u̱ta ye gavi̍ṣṭau̱ sarvā̱m̐ it tām̐ upa̍ yātā̱ piba̍dhyai ||
8.57.4	a̱yaṁ vā̍m bhā̱go nihi̍to yajatre̱mā giro̍ nāsa̱tyopa̍ yātam | piba̍ta̱ṁ soma̱m madhu̍mantam a̱sme pra dā̱śvāṁsa̍m avata̱ṁ śacī̍bhiḥ ||
8.58.1	yam ṛ̱tvijo̍ bahu̱dhā ka̱lpaya̍nta̱ḥ sace̍taso ya̱jñam i̱maṁ vaha̍nti | yo a̍nūcā̱no brā̍hma̱ṇo yu̱kta ā̍sī̱t kā svi̱t tatra̱ yaja̍mānasya sa̱ṁvit ||
8.58.2	eka̍ e̱vāgnir ba̍hu̱dhā sami̍ddha̱ eka̱ḥ sūryo̱ viśva̱m anu̱ prabhū̍taḥ | ekai̱voṣāḥ sarva̍m i̱daṁ vi bhā̱ty eka̱ṁ vā i̱daṁ vi ba̍bhūva̱ sarva̍m ||
8.58.3	jyoti̍ṣmantaṁ ketu̱manta̍ṁ trica̱kraṁ su̱khaṁ ratha̍ṁ su̱ṣada̱m bhūri̍vāram | ci̱trāma̍ghā̱ yasya̱ yoge̍ 'dhijajñe̱ taṁ vā̍ṁ hu̱ve ati̍ rikta̱m piba̍dhyai ||
8.59.1	i̱māni̍ vām bhāga̱dheyā̍ni sisrata̱ indrā̍varuṇā̱ pra ma̱he su̱teṣu̍ vām | ya̱jñe-ya̍jñe ha̱ sava̍nā bhura̱ṇyatho̱ yat su̍nva̱te yaja̍mānāya̱ śikṣa̍thaḥ ||
8.59.2	ni̱ṣṣidhva̍rī̱r oṣa̍dhī̱r āpa̍ āstā̱m indrā̍varuṇā mahi̱māna̱m āśa̍ta | yā sisra̍tū̱ raja̍saḥ pā̱re adhva̍no̱ yayo̱ḥ śatru̱r naki̱r āde̍va̱ oha̍te ||
8.59.3	sa̱tyaṁ tad i̍ndrāvaruṇā kṛ̱śasya̍ vā̱m madhva̍ ū̱rmiṁ du̍hate sa̱pta vāṇī̍ḥ | tābhi̍r dā̱śvāṁsa̍m avataṁ śubhas patī̱ yo vā̱m ada̍bdho a̱bhi pāti̱ citti̍bhiḥ ||
8.59.4	ghṛ̱ta̱pruṣa̱ḥ saumyā̍ jī̱radā̍navaḥ sa̱pta svasā̍ra̱ḥ sada̍na ṛ̱tasya̍ | yā ha̍ vām indrāvaruṇā ghṛta̱ścuta̱s tābhi̍r dhatta̱ṁ yaja̍mānāya śikṣatam ||
8.59.5	avo̍cāma maha̱te saubha̍gāya sa̱tyaṁ tve̱ṣābhyā̍m mahi̱māna̍m indri̱yam | a̱smān sv i̍ndrāvaruṇā ghṛta̱ścuta̱s tribhi̍ḥ sā̱ptebhi̍r avataṁ śubhas patī ||
8.59.6	indrā̍varuṇā̱ yad ṛ̱ṣibhyo̍ manī̱ṣāṁ vā̱co ma̱tiṁ śru̱tam a̍datta̱m agre̍ | yāni̱ sthānā̍ny asṛjanta̱ dhīrā̍ ya̱jñaṁ ta̍nvā̱nās tapa̍sā̱bhy a̍paśyam ||
8.59.7	indrā̍varuṇā saumana̱sam adṛ̍ptaṁ rā̱yas poṣa̱ṁ yaja̍māneṣu dhattam | pra̱jām pu̱ṣṭim bhū̍tim a̱smāsu̍ dhattaṁ dīrghāyu̱tvāya̱ pra ti̍rataṁ na̱ āyu̍ḥ ||
8.60.1	agna̱ ā yā̍hy a̱gnibhi̱r hotā̍raṁ tvā vṛṇīmahe | ā tvām a̍naktu̱ praya̍tā ha̱viṣma̍tī̱ yaji̍ṣṭham ba̱rhir ā̱sade̍ ||
8.60.2	acchā̱ hi tvā̍ sahasaḥ sūno aṅgira̱ḥ sruca̱ś cara̍nty adhva̱re | ū̱rjo napā̍taṁ ghṛ̱take̍śam īmahe̱ 'gniṁ ya̱jñeṣu̍ pū̱rvyam ||
8.60.3	agne̍ ka̱vir ve̱dhā a̍si̱ hotā̍ pāvaka̱ yakṣya̍ḥ | ma̱ndro yaji̍ṣṭho adhva̱reṣv īḍyo̱ vipre̍bhiḥ śukra̱ manma̍bhiḥ ||
8.60.4	adro̍gha̱m ā va̍hośa̱to ya̍viṣṭhya de̱vām̐ a̍jasra vī̱taye̍ | a̱bhi prayā̍ṁsi̱ sudhi̱tā va̍so gahi̱ manda̍sva dhī̱tibhi̍r hi̱taḥ ||
8.60.5	tvam it sa̱prathā̍ a̱sy agne̍ trātar ṛ̱tas ka̱viḥ | tvāṁ viprā̍saḥ samidhāna dīdiva̱ ā vi̍vāsanti ve̱dhasa̍ḥ ||
8.60.6	śocā̍ śociṣṭha dīdi̱hi vi̱śe mayo̱ rāsva̍ sto̱tre ma̱hām̐ a̍si | de̱vānā̱ṁ śarma̱n mama̍ santu sū̱raya̍ḥ śatrū̱ṣāha̍ḥ sva̱gnaya̍ḥ ||
8.60.7	yathā̍ cid vṛ̱ddham a̍ta̱sam agne̍ sa̱ṁjūrva̍si̱ kṣami̍ | e̱vā da̍ha mitramaho̱ yo a̍sma̱dhrug du̱rmanmā̱ kaś ca̱ vena̍ti ||
8.60.8	mā no̱ martā̍ya ri̱pave̍ rakṣa̱svine̱ māghaśa̍ṁsāya rīradhaḥ | asre̍dhadbhis ta̱raṇi̍bhir yaviṣṭhya śi̱vebhi̍ḥ pāhi pā̱yubhi̍ḥ ||
8.60.9	pā̱hi no̍ agna̱ eka̍yā pā̱hy u1̱̍ta dvi̱tīya̍yā | pā̱hi gī̱rbhis ti̱sṛbhi̍r ūrjām pate pā̱hi ca̍ta̱sṛbhi̍r vaso ||
8.60.10	pā̱hi viśva̍smād ra̱kṣaso̱ arā̍vṇa̱ḥ pra sma̱ vāje̍ṣu no 'va | tvām id dhi nedi̍ṣṭhaṁ de̱vatā̍taya ā̱piṁ nakṣā̍mahe vṛ̱dhe ||
8.60.11	ā no̍ agne vayo̱vṛdha̍ṁ ra̱yim pā̍vaka̱ śaṁsya̍m | rāsvā̍ ca na upamāte puru̱spṛha̱ṁ sunī̍tī̱ svaya̍śastaram ||
8.60.12	yena̱ vaṁsā̍ma̱ pṛta̍nāsu̱ śardha̍ta̱s tara̍nto a̱rya ā̱diśa̍ḥ | sa tvaṁ no̍ vardha̱ praya̍sā śacīvaso̱ jinvā̱ dhiyo̍ vasu̱vida̍ḥ ||
8.60.13	śiśā̍no vṛṣa̱bho ya̍thā̱gniḥ śṛṅge̱ davi̍dhvat | ti̱gmā a̍sya̱ hana̍vo̱ na pra̍ti̱dhṛṣe̍ su̱jambha̱ḥ saha̍so ya̱huḥ ||
8.60.14	na̱hi te̍ agne vṛṣabha prati̱dhṛṣe̱ jambhā̍so̱ yad vi̱tiṣṭha̍se | sa tvaṁ no̍ hota̱ḥ suhu̍taṁ ha̱viṣ kṛ̍dhi̱ vaṁsvā̍ no̱ vāryā̍ pu̱ru ||
8.60.15	śeṣe̱ vane̍ṣu mā̱troḥ saṁ tvā̱ martā̍sa indhate | ata̍ndro ha̱vyā va̍hasi havi̱ṣkṛta̱ ād id de̱veṣu̍ rājasi ||
8.60.16	sa̱pta hotā̍ra̱s tam id ī̍ḻate̱ tvāgne̍ su̱tyaja̱m ahra̍yam | bhi̱natsy adri̱ṁ tapa̍sā̱ vi śo̱ciṣā̱ prāgne̍ tiṣṭha̱ janā̱m̐ ati̍ ||
8.60.17	a̱gnim-a̍gniṁ vo̱ adhri̍guṁ hu̱vema̍ vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣaḥ | a̱gniṁ hi̱tapra̍yasaḥ śaśva̱tīṣv ā hotā̍raṁ carṣaṇī̱nām ||
8.60.18	kete̍na̱ śarma̍n sacate suṣā̱maṇy agne̱ tubhya̍ṁ ciki̱tvanā̍ | i̱ṣa̱ṇyayā̍ naḥ puru̱rūpa̱m ā bha̍ra̱ vāja̱ṁ nedi̍ṣṭham ū̱taye̍ ||
8.60.19	agne̱ jari̍tar vi̱śpati̍s tepā̱no de̍va ra̱kṣasa̍ḥ | apro̍ṣivān gṛ̱hapa̍tir ma̱hām̐ a̍si di̱vas pā̱yur du̍roṇa̱yuḥ ||
8.60.20	mā no̱ rakṣa̱ ā ve̍śīd āghṛṇīvaso̱ mā yā̱tur yā̍tu̱māva̍tām | pa̱ro̱ga̱vyū̱ty ani̍rā̱m apa̱ kṣudha̱m agne̱ sedha̍ rakṣa̱svina̍ḥ ||
8.61.1	u̱bhaya̍ṁ śṛ̱ṇava̍c ca na̱ indro̍ a̱rvāg i̱daṁ vaca̍ḥ | sa̱trācyā̍ ma̱ghavā̱ soma̍pītaye dhi̱yā śavi̍ṣṭha̱ ā ga̍mat ||
8.61.2	taṁ hi sva̱rāja̍ṁ vṛṣa̱bhaṁ tam oja̍se dhi̱ṣaṇe̍ niṣṭata̱kṣatu̍ḥ | u̱topa̱mānā̍m pratha̱mo ni ṣī̍dasi̱ soma̍kāma̱ṁ hi te̱ mana̍ḥ ||
8.61.3	ā vṛ̍ṣasva purūvaso su̱tasye̱ndrāndha̍saḥ | vi̱dmā hi tvā̍ harivaḥ pṛ̱tsu sā̍sa̱him adhṛ̍ṣṭaṁ cid dadhṛ̱ṣvaṇi̍m ||
8.61.4	aprā̍misatya maghava̱n tathed a̍sa̱d indra̱ kratvā̱ yathā̱ vaśa̍ḥ | sa̱nema̱ vāja̱ṁ tava̍ śipri̱nn ava̍sā ma̱kṣū ci̱d yanto̍ adrivaḥ ||
8.61.5	śa̱gdhy ū̱3̱̍ ṣu śa̍cīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | bhaga̱ṁ na hi tvā̍ ya̱śasa̍ṁ vasu̱vida̱m anu̍ śūra̱ carā̍masi ||
8.61.6	pau̱ro aśva̍sya puru̱kṛd gavā̍m a̱sy utso̍ deva hira̱ṇyaya̍ḥ | naki̱r hi dāna̍m pari̱mardhi̍ṣa̱t tve yad-ya̱d yāmi̱ tad ā bha̍ra ||
8.61.7	tvaṁ hy ehi̱ cera̍ve vi̱dā bhaga̱ṁ vasu̍ttaye | ud vā̍vṛṣasva maghava̱n gavi̍ṣṭaya̱ ud i̱ndrāśva̍miṣṭaye ||
8.61.8	tvam pu̱rū sa̱hasrā̍ṇi śa̱tāni̍ ca yū̱thā dā̱nāya̍ maṁhase | ā pu̍raṁda̱raṁ ca̍kṛma̱ vipra̍vacasa̱ indra̱ṁ gāya̱nto 'va̍se ||
8.61.9	a̱vi̱pro vā̱ yad avi̍dha̱d vipro̍ vendra te̱ vaca̍ḥ | sa pra ma̍mandat tvā̱yā śa̍takrato̱ prācā̍manyo̱ aha̍ṁsana ||
8.61.10	u̱grabā̍hur mrakṣa̱kṛtvā̍ puraṁda̱ro yadi̍ me śṛ̱ṇava̱d dhava̍m | va̱sū̱yavo̱ vasu̍patiṁ śa̱takra̍tu̱ṁ stomai̱r indra̍ṁ havāmahe ||
8.61.11	na pā̱pāso̍ manāmahe̱ nārā̍yāso̱ na jaḻha̍vaḥ | yad in nv indra̱ṁ vṛṣa̍ṇa̱ṁ sacā̍ su̱te sakhā̍yaṁ kṛ̱ṇavā̍mahai ||
8.61.12	u̱graṁ yu̍yujma̱ pṛta̍nāsu sāsa̱him ṛ̱ṇakā̍ti̱m adā̍bhyam | vedā̍ bhṛ̱maṁ ci̱t sani̍tā ra̱thīta̍mo vā̱jina̱ṁ yam id ū̱ naśa̍t ||
8.61.13	yata̍ indra̱ bhayā̍mahe̱ tato̍ no̱ abha̍yaṁ kṛdhi | magha̍vañ cha̱gdhi tava̱ tan na̍ ū̱tibhi̱r vi dviṣo̱ vi mṛdho̍ jahi ||
8.61.14	tvaṁ hi rā̍dhaspate̱ rādha̍so ma̱haḥ kṣaya̱syāsi̍ vidha̱taḥ | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yam ma̍ghavann indra girvaṇaḥ su̱tāva̍nto havāmahe ||
8.61.15	indra̱ḥ spaḻ u̱ta vṛ̍tra̱hā pa̍ra̱spā no̱ vare̍ṇyaḥ | sa no̍ rakṣiṣac cara̱maṁ sa ma̍dhya̱maṁ sa pa̱ścāt pā̍tu naḥ pu̱raḥ ||
8.61.16	tvaṁ na̍ḥ pa̱ścād a̍dha̱rād u̍tta̱rāt pu̱ra indra̱ ni pā̍hi vi̱śvata̍ḥ | ā̱re a̱smat kṛ̍ṇuhi̱ daivya̍m bha̱yam ā̱re he̱tīr ade̍vīḥ ||
8.61.17	a̱dyādyā̱ śvaḥ-śva̱ indra̱ trāsva̍ pa̱re ca̍ naḥ | viśvā̍ ca no jari̱tṝn sa̍tpate̱ ahā̱ divā̱ nakta̍ṁ ca rakṣiṣaḥ ||
8.61.18	pra̱bha̱ṅgī śūro̍ ma̱ghavā̍ tu̱vīma̍gha̱ḥ sammi̍ślo vi̱ryā̍ya̱ kam | u̱bhā te̍ bā̱hū vṛṣa̍ṇā śatakrato̱ ni yā vajra̍m mimi̱kṣatu̍ḥ ||
8.62.1	pro a̍smā̱ upa̍stuti̱m bhara̍tā̱ yaj jujo̍ṣati | u̱kthair indra̍sya̱ māhi̍na̱ṁ vayo̍ vardhanti so̱mino̍ bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.2	a̱yu̱jo asa̍mo̱ nṛbhi̱r eka̍ḥ kṛ̱ṣṭīr a̱yāsya̍ḥ | pū̱rvīr ati̱ pra vā̍vṛdhe̱ viśvā̍ jā̱tāny oja̍sā bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.3	ahi̍tena ci̱d arva̍tā jī̱radā̍nuḥ siṣāsati | pra̱vācya̍m indra̱ tat tava̍ vī̱ryā̍ṇi kariṣya̱to bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.4	ā yā̍hi kṛ̱ṇavā̍ma ta̱ indra̱ brahmā̍ṇi̱ vardha̍nā | yebhi̍ḥ śaviṣṭha cā̱kano̍ bha̱dram i̱ha śra̍vasya̱te bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.5	dhṛ̱ṣa̱taś ci̍d dhṛ̱ṣan mana̍ḥ kṛ̱ṇoṣī̍ndra̱ yat tvam | tī̱vraiḥ somai̍ḥ saparya̱to namo̍bhiḥ prati̱bhūṣa̍to bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.6	ava̍ caṣṭa̱ ṛcī̍ṣamo 'va̱tām̐ i̍va̱ mānu̍ṣaḥ | ju̱ṣṭvī dakṣa̍sya so̱mina̱ḥ sakhā̍yaṁ kṛṇute̱ yuja̍m bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.7	viśve̍ ta indra vī̱rya̍ṁ de̱vā anu̱ kratu̍ṁ daduḥ | bhuvo̱ viśva̍sya̱ gopa̍tiḥ puruṣṭuta bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.8	gṛ̱ṇe tad i̍ndra te̱ śava̍ upa̱maṁ de̱vatā̍taye | yad dhaṁsi̍ vṛ̱tram oja̍sā śacīpate bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.9	sama̍neva vapuṣya̱taḥ kṛ̱ṇava̱n mānu̍ṣā yu̱gā | vi̱de tad indra̱ś ceta̍na̱m adha̍ śru̱to bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.10	uj jā̱tam i̍ndra te̱ śava̱ ut tvām ut tava̱ kratu̍m | bhūri̍go̱ bhūri̍ vāvṛdhu̱r magha̍va̱n tava̱ śarma̍ṇi bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.11	a̱haṁ ca̱ tvaṁ ca̍ vṛtraha̱n saṁ yu̍jyāva sa̱nibhya̱ ā | a̱rā̱tī̱vā ci̍d adri̱vo 'nu̍ nau śūra maṁsate bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.62.12	sa̱tyam id vā u̱ taṁ va̱yam indra̍ṁ stavāma̱ nānṛ̍tam | ma̱hām̐ asu̍nvato va̱dho bhūri̱ jyotī̍ṁṣi sunva̱to bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.63.1	sa pū̱rvyo ma̱hānā̍ṁ ve̱naḥ kratu̍bhir ānaje | yasya̱ dvārā̱ manu̍ṣ pi̱tā de̱veṣu̱ dhiya̍ āna̱je ||
8.63.2	di̱vo māna̱ṁ not sa̍da̱n soma̍pṛṣṭhāso̱ adra̍yaḥ | u̱kthā brahma̍ ca̱ śaṁsyā̍ ||
8.63.3	sa vi̱dvām̐ aṅgi̍robhya̱ indro̱ gā a̍vṛṇo̱d apa̍ | stu̱ṣe tad a̍sya̱ pauṁsya̍m ||
8.63.4	sa pra̱tnathā̍ kavivṛ̱dha indro̍ vā̱kasya̍ va̱kṣaṇi̍ḥ | śi̱vo a̱rkasya̱ homa̍ny asma̱trā ga̱ntv ava̍se ||
8.63.5	ād ū̱ nu te̱ anu̱ kratu̱ṁ svāhā̱ vara̍sya̱ yajya̍vaḥ | śvā̱tram a̱rkā a̍nūṣa̱tendra̍ go̱trasya̍ dā̱vane̍ ||
8.63.6	indre̱ viśvā̍ni vī̱ryā̍ kṛ̱tāni̱ kartvā̍ni ca | yam a̱rkā a̍dhva̱raṁ vi̱duḥ ||
8.63.7	yat pāñca̍janyayā vi̱śendre̱ ghoṣā̱ asṛ̍kṣata | astṛ̍ṇād ba̱rhaṇā̍ vi̱po̱3̱̍ 'ryo māna̍sya̱ sa kṣaya̍ḥ ||
8.63.8	i̱yam u̍ te̱ anu̍ṣṭutiś cakṛ̱ṣe tāni̱ pauṁsyā̍ | prāva̍ś ca̱krasya̍ varta̱nim ||
8.63.9	a̱sya vṛṣṇo̱ vyoda̍na u̱ru kra̍miṣṭa jī̱vase̍ | yava̱ṁ na pa̱śva ā da̍de ||
8.63.10	tad dadhā̍nā ava̱syavo̍ yu̱ṣmābhi̱r dakṣa̍pitaraḥ | syāma̍ ma̱rutva̍to vṛ̱dhe ||
8.63.11	baḻ ṛ̱tviyā̍ya̱ dhāmna̱ ṛkva̍bhiḥ śūra nonumaḥ | jeṣā̍mendra̱ tvayā̍ yu̱jā ||
8.63.12	a̱sme ru̱drā me̱hanā̱ parva̍tāso vṛtra̱hatye̱ bhara̍hūtau sa̱joṣā̍ḥ | yaḥ śaṁsa̍te stuva̱te dhāyi̍ pa̱jra indra̍jyeṣṭhā a̱smām̐ a̍vantu de̱vāḥ ||
8.64.1	ut tvā̍ mandantu̱ stomā̍ḥ kṛṇu̱ṣva rādho̍ adrivaḥ | ava̍ brahma̱dviṣo̍ jahi ||
8.64.2	pa̱dā pa̱ṇīm̐r a̍rā̱dhaso̱ ni bā̍dhasva ma̱hām̐ a̍si | na̱hi tvā̱ kaś ca̱na prati̍ ||
8.64.3	tvam ī̍śiṣe su̱tānā̱m indra̱ tvam asu̍tānām | tvaṁ rājā̱ janā̍nām ||
8.64.4	ehi̱ prehi̱ kṣayo̍ di̱vy ā̱3̱̍ghoṣa̍ñ carṣaṇī̱nām | obhe pṛ̍ṇāsi̱ roda̍sī ||
8.64.5	tyaṁ ci̱t parva̍taṁ gi̱riṁ śa̱tava̍ntaṁ saha̱sriṇa̍m | vi sto̱tṛbhyo̍ rurojitha ||
8.64.6	va̱yam u̍ tvā̱ divā̍ su̱te va̱yaṁ nakta̍ṁ havāmahe | a̱smāka̱ṁ kāma̱m ā pṛ̍ṇa ||
8.64.7	kva1̱̍ sya vṛ̍ṣa̱bho yuvā̍ tuvi̱grīvo̱ anā̍nataḥ | bra̱hmā kas taṁ sa̍paryati ||
8.64.8	kasya̍ svi̱t sava̍na̱ṁ vṛṣā̍ juju̱ṣvām̐ ava̍ gacchati | indra̱ṁ ka u̍ svi̱d ā ca̍ke ||
8.64.9	kaṁ te̍ dā̱nā a̍sakṣata̱ vṛtra̍ha̱n kaṁ su̱vīryā̍ | u̱kthe ka u̍ svi̱d anta̍maḥ ||
8.64.10	a̱yaṁ te̱ mānu̍ṣe̱ jane̱ soma̍ḥ pū̱ruṣu̍ sūyate | tasyehi̱ pra dra̍vā̱ piba̍ ||
8.64.11	a̱yaṁ te̍ śarya̱ṇāva̍ti su̱ṣomā̍yā̱m adhi̍ pri̱yaḥ | ā̱rjī̱kīye̍ ma̱dinta̍maḥ ||
8.64.12	tam a̱dya rādha̍se ma̱he cāru̱m madā̍ya̱ ghṛṣva̍ye | ehī̍m indra̱ dravā̱ piba̍ ||
8.65.1	yad i̍ndra̱ prāg apā̱g uda̱ṅ nya̍g vā hū̱yase̱ nṛbhi̍ḥ | ā yā̍hi̱ tūya̍m ā̱śubhi̍ḥ ||
8.65.2	yad vā̍ pra̱srava̍ṇe di̱vo mā̱dayā̍se̱ sva̍rṇare | yad vā̍ samu̱dre andha̍saḥ ||
8.65.3	ā tvā̍ gī̱rbhir ma̱hām u̱ruṁ hu̱ve gām i̍va̱ bhoja̍se | indra̱ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.65.4	ā ta̍ indra mahi̱māna̱ṁ hara̍yo deva te̱ maha̍ḥ | rathe̍ vahantu̱ bibhra̍taḥ ||
8.65.5	indra̍ gṛṇī̱ṣa u̍ stu̱ṣe ma̱hām̐ u̱gra ī̍śāna̱kṛt | ehi̍ naḥ su̱tam piba̍ ||
8.65.6	su̱tāva̍ntas tvā va̱yam praya̍svanto havāmahe | i̱daṁ no̍ ba̱rhir ā̱sade̍ ||
8.65.7	yac ci̱d dhi śaśva̍tā̱m asīndra̱ sādhā̍raṇa̱s tvam | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yaṁ ha̍vāmahe ||
8.65.8	i̱daṁ te̍ so̱myam madhv adhu̍kṣa̱nn adri̍bhi̱r nara̍ḥ | ju̱ṣā̱ṇa i̍ndra̱ tat pi̍ba ||
8.65.9	viśvā̍m̐ a̱ryo vi̍pa̱ścito 'ti̍ khya̱s tūya̱m ā ga̍hi | a̱sme dhe̍hi̱ śravo̍ bṛ̱hat ||
8.65.10	dā̱tā me̱ pṛṣa̍tīnā̱ṁ rājā̍ hiraṇya̱vīnā̍m | mā de̍vā ma̱ghavā̍ riṣat ||
8.65.11	sa̱hasre̱ pṛṣa̍tīnā̱m adhi̍ śca̱ndram bṛ̱hat pṛ̱thu | śu̱kraṁ hira̍ṇya̱m ā da̍de ||
8.65.12	napā̍to du̱rgaha̍sya me sa̱hasre̍ṇa su̱rādha̍saḥ | śravo̍ de̱veṣv a̍krata ||
8.66.1	taro̍bhir vo vi̱dadva̍su̱m indra̍ṁ sa̱bādha̍ ū̱taye̍ | bṛ̱had gāya̍ntaḥ su̱taso̍me adhva̱re hu̱ve bhara̱ṁ na kā̱riṇa̍m ||
8.66.2	na yaṁ du̱dhrā vara̍nte̱ na sthi̱rā muro̱ made̍ suśi̱pram andha̍saḥ | ya ā̱dṛtyā̍ śaśamā̱nāya̍ sunva̱te dātā̍ jari̱tra u̱kthya̍m ||
8.66.3	yaḥ śa̱kro mṛ̱kṣo aśvyo̱ yo vā̱ kījo̍ hira̱ṇyaya̍ḥ | sa ū̱rvasya̍ rejaya̱ty apā̍vṛti̱m indro̱ gavya̍sya vṛtra̱hā ||
8.66.4	nikhā̍taṁ ci̱d yaḥ pu̍rusambhṛ̱taṁ vasūd id vapa̍ti dā̱śuṣe̍ | va̱jrī su̍śi̱pro harya̍śva̱ it ka̍ra̱d indra̱ḥ kratvā̱ yathā̱ vaśa̍t ||
8.66.5	yad vā̱vantha̍ puruṣṭuta pu̱rā ci̍c chūra nṛ̱ṇām | va̱yaṁ tat ta̍ indra̱ sam bha̍rāmasi ya̱jñam u̱kthaṁ tu̱raṁ vaca̍ḥ ||
8.66.6	sacā̱ some̍ṣu puruhūta vajrivo̱ madā̍ya dyukṣa somapāḥ | tvam id dhi bra̍hma̱kṛte̱ kāmya̱ṁ vasu̱ deṣṭha̍ḥ sunva̱te bhuva̍ḥ ||
8.66.7	va̱yam e̍nam i̱dā hyo 'pī̍peme̱ha va̱jriṇa̍m | tasmā̍ u a̱dya sa̍ma̱nā su̱tam bha̱rā nū̱nam bhū̍ṣata śru̱te ||
8.66.8	vṛka̍ś cid asya vāra̱ṇa u̍rā̱mathi̱r ā va̱yune̍ṣu bhūṣati | semaṁ na̱ḥ stoma̍ṁ jujuṣā̱ṇa ā ga̱hīndra̱ pra ci̱trayā̍ dhi̱yā ||
8.66.9	kad ū̱ nv a1̱̍syākṛ̍ta̱m indra̍syāsti̱ pauṁsya̍m | keno̱ nu ka̱ṁ śroma̍tena̱ na śu̍śruve ja̱nuṣa̱ḥ pari̍ vṛtra̱hā ||
8.66.10	kad ū̍ ma̱hīr adhṛ̍ṣṭā asya̱ tavi̍ṣī̱ḥ kad u̍ vṛtra̱ghno astṛ̍tam | indro̱ viśvā̍n beka̱nāṭā̍m̐ aha̱rdṛśa̍ u̱ta kratvā̍ pa̱ṇīm̐r a̱bhi ||
8.66.11	va̱yaṁ ghā̍ te̱ apū̱rvyendra̱ brahmā̍ṇi vṛtrahan | pu̱rū̱tamā̍saḥ puruhūta vajrivo bhṛ̱tiṁ na pra bha̍rāmasi ||
8.66.12	pū̱rvīś ci̱d dhi tve tu̍vikūrminn ā̱śaso̱ hava̍nta indro̱taya̍ḥ | ti̱raś ci̍d a̱ryaḥ sava̱nā va̍so gahi̱ śavi̍ṣṭha śru̱dhi me̱ hava̍m ||
8.66.13	va̱yaṁ ghā̍ te̱ tve id v indra̱ viprā̱ api̍ ṣmasi | na̱hi tvad a̱nyaḥ pu̍ruhūta̱ kaś ca̱na magha̍va̱nn asti̍ marḍi̱tā ||
8.66.14	tvaṁ no̍ a̱syā ama̍ter u̱ta kṣu̱dho̱3̱̍ 'bhiśa̍ste̱r ava̍ spṛdhi | tvaṁ na̍ ū̱tī tava̍ ci̱trayā̍ dhi̱yā śikṣā̍ śaciṣṭha gātu̱vit ||
8.66.15	soma̱ id va̍ḥ su̱to a̍stu̱ kala̍yo̱ mā bi̍bhītana | aped e̱ṣa dhva̱smāya̍ti sva̱yaṁ ghai̱ṣo apā̍yati ||
8.67.1	tyān nu kṣa̱triyā̱m̐ ava̍ ādi̱tyān yā̍ciṣāmahe | su̱mṛ̱ḻī̱kām̐ a̱bhiṣṭa̍ye ||
8.67.2	mi̱tro no̱ aty a̍ṁha̱tiṁ varu̍ṇaḥ parṣad arya̱mā | ā̱di̱tyāso̱ yathā̍ vi̱duḥ ||
8.67.3	teṣā̱ṁ hi ci̱tram u̱kthya1̱̍ṁ varū̍tha̱m asti̍ dā̱śuṣe̍ | ā̱di̱tyānā̍m ara̱ṁkṛte̍ ||
8.67.4	mahi̍ vo maha̱tām avo̱ varu̍ṇa̱ mitrārya̍man | avā̱ṁsy ā vṛ̍ṇīmahe ||
8.67.5	jī̱vān no̍ a̱bhi dhe̍ta̱nādi̍tyāsaḥ pu̱rā hathā̍t | kad dha̍ stha havanaśrutaḥ ||
8.67.6	yad va̍ḥ śrā̱ntāya̍ sunva̱te varū̍tha̱m asti̱ yac cha̱rdiḥ | tenā̍ no̱ adhi̍ vocata ||
8.67.7	asti̍ devā a̱ṁhor u̱rv asti̱ ratna̱m anā̍gasaḥ | ādi̍tyā̱ adbhu̍tainasaḥ ||
8.67.8	mā na̱ḥ setu̍ḥ siṣed a̱yam ma̱he vṛ̍ṇaktu na̱s pari̍ | indra̱ id dhi śru̱to va̱śī ||
8.67.9	mā no̍ mṛ̱cā ri̍pū̱ṇāṁ vṛ̍ji̱nānā̍m aviṣyavaḥ | devā̍ a̱bhi pra mṛ̍kṣata ||
8.67.10	u̱ta tvām a̍dite mahy a̱haṁ de̱vy upa̍ bruve | su̱mṛ̱ḻī̱kām a̱bhiṣṭa̍ye ||
8.67.11	parṣi̍ dī̱ne ga̍bhī̱ra ām̐ ugra̍putre̱ jighā̍ṁsataḥ | māki̍s to̱kasya̍ no riṣat ||
8.67.12	a̱ne̱ho na̍ uruvraja̱ urū̍ci̱ vi prasa̍rtave | kṛ̱dhi to̱kāya̍ jī̱vase̍ ||
8.67.13	ye mū̱rdhāna̍ḥ kṣitī̱nām ada̍bdhāsa̱ḥ svaya̍śasaḥ | vra̱tā rakṣa̍nte a̱druha̍ḥ ||
8.67.14	te na̍ ā̱sno vṛkā̍ṇā̱m ādi̍tyāso mu̱moca̍ta | ste̱nam ba̱ddham i̍vādite ||
8.67.15	apo̱ ṣu ṇa̍ i̱yaṁ śaru̱r ādi̍tyā̱ apa̍ durma̱tiḥ | a̱smad e̱tv aja̍ghnuṣī ||
8.67.16	śaśva̱d dhi va̍ḥ sudānava̱ ādi̍tyā ū̱tibhi̍r va̱yam | pu̱rā nū̱nam bu̍bhu̱jmahe̍ ||
8.67.17	śaśva̍nta̱ṁ hi pra̍cetasaḥ prati̱yanta̍ṁ ci̱d ena̍saḥ | devā̍ḥ kṛṇu̱tha jī̱vase̍ ||
8.67.18	tat su no̱ navya̱ṁ sanya̍sa̱ ādi̍tyā̱ yan mumo̍cati | ba̱ndhād ba̱ddham i̍vādite ||
8.67.19	nāsmāka̍m asti̱ tat tara̱ ādi̍tyāso ati̱ṣkade̍ | yū̱yam a̱smabhya̍m mṛḻata ||
8.67.20	mā no̍ he̱tir vi̱vasva̍ta̱ ādi̍tyāḥ kṛ̱trimā̱ śaru̍ḥ | pu̱rā nu ja̱raso̍ vadhīt ||
8.67.21	vi ṣu dveṣo̱ vy a̍ṁha̱tim ādi̍tyāso̱ vi saṁhi̍tam | viṣva̱g vi vṛ̍hatā̱ rapa̍ḥ ||
8.68.1	ā tvā̱ ratha̱ṁ yatho̱taye̍ su̱mnāya̍ vartayāmasi | tu̱vi̱kū̱rmim ṛ̍tī̱ṣaha̱m indra̱ śavi̍ṣṭha̱ satpa̍te ||
8.68.2	tuvi̍śuṣma̱ tuvi̍krato̱ śacī̍vo̱ viśva̍yā mate | ā pa̍prātha mahitva̱nā ||
8.68.3	yasya̍ te mahi̱nā ma̱haḥ pari̍ jmā̱yanta̍m ī̱yatu̍ḥ | hastā̱ vajra̍ṁ hira̱ṇyaya̍m ||
8.68.4	vi̱śvāna̍rasya va̱s pati̱m anā̍natasya̱ śava̍saḥ | evai̍ś ca carṣaṇī̱nām ū̱tī hu̍ve̱ rathā̍nām ||
8.68.5	a̱bhiṣṭa̍ye sa̱dāvṛ̍dha̱ṁ sva̍rmīḻheṣu̱ yaṁ nara̍ḥ | nānā̱ hava̍nta ū̱taye̍ ||
8.68.6	pa̱romā̍tra̱m ṛcī̍ṣama̱m indra̍m u̱graṁ su̱rādha̍sam | īśā̍naṁ ci̱d vasū̍nām ||
8.68.7	taṁ-ta̱m id rādha̍se ma̱ha indra̍ṁ codāmi pī̱taye̍ | yaḥ pū̱rvyām anu̍ṣṭuti̱m īśe̍ kṛṣṭī̱nāṁ nṛ̱tuḥ ||
8.68.8	na yasya̍ te śavasāna sa̱khyam ā̱naṁśa̱ martya̍ḥ | naki̱ḥ śavā̍ṁsi te naśat ||
8.68.9	tvotā̍sa̱s tvā yu̱jāpsu sūrye̍ ma̱had dhana̍m | jaye̍ma pṛ̱tsu va̍jrivaḥ ||
8.68.10	taṁ tvā̍ ya̱jñebhi̍r īmahe̱ taṁ gī̱rbhir gi̍rvaṇastama | indra̱ yathā̍ ci̱d āvi̍tha̱ vāje̍ṣu puru̱māyya̍m ||
8.68.11	yasya̍ te svā̱du sa̱khyaṁ svā̱dvī praṇī̍tir adrivaḥ | ya̱jño vi̍tanta̱sāyya̍ḥ ||
8.68.12	u̱ru ṇa̍s ta̱nve̱3̱̍ tana̍ u̱ru kṣayā̍ya nas kṛdhi | u̱ru ṇo̍ yandhi jī̱vase̍ ||
8.68.13	u̱ruṁ nṛbhya̍ u̱ruṁ gava̍ u̱ruṁ rathā̍ya̱ panthā̍m | de̱vavī̍tim manāmahe ||
8.68.14	upa̍ mā̱ ṣaḍ dvā-dvā̱ nara̱ḥ soma̍sya̱ harṣyā̍ | tiṣṭha̍nti svādurā̱taya̍ḥ ||
8.68.15	ṛ̱jrāv i̍ndro̱ta ā da̍de̱ harī̱ ṛkṣa̍sya sū̱navi̍ | ā̱śva̱me̱dhasya̱ rohi̍tā ||
8.68.16	su̱rathā̍m̐ ātithi̱gve sva̍bhī̱śūm̐r ā̱rkṣe | ā̱śva̱me̱dhe su̱peśa̍saḥ ||
8.68.17	ṣaḻ aśvā̍m̐ ātithi̱gva i̍ndro̱te va̱dhūma̍taḥ | sacā̍ pū̱takra̍tau sanam ||
8.68.18	aiṣu̍ ceta̱d vṛṣa̍ṇvaty a̱ntar ṛ̱jreṣv aru̍ṣī | sva̱bhī̱śuḥ kaśā̍vatī ||
8.68.19	na yu̱ṣme vā̍jabandhavo nini̱tsuś ca̱na martya̍ḥ | a̱va̱dyam adhi̍ dīdharat ||
8.69.1	pra-pra̍ vas tri̱ṣṭubha̱m iṣa̍m ma̱ndadvī̍rā̱yenda̍ve | dhi̱yā vo̍ me̱dhasā̍taye̱ pura̱ṁdhyā vi̍vāsati ||
8.69.2	na̱daṁ va̱ oda̍tīnāṁ na̱daṁ yoyu̍vatīnām | pati̍ṁ vo̱ aghnyā̍nāṁ dhenū̱nām i̍ṣudhyasi ||
8.69.3	tā a̍sya̱ sūda̍dohasa̱ḥ soma̍ṁ śrīṇanti̱ pṛśna̍yaḥ | janma̍n de̱vānā̱ṁ viśa̍s tri̱ṣv ā ro̍ca̱ne di̱vaḥ ||
8.69.4	a̱bhi pra gopa̍tiṁ gi̱rendra̍m arca̱ yathā̍ vi̱de | sū̱nuṁ sa̱tyasya̱ satpa̍tim ||
8.69.5	ā hara̍yaḥ sasṛjri̱re 'ru̍ṣī̱r adhi̍ ba̱rhiṣi̍ | yatrā̱bhi sa̱ṁnavā̍mahe ||
8.69.6	indrā̍ya̱ gāva̍ ā̱śira̍ṁ dudu̱hre va̱jriṇe̱ madhu̍ | yat sī̍m upahva̱re vi̱dat ||
8.69.7	ud yad bra̱dhnasya̍ vi̱ṣṭapa̍ṁ gṛ̱ham indra̍ś ca̱ ganva̍hi | madhva̍ḥ pī̱tvā sa̍cevahi̱ triḥ sa̱pta sakhyu̍ḥ pa̱de ||
8.69.8	arca̍ta̱ prārca̍ta̱ priya̍medhāso̱ arca̍ta | arca̍ntu putra̱kā u̱ta pura̱ṁ na dhṛ̱ṣṇv a̍rcata ||
8.69.9	ava̍ svarāti̱ garga̍ro go̱dhā pari̍ saniṣvaṇat | piṅgā̱ pari̍ caniṣkada̱d indrā̍ya̱ brahmodya̍tam ||
8.69.10	ā yat pata̍nty e̱nya̍ḥ su̱dughā̱ ana̍pasphuraḥ | a̱pa̱sphura̍ṁ gṛbhāyata̱ soma̱m indrā̍ya̱ pāta̍ve ||
8.69.11	apā̱d indro̱ apā̍d a̱gnir viśve̍ de̱vā a̍matsata | varu̍ṇa̱ id i̱ha kṣa̍ya̱t tam āpo̍ a̱bhy a̍nūṣata va̱tsaṁ sa̱ṁśiśva̍rīr iva ||
8.69.12	su̱de̱vo a̍si varuṇa̱ yasya̍ te sa̱pta sindha̍vaḥ | a̱nu̱kṣara̍nti kā̱kuda̍ṁ sū̱rmya̍ṁ suṣi̱rām i̍va ||
8.69.13	yo vyatī̱m̐r aphā̍ṇaya̱t suyu̍ktā̱m̐ upa̍ dā̱śuṣe̍ | ta̱kvo ne̱tā tad id vapu̍r upa̱mā yo amu̍cyata ||
8.69.14	atīd u̍ śa̱kra o̍hata̱ indro̱ viśvā̱ ati̱ dviṣa̍ḥ | bhi̱nat ka̱nīna̍ oda̱nam pa̱cyamā̍nam pa̱ro gi̱rā ||
8.69.15	a̱rbha̱ko na ku̍māra̱ko 'dhi̍ tiṣṭha̱n nava̱ṁ ratha̍m | sa pa̍kṣan mahi̱ṣam mṛ̱gam pi̱tre mā̱tre vi̍bhu̱kratu̍m ||
8.69.16	ā tū su̍śipra dampate̱ ratha̍ṁ tiṣṭhā hira̱ṇyaya̍m | adha̍ dyu̱kṣaṁ sa̍cevahi sa̱hasra̍pādam aru̱ṣaṁ sva̍sti̱gām a̍ne̱hasa̍m ||
8.69.17	taṁ ghe̍m i̱tthā na̍ma̱svina̱ upa̍ sva̱rāja̍m āsate | artha̍ṁ cid asya̱ sudhi̍ta̱ṁ yad eta̍va āva̱rtaya̍nti dā̱vane̍ ||
8.69.18	anu̍ pra̱tnasyauka̍saḥ pri̱yame̍dhāsa eṣām | pūrvā̱m anu̱ praya̍tiṁ vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣo hi̱tapra̍yasa āśata ||
8.70.1	yo rājā̍ carṣaṇī̱nāṁ yātā̱ rathe̍bhi̱r adhri̍guḥ | viśvā̍sāṁ taru̱tā pṛta̍nānā̱ṁ jyeṣṭho̱ yo vṛ̍tra̱hā gṛ̱ṇe ||
8.70.2	indra̱ṁ taṁ śu̍mbha puruhanma̱nn ava̍se̱ yasya̍ dvi̱tā vi̍dha̱rtari̍ | hastā̍ya̱ vajra̱ḥ prati̍ dhāyi darśa̱to ma̱ho di̱ve na sūrya̍ḥ ||
8.70.3	naki̱ṣ ṭaṁ karma̍ṇā naśa̱d yaś ca̱kāra̍ sa̱dāvṛ̍dham | indra̱ṁ na ya̱jñair vi̱śvagū̍rta̱m ṛbhva̍sa̱m adhṛ̍ṣṭaṁ dhṛ̱ṣṇvo̍jasam ||
8.70.4	aṣā̍ḻham u̱gram pṛta̍nāsu sāsa̱hiṁ yasmi̍n ma̱hīr u̍ru̱jraya̍ḥ | saṁ dhe̱navo̱ jāya̍māne anonavu̱r dyāva̱ḥ kṣāmo̍ anonavuḥ ||
8.70.5	yad dyāva̍ indra te śa̱taṁ śa̱tam bhūmī̍r u̱ta syuḥ | na tvā̍ vajrin sa̱hasra̱ṁ sūryā̱ anu̱ na jā̱tam a̍ṣṭa̱ roda̍sī ||
8.70.6	ā pa̍prātha mahi̱nā vṛṣṇyā̍ vṛṣa̱n viśvā̍ śaviṣṭha̱ śava̍sā | a̱smām̐ a̍va maghava̱n goma̍ti vra̱je vajri̍ñ ci̱trābhi̍r ū̱tibhi̍ḥ ||
8.70.7	na sī̱m ade̍va āpa̱d iṣa̍ṁ dīrghāyo̱ martya̍ḥ | eta̍gvā ci̱d ya eta̍śā yu̱yoja̍te̱ harī̱ indro̍ yu̱yoja̍te ||
8.70.8	taṁ vo̍ ma̱ho ma̱hāyya̱m indra̍ṁ dā̱nāya̍ sa̱kṣaṇi̍m | yo gā̱dheṣu̱ ya āra̍ṇeṣu̱ havyo̱ vāje̱ṣv asti̱ havya̍ḥ ||
8.70.9	ud ū̱ ṣu ṇo̍ vaso ma̱he mṛ̱śasva̍ śūra̱ rādha̍se | ud ū̱ ṣu ma̱hyai ma̍ghavan ma̱ghatta̍ya̱ ud i̍ndra̱ śrava̍se ma̱he ||
8.70.10	tvaṁ na̍ indra ṛta̱yus tvā̱nido̱ ni tṛ̍mpasi | madhye̍ vasiṣva tuvinṛmṇo̱rvor ni dā̱saṁ śi̍śnatho̱ hathai̍ḥ ||
8.70.11	a̱nyavra̍ta̱m amā̍nuṣa̱m aya̍jvāna̱m ade̍vayum | ava̱ svaḥ sakhā̍ dudhuvīta̱ parva̍taḥ su̱ghnāya̱ dasyu̱m parva̍taḥ ||
8.70.12	tvaṁ na̍ indrāsā̱ṁ haste̍ śaviṣṭha dā̱vane̍ | dhā̱nānā̱ṁ na saṁ gṛ̍bhāyāsma̱yur dviḥ saṁ gṛ̍bhāyāsma̱yuḥ ||
8.70.13	sakhā̍ya̱ḥ kratu̍m icchata ka̱thā rā̍dhāma śa̱rasya̍ | upa̍stutim bho̱jaḥ sū̱rir yo ahra̍yaḥ ||
8.70.14	bhūri̍bhiḥ samaha̱ ṛṣi̍bhir ba̱rhiṣma̍dbhiḥ staviṣyase | yad i̱ttham eka̍m-eka̱m ic chara̍ va̱tsān pa̍rā̱dada̍ḥ ||
8.70.15	ka̱rṇa̱gṛhyā̍ ma̱ghavā̍ śaurade̱vyo va̱tsaṁ na̍s tri̱bhya āna̍yat | a̱jāṁ sū̱rir na dhāta̍ve ||
8.71.1	tvaṁ no̍ agne̱ maho̍bhiḥ pā̱hi viśva̍syā̱ arā̍teḥ | u̱ta dvi̱ṣo martya̍sya ||
8.71.2	na̱hi ma̱nyuḥ pauru̍ṣeya̱ īśe̱ hi va̍ḥ priyajāta | tvam id a̍si̱ kṣapā̍vān ||
8.71.3	sa no̱ viśve̍bhir de̱vebhi̱r ūrjo̍ napā̱d bhadra̍śoce | ra̱yiṁ de̍hi vi̱śvavā̍ram ||
8.71.4	na tam a̍gne̱ arā̍tayo̱ marta̍ṁ yuvanta rā̱yaḥ | yaṁ trāya̍se dā̱śvāṁsa̍m ||
8.71.5	yaṁ tvaṁ vi̍pra me̱dhasā̍tā̱v agne̍ hi̱noṣi̱ dhanā̍ya | sa tavo̱tī goṣu̱ gantā̍ ||
8.71.6	tvaṁ ra̱yim pu̍ru̱vīra̱m agne̍ dā̱śuṣe̱ martā̍ya | pra ṇo̍ naya̱ vasyo̱ accha̍ ||
8.71.7	u̱ru̱ṣyā ṇo̱ mā parā̍ dā aghāya̱te jā̍tavedaḥ | du̱rā̱dhye̱3̱̍ martā̍ya ||
8.71.8	agne̱ māki̍ṣ ṭe de̱vasya̍ rā̱tim ade̍vo yuyota | tvam ī̍śiṣe̱ vasū̍nām ||
8.71.9	sa no̱ vasva̱ upa̍ mā̱sy ūrjo̍ napā̱n māhi̍nasya | sakhe̍ vaso jari̱tṛbhya̍ḥ ||
8.71.10	acchā̍ naḥ śī̱raśo̍ciṣa̱ṁ giro̍ yantu darśa̱tam | acchā̍ ya̱jñāso̱ nama̍sā purū̱vasu̍m purupraśa̱stam ū̱taye̍ ||
8.71.11	a̱gniṁ sū̱nuṁ saha̍so jā̱tave̍dasaṁ dā̱nāya̱ vāryā̍ṇām | dvi̱tā yo bhūd a̱mṛto̱ martye̱ṣv ā hotā̍ ma̱ndrata̍mo vi̱śi ||
8.71.12	a̱gniṁ vo̍ devaya̱jyayā̱gnim pra̍ya̱ty a̍dhva̱re | a̱gniṁ dhī̱ṣu pra̍tha̱mam a̱gnim arva̍ty a̱gniṁ kṣaitrā̍ya̱ sādha̍se ||
8.71.13	a̱gnir i̱ṣāṁ sa̱khye da̍dātu na̱ īśe̱ yo vāryā̍ṇām | a̱gniṁ to̱ke tana̍ye̱ śaśva̍d īmahe̱ vasu̱ṁ santa̍ṁ tanū̱pām ||
8.71.14	a̱gnim ī̍ḻi̱ṣvāva̍se̱ gāthā̍bhiḥ śī̱raśo̍ciṣam | a̱gniṁ rā̱ye pu̍rumīḻha śru̱taṁ naro̱ 'gniṁ su̍dī̱taye̍ cha̱rdiḥ ||
8.71.15	a̱gniṁ dveṣo̱ yota̱vai no̍ gṛṇīmasy a̱gniṁ śaṁ yoś ca̱ dāta̍ve | viśvā̍su vi̱kṣv a̍vi̱teva̱ havyo̱ bhuva̱d vastu̍r ṛṣū̱ṇām ||
8.72.1	ha̱viṣ kṛ̍ṇudhva̱m ā ga̍mad adhva̱ryur va̍nate̱ puna̍ḥ | vi̱dvām̐ a̍sya pra̱śāsa̍nam ||
8.72.2	ni ti̱gmam a̱bhy a1̱̍ṁśuṁ sīda̱d dhotā̍ ma̱nāv adhi̍ | ju̱ṣā̱ṇo a̍sya sa̱khyam ||
8.72.3	a̱ntar i̍cchanti̱ taṁ jane̍ ru̱dram pa̱ro ma̍nī̱ṣayā̍ | gṛ̱bhṇanti̍ ji̱hvayā̍ sa̱sam ||
8.72.4	jā̱my a̍tītape̱ dhanu̍r vayo̱dhā a̍ruha̱d vana̍m | dṛ̱ṣada̍ṁ ji̱hvayāva̍dhīt ||
8.72.5	cara̍n va̱tso ruśa̍nn i̱ha ni̍dā̱tāra̱ṁ na vi̍ndate | veti̱ stota̍va a̱mbya̍m ||
8.72.6	u̱to nv a̍sya̱ yan ma̱had aśvā̍va̱d yoja̍nam bṛ̱had | dā̱mā ratha̍sya̱ dadṛ̍śe ||
8.72.7	du̱hanti̍ sa̱ptaikā̱m upa̱ dvā pañca̍ sṛjataḥ | tī̱rthe sindho̱r adhi̍ sva̱re ||
8.72.8	ā da̱śabhi̍r vi̱vasva̍ta̱ indra̱ḥ kośa̍m acucyavīt | kheda̍yā tri̱vṛtā̍ di̱vaḥ ||
8.72.9	pari̍ tri̱dhātu̍r adhva̱raṁ jū̱rṇir e̍ti̱ navī̍yasī | madhvā̱ hotā̍ro añjate ||
8.72.10	si̱ñcanti̱ nama̍sāva̱tam u̱ccāca̍kra̱m pari̍jmānam | nī̱cīna̍bāra̱m akṣi̍tam ||
8.72.11	a̱bhyāra̱m id adra̍yo̱ niṣi̍kta̱m puṣka̍re̱ madhu̍ | a̱va̱tasya̍ vi̱sarja̍ne ||
8.72.12	gāva̱ upā̍vatāva̱tam ma̱hī ya̱jñasya̍ ra̱psudā̍ | u̱bhā karṇā̍ hira̱ṇyayā̍ ||
8.72.13	ā su̱te si̍ñcata̱ śriya̱ṁ roda̍syor abhi̱śriya̍m | ra̱sā da̍dhīta vṛṣa̱bham ||
8.72.14	te jā̍nata̱ svam o̱kya1̱̍ṁ saṁ va̱tsāso̱ na mā̱tṛbhi̍ḥ | mi̱tho na̍santa jā̱mibhi̍ḥ ||
8.72.15	upa̱ srakve̍ṣu̱ bapsa̍taḥ kṛṇva̱te dha̱ruṇa̍ṁ di̱vi | indre̍ a̱gnā nama̱ḥ sva̍ḥ ||
8.72.16	adhu̍kṣat pi̱pyuṣī̱m iṣa̱m ūrja̍ṁ sa̱ptapa̍dīm a̱riḥ | sūrya̍sya sa̱pta ra̱śmibhi̍ḥ ||
8.72.17	soma̍sya mitrāvaru̱ṇodi̍tā̱ sūra̱ ā da̍de | tad ātu̍rasya bheṣa̱jam ||
8.72.18	u̱to nv a̍sya̱ yat pa̱daṁ ha̍rya̱tasya̍ nidhā̱nya̍m | pari̱ dyāṁ ji̱hvayā̍tanat ||
8.73.1	ud ī̍rāthām ṛtāya̱te yu̱ñjāthā̍m aśvinā̱ ratha̍m | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.2	ni̱miṣa̍ś ci̱j javī̍yasā̱ rathe̱nā yā̍tam aśvinā | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.3	upa̍ stṛṇīta̱m atra̍ye hi̱mena̍ gha̱rmam a̍śvinā | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.4	kuha̍ stha̱ḥ kuha̍ jagmathu̱ḥ kuha̍ śye̱neva̍ petathuḥ | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.5	yad a̱dya karhi̱ karhi̍ cic chuśrū̱yāta̍m i̱maṁ hava̍m | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.6	a̱śvinā̍ yāma̱hūta̍mā̱ nedi̍ṣṭhaṁ yā̱my āpya̍m | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.7	ava̍nta̱m atra̍ye gṛ̱haṁ kṛ̍ṇu̱taṁ yu̱vam a̍śvinā | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.8	vare̍the a̱gnim ā̱tapo̱ vada̍te va̱lgv atra̍ye | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.9	pra sa̱ptava̍dhrir ā̱śasā̱ dhārā̍m a̱gner a̍śāyata | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.10	i̱hā ga̍taṁ vṛṣaṇvasū śṛṇu̱tam ma̍ i̱maṁ hava̍m | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.11	kim i̱daṁ vā̍m purāṇa̱vaj jara̍tor iva śasyate | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.12	sa̱mā̱naṁ vā̍ṁ sajā̱tya̍ṁ samā̱no bandhu̍r aśvinā | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.13	yo vā̱ṁ rajā̍ṁsy aśvinā̱ ratho̍ vi̱yāti̱ roda̍sī | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.14	ā no̱ gavye̍bhi̱r aśvyai̍ḥ sa̱hasrai̱r upa̍ gacchatam | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.15	mā no̱ gavye̍bhi̱r aśvyai̍ḥ sa̱hasre̍bhi̱r ati̍ khyatam | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.16	a̱ru̱ṇapsu̍r u̱ṣā a̍bhū̱d aka̱r jyoti̍r ṛ̱tāva̍rī | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.17	a̱śvinā̱ su vi̱cāka̍śad vṛ̱kṣam pa̍raśu̱mām̐ i̍va | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.73.18	pura̱ṁ na dhṛ̍ṣṇa̱v ā ru̍ja kṛ̱ṣṇayā̍ bādhi̱to vi̱śā | anti̱ ṣad bhū̍tu vā̱m ava̍ḥ ||
8.74.1	vi̱śo-vi̍śo vo̱ ati̍thiṁ vāja̱yanta̍ḥ purupri̱yam | a̱gniṁ vo̱ durya̱ṁ vaca̍ḥ stu̱ṣe śū̱ṣasya̱ manma̍bhiḥ ||
8.74.2	yaṁ janā̍so ha̱viṣma̍nto mi̱traṁ na sa̱rpirā̍sutim | pra̱śaṁsa̍nti̱ praśa̍stibhiḥ ||
8.74.3	panyā̍ṁsaṁ jā̱tave̍dasa̱ṁ yo de̱vatā̱ty udya̍tā | ha̱vyāny aira̍yad di̱vi ||
8.74.4	āga̍nma vṛtra̱hanta̍ma̱ṁ jyeṣṭha̍m a̱gnim āna̍vam | yasya̍ śru̱tarvā̍ bṛ̱hann ā̱rkṣo anī̍ka̱ edha̍te ||
8.74.5	a̱mṛta̍ṁ jā̱tave̍dasaṁ ti̱ras tamā̍ṁsi darśa̱tam | ghṛ̱tāha̍vana̱m īḍya̍m ||
8.74.6	sa̱bādho̱ yaṁ janā̍ i̱me̱3̱̍ 'gniṁ ha̱vyebhi̱r īḻa̍te | juhvā̍nāso ya̱tasru̍caḥ ||
8.74.7	i̱yaṁ te̱ navya̍sī ma̱tir agne̱ adhā̍yy a̱smad ā | mandra̱ sujā̍ta̱ sukra̱to 'mū̍ra̱ dasmāti̍the ||
8.74.8	sā te̍ agne̱ śaṁta̍mā̱ cani̍ṣṭhā bhavatu pri̱yā | tayā̍ vardhasva̱ suṣṭu̍taḥ ||
8.74.9	sā dyu̱mnair dyu̱mninī̍ bṛ̱had upo̍pa̱ śrava̍si̱ śrava̍ḥ | dadhī̍ta vṛtra̱tūrye̍ ||
8.74.10	aśva̱m id gāṁ ra̍tha̱prāṁ tve̱ṣam indra̱ṁ na satpa̍tim | yasya̱ śravā̍ṁsi̱ tūrva̍tha̱ panya̍m-panyaṁ ca kṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ḥ ||
8.74.11	yaṁ tvā̍ go̱pava̍no gi̱rā cani̍ṣṭhad agne aṅgiraḥ | sa pā̍vaka śrudhī̱ hava̍m ||
8.74.12	yaṁ tvā̱ janā̍sa̱ īḻa̍te sa̱bādho̱ vāja̍sātaye | sa bo̍dhi vṛtra̱tūrye̍ ||
8.74.13	a̱haṁ hu̍vā̱na ā̱rkṣe śru̱tarva̍ṇi mada̱cyuti̍ | śardhā̍ṁsīva stukā̱vinā̍m mṛ̱kṣā śī̱rṣā ca̍tu̱rṇām ||
8.74.14	māṁ ca̱tvāra̍ ā̱śava̱ḥ śavi̍ṣṭhasya dravi̱tnava̍ḥ | su̱rathā̍so a̱bhi prayo̱ vakṣa̱n vayo̱ na tugrya̍m ||
8.74.15	sa̱tyam it tvā̍ mahenadi̱ paru̱ṣṇy ava̍ dediśam | nem ā̍po aśva̱dāta̍ra̱ḥ śavi̍ṣṭhād asti̱ martya̍ḥ ||
8.75.1	yu̱kṣvā hi de̍va̱hūta̍mā̱m̐ aśvā̍m̐ agne ra̱thīr i̍va | ni hotā̍ pū̱rvyaḥ sa̍daḥ ||
8.75.2	u̱ta no̍ deva de̱vām̐ acchā̍ voco vi̱duṣṭa̍raḥ | śrad viśvā̱ vāryā̍ kṛdhi ||
8.75.3	tvaṁ ha̱ yad ya̍viṣṭhya̱ saha̍saḥ sūnav āhuta | ṛ̱tāvā̍ ya̱jñiyo̱ bhuva̍ḥ ||
8.75.4	a̱yam a̱gniḥ sa̍ha̱sriṇo̱ vāja̍sya śa̱tina̱s pati̍ḥ | mū̱rdhā ka̱vī ra̍yī̱ṇām ||
8.75.5	taṁ ne̱mim ṛ̱bhavo̍ ya̱thā na̍masva̱ sahū̍tibhiḥ | nedī̍yo ya̱jñam a̍ṅgiraḥ ||
8.75.6	tasmai̍ nū̱nam a̱bhidya̍ve vā̱cā vi̍rūpa̱ nitya̍yā | vṛṣṇe̍ codasva suṣṭu̱tim ||
8.75.7	kam u̍ ṣvid asya̱ sena̍yā̱gner apā̍kacakṣasaḥ | pa̱ṇiṁ goṣu̍ starāmahe ||
8.75.8	mā no̍ de̱vānā̱ṁ viśa̍ḥ prasnā̱tīr i̍vo̱srāḥ | kṛ̱śaṁ na hā̍su̱r aghnyā̍ḥ ||
8.75.9	mā na̍ḥ samasya dū̱ḍhya1̱̍ḥ pari̍dveṣaso aṁha̱tiḥ | ū̱rmir na nāva̱m ā va̍dhīt ||
8.75.10	nama̍s te agna̱ oja̍se gṛ̱ṇanti̍ deva kṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ḥ | amai̍r a̱mitra̍m ardaya ||
8.75.11	ku̱vit su no̱ gavi̍ṣṭa̱ye 'gne̍ sa̱ṁveṣi̍ṣo ra̱yim | uru̍kṛd u̱ru ṇa̍s kṛdhi ||
8.75.12	mā no̍ a̱smin ma̍hādha̱ne parā̍ varg bhāra̱bhṛd ya̍thā | sa̱ṁvarga̱ṁ saṁ ra̱yiṁ ja̍ya ||
8.75.13	a̱nyam a̱smad bhi̱yā i̱yam agne̱ siṣa̍ktu du̱cchunā̍ | vardhā̍ no̱ ama̍va̱c chava̍ḥ ||
8.75.14	yasyāju̍ṣan nama̱svina̱ḥ śamī̱m adu̍rmakhasya vā | taṁ ghed a̱gnir vṛ̱dhāva̍ti ||
8.75.15	para̍syā̱ adhi̍ sa̱ṁvato 'va̍rām̐ a̱bhy ā ta̍ra | yatrā̱ham asmi̱ tām̐ a̍va ||
8.75.16	vi̱dmā hi te̍ pu̱rā va̱yam agne̍ pi̱tur yathāva̍saḥ | adhā̍ te su̱mnam ī̍mahe ||
8.76.1	i̱maṁ nu mā̱yina̍ṁ huva̱ indra̱m īśā̍na̱m oja̍sā | ma̱rutva̍nta̱ṁ na vṛ̱ñjase̍ ||
8.76.2	a̱yam indro̍ ma̱rutsa̍khā̱ vi vṛ̱trasyā̍bhina̱c chira̍ḥ | vajre̍ṇa śa̱tapa̍rvaṇā ||
8.76.3	vā̱vṛ̱dhā̱no ma̱rutsa̱khendro̱ vi vṛ̱tram ai̍rayat | sṛ̱jan sa̍mu̱driyā̍ a̱paḥ ||
8.76.4	a̱yaṁ ha̱ yena̱ vā i̱daṁ sva̍r ma̱rutva̍tā ji̱tam | indre̍ṇa̱ soma̍pītaye ||
8.76.5	ma̱rutva̍ntam ṛjī̱ṣiṇa̱m oja̍svantaṁ vira̱pśina̍m | indra̍ṁ gī̱rbhir ha̍vāmahe ||
8.76.6	indra̍m pra̱tnena̱ manma̍nā ma̱rutva̍ntaṁ havāmahe | a̱sya soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.76.7	ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra mīḍhva̱ḥ pibā̱ soma̍ṁ śatakrato | a̱smin ya̱jñe pu̍ruṣṭuta ||
8.76.8	tubhyed i̍ndra ma̱rutva̍te su̱tāḥ somā̍so adrivaḥ | hṛ̱dā hū̍yanta u̱kthina̍ḥ ||
8.76.9	pibed i̍ndra ma̱rutsa̍khā su̱taṁ soma̱ṁ divi̍ṣṭiṣu | vajra̱ṁ śiśā̍na̱ oja̍sā ||
8.76.10	u̱ttiṣṭha̱nn oja̍sā sa̱ha pī̱tvī śipre̍ avepayaḥ | soma̍m indra ca̱mū su̱tam ||
8.76.11	anu̍ tvā̱ roda̍sī u̱bhe krakṣa̍māṇam akṛpetām | indra̱ yad da̍syu̱hābha̍vaḥ ||
8.76.12	vāca̍m a̱ṣṭāpa̍dīm a̱haṁ nava̍sraktim ṛta̱spṛśa̍m | indrā̱t pari̍ ta̱nva̍m mame ||
8.77.1	ja̱jñā̱no nu śa̱takra̍tu̱r vi pṛ̍ccha̱d iti̍ mā̱tara̍m | ka u̱grāḥ ke ha̍ śṛṇvire ||
8.77.2	ād ī̍ṁ śava̱sy a̍bravīd aurṇavā̱bham a̍hī̱śuva̍m | te pu̍tra santu ni̱ṣṭura̍ḥ ||
8.77.3	sam it tān vṛ̍tra̱hākhi̍da̱t khe a̱rām̐ i̍va̱ kheda̍yā | pravṛ̍ddho dasyu̱hābha̍vat ||
8.77.4	eka̍yā prati̱dhāpi̍bat sā̱kaṁ sarā̍ṁsi tri̱ṁśata̍m | indra̱ḥ soma̍sya kāṇu̱kā ||
8.77.5	a̱bhi ga̍ndha̱rvam a̍tṛṇad abu̱dhneṣu̱ raja̱ḥsv ā | indro̍ bra̱hmabhya̱ id vṛ̱dhe ||
8.77.6	nir ā̍vidhyad gi̱ribhya̱ ā dhā̱raya̍t pa̱kvam o̍da̱nam | indro̍ bu̱ndaṁ svā̍tatam ||
8.77.7	śa̱tabra̍dhna̱ iṣu̱s tava̍ sa̱hasra̍parṇa̱ eka̱ it | yam i̍ndra cakṛ̱ṣe yuja̍m ||
8.77.8	tena̍ sto̱tṛbhya̱ ā bha̍ra̱ nṛbhyo̱ nāri̍bhyo̱ atta̍ve | sa̱dyo jā̱ta ṛ̍bhuṣṭhira ||
8.77.9	e̱tā cyau̱tnāni̍ te kṛ̱tā varṣi̍ṣṭhāni̱ parī̍ṇasā | hṛ̱dā vī̱ḍv a̍dhārayaḥ ||
8.77.10	viśvet tā viṣṇu̱r ābha̍rad urukra̱mas tveṣi̍taḥ | śa̱tam ma̍hi̱ṣān kṣī̍rapā̱kam o̍da̱naṁ va̍rā̱ham indra̍ emu̱ṣam ||
8.77.11	tu̱vi̱kṣaṁ te̱ sukṛ̍taṁ sū̱maya̱ṁ dhanu̍ḥ sā̱dhur bu̱ndo hi̍ra̱ṇyaya̍ḥ | u̱bhā te̍ bā̱hū raṇyā̱ susa̍ṁskṛta ṛdū̱pe ci̍d ṛdū̱vṛdhā̍ ||
8.78.1	pu̱ro̱ḻāśa̍ṁ no̱ andha̍sa̱ indra̍ sa̱hasra̱m ā bha̍ra | śa̱tā ca̍ śūra̱ gonā̍m ||
8.78.2	ā no̍ bhara̱ vyañja̍na̱ṁ gām aśva̍m a̱bhyañja̍nam | sacā̍ ma̱nā hi̍ra̱ṇyayā̍ ||
8.78.3	u̱ta na̍ḥ karṇa̱śobha̍nā pu̱rūṇi̍ dhṛṣṇa̱v ā bha̍ra | tvaṁ hi śṛ̍ṇvi̱ṣe va̍so ||
8.78.4	nakī̍ṁ vṛdhī̱ka i̍ndra te̱ na su̱ṣā na su̱dā u̱ta | nānyas tvac chū̍ra vā̱ghata̍ḥ ||
8.78.5	nakī̱m indro̱ nika̍rtave̱ na śa̱kraḥ pari̍śaktave | viśva̍ṁ śṛṇoti̱ paśya̍ti ||
8.78.6	sa ma̱nyum martyā̍nā̱m ada̍bdho̱ ni ci̍kīṣate | pu̱rā ni̱daś ci̍kīṣate ||
8.78.7	kratva̱ it pū̱rṇam u̱dara̍ṁ tu̱rasyā̍sti vidha̱taḥ | vṛ̱tra̱ghnaḥ so̍ma̱pāvna̍ḥ ||
8.78.8	tve vasū̍ni̱ saṁga̍tā̱ viśvā̍ ca soma̱ saubha̍gā | su̱dātv apa̍rihvṛtā ||
8.78.9	tvām id ya̍va̱yur mama̱ kāmo̍ ga̱vyur hi̍raṇya̱yuḥ | tvām a̍śva̱yur eṣa̍te ||
8.78.10	taved i̍ndrā̱ham ā̱śasā̱ haste̱ dātra̍ṁ ca̱nā da̍de | di̱nasya̍ vā maghava̱n sambhṛ̍tasya vā pū̱rdhi yava̍sya kā̱śinā̍ ||
8.79.1	a̱yaṁ kṛ̱tnur agṛ̍bhīto viśva̱jid u̱dbhid it soma̍ḥ | ṛṣi̱r vipra̱ḥ kāvye̍na ||
8.79.2	a̱bhy ū̍rṇoti̱ yan na̱gnam bhi̱ṣakti̱ viśva̱ṁ yat tu̱ram | prem a̱ndhaḥ khya̱n niḥ śro̱ṇo bhū̍t ||
8.79.3	tvaṁ so̍ma tanū̱kṛdbhyo̱ dveṣo̍bhyo̱ 'nyakṛ̍tebhyaḥ | u̱ru ya̱ntāsi̱ varū̍tham ||
8.79.4	tvaṁ ci̱ttī tava̱ dakṣai̍r di̱va ā pṛ̍thi̱vyā ṛ̍jīṣin | yāvī̍r a̱ghasya̍ ci̱d dveṣa̍ḥ ||
8.79.5	a̱rthino̱ yanti̱ ced artha̱ṁ gacchā̱n id da̱duṣo̍ rā̱tim | va̱vṛ̱jyus tṛṣya̍ta̱ḥ kāma̍m ||
8.79.6	vi̱dad yat pū̱rvyaṁ na̱ṣṭam ud ī̍m ṛtā̱yum ī̍rayat | prem āyu̍s tārī̱d atī̍rṇam ||
8.79.7	su̱śevo̍ no mṛḻa̱yāku̱r adṛ̍ptakratur avā̱taḥ | bhavā̍ naḥ soma̱ śaṁ hṛ̱de ||
8.79.8	mā na̍ḥ soma̱ saṁ vī̍vijo̱ mā vi bī̍bhiṣathā rājan | mā no̱ hārdi̍ tvi̱ṣā va̍dhīḥ ||
8.79.9	ava̱ yat sve sa̱dhasthe̍ de̱vānā̍ṁ durma̱tīr īkṣe̍ | rāja̱nn apa̱ dviṣa̍ḥ sedha̱ mīḍhvo̱ apa̱ sridha̍ḥ sedha ||
8.80.1	na̱hy a1̱̍nyam ba̱ḻāka̍ram marḍi̱tāra̍ṁ śatakrato | tvaṁ na̍ indra mṛḻaya ||
8.80.2	yo na̱ḥ śaśva̍t pu̱rāvi̱thāmṛ̍dhro̱ vāja̍sātaye | sa tvaṁ na̍ indra mṛḻaya ||
8.80.3	kim a̱ṅga ra̍dhra̱coda̍naḥ sunvā̱nasyā̍vi̱ted a̍si | ku̱vit sv i̍ndra ṇa̱ḥ śaka̍ḥ ||
8.80.4	indra̱ pra ṇo̱ ratha̍m ava pa̱ścāc ci̱t santa̍m adrivaḥ | pu̱rastā̍d enam me kṛdhi ||
8.80.5	hanto̱ nu kim ā̍sase pratha̱maṁ no̱ ratha̍ṁ kṛdhi | u̱pa̱maṁ vā̍ja̱yu śrava̍ḥ ||
8.80.6	avā̍ no vāja̱yuṁ ratha̍ṁ su̱kara̍ṁ te̱ kim it pari̍ | a̱smān su ji̱gyuṣa̍s kṛdhi ||
8.80.7	indra̱ dṛhya̍sva̱ pūr a̍si bha̱drā ta̍ eti niṣkṛ̱tam | i̱yaṁ dhīr ṛ̱tviyā̍vatī ||
8.80.8	mā sī̍m ava̱dya ā bhā̍g u̱rvī kāṣṭhā̍ hi̱taṁ dhana̍m | a̱pāvṛ̍ktā ara̱tnaya̍ḥ ||
8.80.9	tu̱rīya̱ṁ nāma̍ ya̱jñiya̍ṁ ya̱dā kara̱s tad u̍śmasi | ād it pati̍r na ohase ||
8.80.10	avī̍vṛdhad vo amṛtā̱ ama̍ndīd eka̱dyūr de̍vā u̱ta yāś ca̍ devīḥ | tasmā̍ u̱ rādha̍ḥ kṛṇuta praśa̱stam prā̱tar ma̱kṣū dhi̱yāva̍sur jagamyāt ||
8.81.1	ā tū na̍ indra kṣu̱manta̍ṁ ci̱traṁ grā̱bhaṁ saṁ gṛ̍bhāya | ma̱hā̱ha̱stī dakṣi̍ṇena ||
8.81.2	vi̱dmā hi tvā̍ tuvikū̱rmiṁ tu̱vide̍ṣṇaṁ tu̱vīma̍gham | tu̱vi̱mā̱tram avo̍bhiḥ ||
8.81.3	na̱hi tvā̍ śūra de̱vā na martā̍so̱ ditsa̍ntam | bhī̱maṁ na gāṁ vā̱raya̍nte ||
8.81.4	eto̱ nv indra̱ṁ stavā̱meśā̍na̱ṁ vasva̍ḥ sva̱rāja̍m | na rādha̍sā mardhiṣan naḥ ||
8.81.5	pra sto̍ṣa̱d upa̍ gāsiṣa̱c chrava̱t sāma̍ gī̱yamā̍nam | a̱bhi rādha̍sā jugurat ||
8.81.6	ā no̍ bhara̱ dakṣi̍ṇenā̱bhi sa̱vyena̱ pra mṛ̍śa | indra̱ mā no̱ vaso̱r nir bhā̍k ||
8.81.7	upa̍ krama̱svā bha̍ra dhṛṣa̱tā dhṛ̍ṣṇo̱ janā̍nām | adā̍śūṣṭarasya̱ veda̍ḥ ||
8.81.8	indra̱ ya u̱ nu te̱ asti̱ vājo̱ vipre̍bhi̱ḥ sani̍tvaḥ | a̱smābhi̱ḥ su taṁ sa̍nuhi ||
8.81.9	sa̱dyo̱juva̍s te̱ vājā̍ a̱smabhya̍ṁ vi̱śvaśca̍ndrāḥ | vaśai̍ś ca ma̱kṣū ja̍rante ||
8.82.1	ā pra dra̍va parā̱vato̍ 'rvā̱vata̍ś ca vṛtrahan | madhva̱ḥ prati̱ prabha̍rmaṇi ||
8.82.2	tī̱vrāḥ somā̍sa̱ ā ga̍hi su̱tāso̍ mādayi̱ṣṇava̍ḥ | pibā̍ da̱dhṛg yatho̍ci̱ṣe ||
8.82.3	i̱ṣā ma̍nda̱svād u̱ te 'ra̱ṁ varā̍ya ma̱nyave̍ | bhuva̍t ta indra̱ śaṁ hṛ̱de ||
8.82.4	ā tv a̍śatra̱v ā ga̍hi̱ ny u1̱̍kthāni̍ ca hūyase | u̱pa̱me ro̍ca̱ne di̱vaḥ ||
8.82.5	tubhyā̱yam adri̍bhiḥ su̱to gobhi̍ḥ śrī̱to madā̍ya̱ kam | pra soma̍ indra hūyate ||
8.82.6	indra̍ śru̱dhi su me̱ hava̍m a̱sme su̱tasya̱ goma̍taḥ | vi pī̱tiṁ tṛ̱ptim a̍śnuhi ||
8.82.7	ya i̍ndra cama̱seṣv ā soma̍ś ca̱mūṣu̍ te su̱taḥ | pibed a̍sya̱ tvam ī̍śiṣe ||
8.82.8	yo a̱psu ca̱ndramā̍ iva̱ soma̍ś ca̱mūṣu̱ dadṛ̍śe | pibed a̍sya̱ tvam ī̍śiṣe ||
8.82.9	yaṁ te̍ śye̱naḥ pa̱dābha̍rat ti̱ro rajā̱ṁsy aspṛ̍tam | pibed a̍sya̱ tvam ī̍śiṣe ||
8.83.1	de̱vānā̱m id avo̍ ma̱hat tad ā vṛ̍ṇīmahe va̱yam | vṛṣṇā̍m a̱smabhya̍m ū̱taye̍ ||
8.83.2	te na̍ḥ santu̱ yuja̱ḥ sadā̱ varu̍ṇo mi̱tro a̍rya̱mā | vṛ̱dhāsa̍ś ca̱ prace̍tasaḥ ||
8.83.3	ati̍ no viṣpi̱tā pu̱ru nau̱bhir a̱po na pa̍rṣatha | yū̱yam ṛ̱tasya̍ rathyaḥ ||
8.83.4	vā̱maṁ no̍ astv aryaman vā̱maṁ va̍ruṇa̱ śaṁsya̍m | vā̱maṁ hy ā̍vṛṇī̱mahe̍ ||
8.83.5	vā̱masya̱ hi pra̍cetasa̱ īśā̍nāśo riśādasaḥ | nem ā̍dityā a̱ghasya̱ yat ||
8.83.6	va̱yam id va̍ḥ sudānavaḥ kṣi̱yanto̱ yānto̱ adhva̱nn ā | devā̍ vṛ̱dhāya̍ hūmahe ||
8.83.7	adhi̍ na indraiṣā̱ṁ viṣṇo̍ sajā̱tyā̍nām | i̱tā maru̍to̱ aśvi̍nā ||
8.83.8	pra bhrā̍tṛ̱tvaṁ su̍dāna̱vo 'dha̍ dvi̱tā sa̍mā̱nyā | mā̱tur garbhe̍ bharāmahe ||
8.83.9	yū̱yaṁ hi ṣṭhā su̍dānava̱ indra̍jyeṣṭhā a̱bhidya̍vaḥ | adhā̍ cid va u̱ta bru̍ve ||
8.84.1	preṣṭha̍ṁ vo̱ ati̍thiṁ stu̱ṣe mi̱tram i̍va pri̱yam | a̱gniṁ ratha̱ṁ na vedya̍m ||
8.84.2	ka̱vim i̍va̱ prace̍tasa̱ṁ yaṁ de̱vāso̱ adha̍ dvi̱tā | ni martye̍ṣv āda̱dhuḥ ||
8.84.3	tvaṁ ya̍viṣṭha dā̱śuṣo̱ nṝm̐ḥ pā̍hi śṛṇu̱dhī gira̍ḥ | rakṣā̍ to̱kam u̱ta tmanā̍ ||
8.84.4	kayā̍ te agne aṅgira̱ ūrjo̍ napā̱d upa̍stutim | varā̍ya deva ma̱nyave̍ ||
8.84.5	dāśe̍ma̱ kasya̱ mana̍sā ya̱jñasya̍ sahaso yaho | kad u̍ voca i̱daṁ nama̍ḥ ||
8.84.6	adhā̱ tvaṁ hi na̱s karo̱ viśvā̍ a̱smabhya̍ṁ sukṣi̱tīḥ | vāja̍draviṇaso̱ gira̍ḥ ||
8.84.7	kasya̍ nū̱nam parī̍ṇaso̱ dhiyo̍ jinvasi dampate | goṣā̍tā̱ yasya̍ te̱ gira̍ḥ ||
8.84.8	tam ma̍rjayanta su̱kratu̍m puro̱yāvā̍nam ā̱jiṣu̍ | sveṣu̱ kṣaye̍ṣu vā̱jina̍m ||
8.84.9	kṣeti̱ kṣeme̍bhiḥ sā̱dhubhi̱r naki̱r yaṁ ghnanti̱ hanti̱ yaḥ | agne̍ su̱vīra̍ edhate ||
8.85.1	ā me̱ hava̍ṁ nāsa̱tyāśvi̍nā̱ gaccha̍taṁ yu̱vam | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.85.2	i̱mam me̱ stoma̍m aśvine̱mam me̍ śṛṇuta̱ṁ hava̍m | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.85.3	a̱yaṁ vā̱ṁ kṛṣṇo̍ aśvinā̱ hava̍te vājinīvasū | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.85.4	śṛ̱ṇu̱taṁ ja̍ri̱tur hava̱ṁ kṛṣṇa̍sya stuva̱to na̍rā | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.85.5	cha̱rdir ya̍nta̱m adā̍bhya̱ṁ viprā̍ya stuva̱te na̍rā | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.85.6	gaccha̍taṁ dā̱śuṣo̍ gṛ̱ham i̱tthā stu̍va̱to a̍śvinā | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.85.7	yu̱ñjāthā̱ṁ rāsa̍bha̱ṁ rathe̍ vī̱ḍva̍ṅge vṛṣaṇvasū | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.85.8	tri̱va̱ndhu̱reṇa̍ tri̱vṛtā̱ rathe̱nā yā̍tam aśvinā | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.85.9	nū me̱ giro̍ nāsa̱tyāśvi̍nā̱ prāva̍taṁ yu̱vam | madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.86.1	u̱bhā hi da̱srā bhi̱ṣajā̍ mayo̱bhuvo̱bhā dakṣa̍sya̱ vaca̍so babhū̱vathu̍ḥ | tā vā̱ṁ viśva̍ko havate tanūkṛ̱the mā no̱ vi yau̍ṣṭaṁ sa̱khyā mu̱moca̍tam ||
8.86.2	ka̱thā nū̱naṁ vā̱ṁ vima̍nā̱ upa̍ stavad yu̱vaṁ dhiya̍ṁ dadathu̱r vasya̍ïṣṭaye | tā vā̱ṁ viśva̍ko havate tanūkṛ̱the mā no̱ vi yau̍ṣṭaṁ sa̱khyā mu̱moca̍tam ||
8.86.3	yu̱vaṁ hi ṣmā̍ purubhuje̱mam e̍dha̱tuṁ vi̍ṣṇā̱pve̍ da̱dathu̱r vasya̍ïṣṭaye | tā vā̱ṁ viśva̍ko havate tanūkṛ̱the mā no̱ vi yau̍ṣṭaṁ sa̱khyā mu̱moca̍tam ||
8.86.4	u̱ta tyaṁ vī̱raṁ dha̍na̱sām ṛ̍jī̱ṣiṇa̍ṁ dū̱re ci̱t santa̱m ava̍se havāmahe | yasya̱ svādi̍ṣṭhā suma̱tiḥ pi̱tur ya̍thā̱ mā no̱ vi yau̍ṣṭaṁ sa̱khyā mu̱moca̍tam ||
8.86.5	ṛ̱tena̍ de̱vaḥ sa̍vi̱tā śa̍māyata ṛ̱tasya̱ śṛṅga̍m urvi̱yā vi pa̍prathe | ṛ̱taṁ sā̍sāha̱ mahi̍ cit pṛtanya̱to mā no̱ vi yau̍ṣṭaṁ sa̱khyā mu̱moca̍tam ||
8.87.1	dyu̱mnī vā̱ṁ stomo̍ aśvinā̱ krivi̱r na seka̱ ā ga̍tam | madhva̍ḥ su̱tasya̱ sa di̱vi pri̱yo na̍rā pā̱taṁ gau̱rāv i̱veri̍ṇe ||
8.87.2	piba̍taṁ gha̱rmam madhu̍mantam aśvi̱nā ba̱rhiḥ sī̍dataṁ narā | tā ma̍ndasā̱nā manu̍ṣo duro̱ṇa ā ni pā̍ta̱ṁ veda̍sā̱ vaya̍ḥ ||
8.87.3	ā vā̱ṁ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ pri̱yame̍dhā ahūṣata | tā va̱rtir yā̍ta̱m upa̍ vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣo̱ juṣṭa̍ṁ ya̱jñaṁ divi̍ṣṭiṣu ||
8.87.4	piba̍ta̱ṁ soma̱m madhu̍mantam aśvi̱nā ba̱rhiḥ sī̍dataṁ su̱mat | tā vā̍vṛdhā̱nā upa̍ suṣṭu̱tiṁ di̱vo ga̱ntaṁ gau̱rāv i̱veri̍ṇam ||
8.87.5	ā nū̱naṁ yā̍tam aśvi̱nāśve̍bhiḥ pruṣi̱tapsu̍bhiḥ | dasrā̱ hira̍ṇyavartanī śubhas patī pā̱taṁ soma̍m ṛtāvṛdhā ||
