8.87.6	va̱yaṁ hi vā̱ṁ havā̍mahe vipa̱nyavo̱ viprā̍so̱ vāja̍sātaye | tā va̱lgū da̱srā pu̍ru̱daṁsa̍sā dhi̱yāśvi̍nā śru̱ṣṭy ā ga̍tam ||
8.88.1	taṁ vo̍ da̱smam ṛ̍tī̱ṣaha̱ṁ vaso̍r mandā̱nam andha̍saḥ | a̱bhi va̱tsaṁ na svasa̍reṣu dhe̱nava̱ indra̍ṁ gī̱rbhir na̍vāmahe ||
8.88.2	dyu̱kṣaṁ su̱dānu̱ṁ tavi̍ṣībhi̱r āvṛ̍taṁ gi̱riṁ na pu̍ru̱bhoja̍sam | kṣu̱manta̱ṁ vāja̍ṁ śa̱tina̍ṁ saha̱sriṇa̍m ma̱kṣū goma̍ntam īmahe ||
8.88.3	na tvā̍ bṛ̱hanto̱ adra̍yo̱ vara̍nta indra vī̱ḻava̍ḥ | yad ditsa̍si stuva̱te māva̍te̱ vasu̱ naki̱ṣ ṭad ā mi̍nāti te ||
8.88.4	yoddhā̍si̱ kratvā̱ śava̍so̱ta da̱ṁsanā̱ viśvā̍ jā̱tābhi ma̱jmanā̍ | ā tvā̱yam a̱rka ū̱taye̍ vavartati̱ yaṁ gota̍mā̱ ajī̍janan ||
8.88.5	pra hi ri̍ri̱kṣa oja̍sā di̱vo ante̍bhya̱s pari̍ | na tvā̍ vivyāca̱ raja̍ indra̱ pārthi̍va̱m anu̍ sva̱dhāṁ va̍vakṣitha ||
8.88.6	naki̱ḥ pari̍ṣṭir maghavan ma̱ghasya̍ te̱ yad dā̱śuṣe̍ daśa̱syasi̍ | a̱smāka̍m bodhy u̱catha̍sya codi̱tā maṁhi̍ṣṭho̱ vāja̍sātaye ||
8.89.1	bṛ̱had indrā̍ya gāyata̱ maru̍to vṛtra̱haṁta̍mam | yena̱ jyoti̱r aja̍nayann ṛtā̱vṛdho̍ de̱vaṁ de̱vāya̱ jāgṛ̍vi ||
8.89.2	apā̍dhamad a̱bhiśa̍stīr aśasti̱hāthendro̍ dyu̱mny ābha̍vat | de̱vās ta̍ indra sa̱khyāya̍ yemire̱ bṛha̍dbhāno̱ maru̍dgaṇa ||
8.89.3	pra va̱ indrā̍ya bṛha̱te maru̍to̱ brahmā̍rcata | vṛ̱traṁ ha̍nati vṛtra̱hā śa̱takra̍tu̱r vajre̍ṇa śa̱tapa̍rvaṇā ||
8.89.4	a̱bhi pra bha̍ra dhṛṣa̱tā dhṛ̍ṣanmana̱ḥ śrava̍ś cit te asad bṛ̱hat | arṣa̱ntv āpo̱ java̍sā̱ vi mā̱taro̱ hano̍ vṛ̱traṁ jayā̱ sva̍ḥ ||
8.89.5	yaj jāya̍thā apūrvya̱ magha̍van vṛtra̱hatyā̍ya | tat pṛ̍thi̱vīm a̍prathaya̱s tad a̍stabhnā u̱ta dyām ||
8.89.6	tat te̍ ya̱jño a̍jāyata̱ tad a̱rka u̱ta haskṛ̍tiḥ | tad viśva̍m abhi̱bhūr a̍si̱ yaj jā̱taṁ yac ca̱ jantva̍m ||
8.89.7	ā̱māsu̍ pa̱kvam aira̍ya̱ ā sūrya̍ṁ rohayo di̱vi | gha̱rmaṁ na sāma̍n tapatā suvṛ̱ktibhi̱r juṣṭa̱ṁ girva̍ṇase bṛ̱hat ||
8.90.1	ā no̱ viśvā̍su̱ havya̱ indra̍ḥ sa̱matsu̍ bhūṣatu | upa̱ brahmā̍ṇi̱ sava̍nāni vṛtra̱hā pa̍rama̱jyā ṛcī̍ṣamaḥ ||
8.90.2	tvaṁ dā̱tā pra̍tha̱mo rādha̍sām a̱sy asi̍ sa̱tya ī̍śāna̱kṛt | tu̱vi̱dyu̱mnasya̱ yujyā vṛ̍ṇīmahe pu̱trasya̱ śava̍so ma̱haḥ ||
8.90.3	brahmā̍ ta indra girvaṇaḥ kri̱yante̱ ana̍tidbhutā | i̱mā ju̍ṣasva haryaśva̱ yoja̱nendra̱ yā te̱ ama̍nmahi ||
8.90.4	tvaṁ hi sa̱tyo ma̍ghava̱nn anā̍nato vṛ̱trā bhūri̍ nyṛ̱ñjase̍ | sa tvaṁ śa̍viṣṭha vajrahasta dā̱śuṣe̱ 'rvāñca̍ṁ ra̱yim ā kṛ̍dhi ||
8.90.5	tvam i̍ndra ya̱śā a̍sy ṛjī̱ṣī śa̍vasas pate | tvaṁ vṛ̱trāṇi̍ haṁsy apra̱tīny eka̱ id anu̍ttā carṣaṇī̱dhṛtā̍ ||
8.90.6	tam u̍ tvā nū̱nam a̍sura̱ prace̍tasa̱ṁ rādho̍ bhā̱gam i̍vemahe | ma̱hīva̱ kṛtti̍ḥ śara̱ṇā ta̍ indra̱ pra te̍ su̱mnā no̍ aśnavan ||
8.91.1	ka̱nyā̱3̱̍ vār a̍vāya̱tī soma̱m api̍ sru̱tāvi̍dat | asta̱m bhara̍nty abravī̱d indrā̍ya sunavai tvā śa̱krāya̍ sunavai tvā ||
8.91.2	a̱sau ya eṣi̍ vīra̱ko gṛ̱haṁ-gṛ̍haṁ vi̱cāka̍śad | i̱maṁ jambha̍sutam piba dhā̱nāva̍ntaṁ kara̱mbhiṇa̍m apū̱pava̍ntam u̱kthina̍m ||
8.91.3	ā ca̱na tvā̍ cikitsā̱mo 'dhi̍ ca̱na tvā̱ nema̍si | śanai̍r iva śana̱kair i̱vendrā̍yendo̱ pari̍ srava ||
8.91.4	ku̱vic chaka̍t ku̱vit kara̍t ku̱vin no̱ vasya̍sa̱s kara̍t | ku̱vit pa̍ti̱dviṣo̍ ya̱tīr indre̍ṇa sa̱ṁgamā̍mahai ||
8.91.5	i̱māni̱ trīṇi̍ vi̱ṣṭapā̱ tānī̍ndra̱ vi ro̍haya | śira̍s ta̱tasyo̱rvarā̱m ād i̱dam ma̱ upo̱dare̍ ||
8.91.6	a̱sau ca̱ yā na̍ u̱rvarād i̱māṁ ta̱nva1̱̍m mama̍ | atho̍ ta̱tasya̱ yac chira̱ḥ sarvā̱ tā ro̍ma̱śā kṛ̍dhi ||
8.91.7	khe ratha̍sya̱ khe 'na̍sa̱ḥ khe yu̱gasya̍ śatakrato | a̱pā̱lām i̍ndra̱ triṣ pū̱tvy akṛ̍ṇo̱ḥ sūrya̍tvacam ||
8.92.1	pānta̱m ā vo̱ andha̍sa̱ indra̍m a̱bhi pra gā̍yata | vi̱śvā̱sāha̍ṁ śa̱takra̍tu̱m maṁhi̍ṣṭhaṁ carṣaṇī̱nām ||
8.92.2	pu̱ru̱hū̱tam pu̍ruṣṭu̱taṁ gā̍thā̱nya1̱̍ṁ sana̍śrutam | indra̱ iti̍ bravītana ||
8.92.3	indra̱ in no̍ ma̱hānā̍ṁ dā̱tā vājā̍nāṁ nṛ̱tuḥ | ma̱hām̐ a̍bhi̱jñv ā ya̍mat ||
8.92.4	apā̍d u śi̱pry andha̍saḥ su̱dakṣa̍sya praho̱ṣiṇa̍ḥ | indo̱r indro̱ yavā̍śiraḥ ||
8.92.5	tam v a̱bhi prārca̱tendra̱ṁ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ | tad id dhy a̍sya̱ vardha̍nam ||
8.92.6	a̱sya pī̱tvā madā̍nāṁ de̱vo de̱vasyauja̍sā | viśvā̱bhi bhuva̍nā bhuvat ||
8.92.7	tyam u̍ vaḥ satrā̱sāha̱ṁ viśvā̍su gī̱rṣv āya̍tam | ā cyā̍vayasy ū̱taye̍ ||
8.92.8	yu̱dhmaṁ santa̍m ana̱rvāṇa̍ṁ soma̱pām ana̍pacyutam | nara̍m avā̱ryakra̍tum ||
8.92.9	śikṣā̍ ṇa indra rā̱ya ā pu̱ru vi̱dvām̐ ṛ̍cīṣama | avā̍ na̱ḥ pārye̱ dhane̍ ||
8.92.10	ata̍ś cid indra ṇa̱ upā yā̍hi śa̱tavā̍jayā | i̱ṣā sa̱hasra̍vājayā ||
8.92.11	ayā̍ma̱ dhīva̍to̱ dhiyo 'rva̍dbhiḥ śakra godare | jaye̍ma pṛ̱tsu va̍jrivaḥ ||
8.92.12	va̱yam u̍ tvā śatakrato̱ gāvo̱ na yava̍se̱ṣv ā | u̱ktheṣu̍ raṇayāmasi ||
8.92.13	viśvā̱ hi ma̍rtyatva̱nānu̍kā̱mā śa̍takrato | aga̍nma vajrinn ā̱śasa̍ḥ ||
8.92.14	tve su pu̍tra śava̱so 'vṛ̍tra̱n kāma̍kātayaḥ | na tvām i̱ndrāti̍ ricyate ||
8.92.15	sa no̍ vṛṣa̱n sani̍ṣṭhayā̱ saṁ gho̱rayā̍ dravi̱tnvā | dhi̱yāvi̍ḍḍhi̱ pura̍ṁdhyā ||
8.92.16	yas te̍ nū̱naṁ śa̍takrata̱v indra̍ dyu̱mnita̍mo̱ mada̍ḥ | tena̍ nū̱nam made̍ madeḥ ||
8.92.17	yas te̍ ci̱traśra̍vastamo̱ ya i̍ndra vṛtra̱hanta̍maḥ | ya o̍jo̱dāta̍mo̱ mada̍ḥ ||
8.92.18	vi̱dmā hi yas te̍ adriva̱s tvāda̍ttaḥ satya somapāḥ | viśvā̍su dasma kṛ̱ṣṭiṣu̍ ||
8.92.19	indrā̍ya̱ madva̍ne su̱tam pari̍ ṣṭobhantu no̱ gira̍ḥ | a̱rkam a̍rcantu kā̱rava̍ḥ ||
8.92.20	yasmi̱n viśvā̱ adhi̱ śriyo̱ raṇa̍nti sa̱pta sa̱ṁsada̍ḥ | indra̍ṁ su̱te ha̍vāmahe ||
8.92.21	trika̍drukeṣu̱ ceta̍naṁ de̱vāso̍ ya̱jñam a̍tnata | tam id va̍rdhantu no̱ gira̍ḥ ||
8.92.22	ā tvā̍ viśa̱ntv inda̍vaḥ samu̱dram i̍va̱ sindha̍vaḥ | na tvām i̱ndrāti̍ ricyate ||
8.92.23	vi̱vyaktha̍ mahi̱nā vṛ̍ṣan bha̱kṣaṁ soma̍sya jāgṛve | ya i̍ndra ja̱ṭhare̍ṣu te ||
8.92.24	ara̍ṁ ta indra ku̱kṣaye̱ somo̍ bhavatu vṛtrahan | ara̱ṁ dhāma̍bhya̱ inda̍vaḥ ||
8.92.25	ara̱m aśvā̍ya gāyati śru̱taka̍kṣo̱ ara̱ṁ gave̍ | ara̱m indra̍sya̱ dhāmne̍ ||
8.92.26	ara̱ṁ hi ṣma̍ su̱teṣu̍ ṇa̱ḥ some̍ṣv indra̱ bhūṣa̍si | ara̍ṁ te śakra dā̱vane̍ ||
8.92.27	pa̱rā̱kāttā̍c cid adriva̱s tvāṁ na̍kṣanta no̱ gira̍ḥ | ara̍ṁ gamāma te va̱yam ||
8.92.28	e̱vā hy asi̍ vīra̱yur e̱vā śūra̍ u̱ta sthi̱raḥ | e̱vā te̱ rādhya̱m mana̍ḥ ||
8.92.29	e̱vā rā̱tis tu̍vīmagha̱ viśve̍bhir dhāyi dhā̱tṛbhi̍ḥ | adhā̍ cid indra me̱ sacā̍ ||
8.92.30	mo ṣu bra̱hmeva̍ tandra̱yur bhuvo̍ vājānām pate | matsvā̍ su̱tasya̱ goma̍taḥ ||
8.92.31	mā na̍ indrā̱bhy ā̱3̱̍diśa̱ḥ sūro̍ a̱ktuṣv ā ya̍man | tvā yu̱jā va̍nema̱ tat ||
8.92.32	tvayed i̍ndra yu̱jā va̱yam prati̍ bruvīmahi̱ spṛdha̍ḥ | tvam a̱smāka̱ṁ tava̍ smasi ||
8.92.33	tvām id dhi tvā̱yavo̍ 'nu̱nonu̍vata̱ś carā̍n | sakhā̍ya indra kā̱rava̍ḥ ||
8.93.1	ud ghed a̱bhi śru̱tāma̍ghaṁ vṛṣa̱bhaṁ naryā̍pasam | astā̍ram eṣi sūrya ||
8.93.2	nava̱ yo na̍va̱tim puro̍ bi̱bheda̍ bā̱hvo̍jasā | ahi̍ṁ ca vṛtra̱hāva̍dhīt ||
8.93.3	sa na̱ indra̍ḥ śi̱vaḥ sakhāśvā̍va̱d goma̱d yava̍mat | u̱rudhā̍reva dohate ||
8.93.4	yad a̱dya kac ca̍ vṛtrahann u̱dagā̍ a̱bhi sū̍rya | sarva̱ṁ tad i̍ndra te̱ vaśe̍ ||
8.93.5	yad vā̍ pravṛddha satpate̱ na ma̍rā̱ iti̱ manya̍se | u̱to tat sa̱tyam it tava̍ ||
8.93.6	ye somā̍saḥ parā̱vati̱ ye a̍rvā̱vati̍ sunvi̱re | sarvā̱m̐s tām̐ i̍ndra gacchasi ||
8.93.7	tam indra̍ṁ vājayāmasi ma̱he vṛ̱trāya̱ hanta̍ve | sa vṛṣā̍ vṛṣa̱bho bhu̍vat ||
8.93.8	indra̱ḥ sa dāma̍ne kṛ̱ta oji̍ṣṭha̱ḥ sa made̍ hi̱taḥ | dyu̱mnī ślo̱kī sa so̱myaḥ ||
8.93.9	gi̱rā vajro̱ na sambhṛ̍ta̱ḥ saba̍lo̱ ana̍pacyutaḥ | va̱va̱kṣa ṛ̱ṣvo astṛ̍taḥ ||
8.93.10	du̱rge ci̍n naḥ su̱gaṁ kṛ̍dhi gṛṇā̱na i̍ndra girvaṇaḥ | tvaṁ ca̍ maghava̱n vaśa̍ḥ ||
8.93.11	yasya̍ te̱ nū ci̍d ā̱diśa̱ṁ na mi̱nanti̍ sva̱rājya̍m | na de̱vo nādhri̍gu̱r jana̍ḥ ||
8.93.12	adhā̍ te̱ apra̍tiṣkutaṁ de̱vī śuṣma̍ṁ saparyataḥ | u̱bhe su̍śipra̱ roda̍sī ||
8.93.13	tvam e̱tad a̍dhārayaḥ kṛ̱ṣṇāsu̱ rohi̍ṇīṣu ca | paru̍ṣṇīṣu̱ ruśa̱t paya̍ḥ ||
8.93.14	vi yad ahe̱r adha̍ tvi̱ṣo viśve̍ de̱vāso̱ akra̍muḥ | vi̱dan mṛ̱gasya̱ tām̐ ama̍ḥ ||
8.93.15	ād u̍ me niva̱ro bhu̍vad vṛtra̱hādi̍ṣṭa̱ pauṁsya̍m | ajā̍taśatru̱r astṛ̍taḥ ||
8.93.16	śru̱taṁ vo̍ vṛtra̱hanta̍ma̱m pra śardha̍ṁ carṣaṇī̱nām | ā śu̍ṣe̱ rādha̍se ma̱he ||
8.93.17	a̱yā dhi̱yā ca̍ gavya̱yā puru̍ṇāma̱n puru̍ṣṭuta | yat some̍-soma̱ ābha̍vaḥ ||
8.93.18	bo̱dhinma̍nā̱ id a̍stu no vṛtra̱hā bhūryā̍sutiḥ | śṛ̱ṇotu̍ śa̱kra ā̱śiṣa̍m ||
8.93.19	kayā̱ tvaṁ na̍ ū̱tyābhi pra ma̍ndase vṛṣan | kayā̍ sto̱tṛbhya̱ ā bha̍ra ||
8.93.20	kasya̱ vṛṣā̍ su̱te sacā̍ ni̱yutvā̍n vṛṣa̱bho ra̍ṇat | vṛ̱tra̱hā soma̍pītaye ||
8.93.21	a̱bhī ṣu ṇa̱s tvaṁ ra̱yim ma̍ndasā̱naḥ sa̍ha̱sriṇa̍m | pra̱ya̱ntā bo̍dhi dā̱śuṣe̍ ||
8.93.22	patnī̍vantaḥ su̱tā i̱ma u̱śanto̍ yanti vī̱taye̍ | a̱pāṁ jagmi̍r nicumpu̱ṇaḥ ||
8.93.23	i̱ṣṭā hotrā̍ asṛkṣa̱tendra̍ṁ vṛ̱dhāso̍ adhva̱re | acchā̍vabhṛ̱tham oja̍sā ||
8.93.24	i̱ha tyā sa̍dha̱mādyā̱ harī̱ hira̍ṇyakeśyā | vo̱ḻhām a̱bhi prayo̍ hi̱tam ||
8.93.25	tubhya̱ṁ somā̍ḥ su̱tā i̱me stī̱rṇam ba̱rhir vi̍bhāvaso | sto̱tṛbhya̱ indra̱m ā va̍ha ||
8.93.26	ā te̱ dakṣa̱ṁ vi ro̍ca̱nā dadha̱d ratnā̱ vi dā̱śuṣe̍ | sto̱tṛbhya̱ indra̍m arcata ||
8.93.27	ā te̍ dadhāmīndri̱yam u̱kthā viśvā̍ śatakrato | sto̱tṛbhya̍ indra mṛḻaya ||
8.93.28	bha̱dram-bha̍draṁ na̱ ā bha̱reṣa̱m ūrja̍ṁ śatakrato | yad i̍ndra mṛ̱ḻayā̍si naḥ ||
8.93.29	sa no̱ viśvā̱ny ā bha̍ra suvi̱tāni̍ śatakrato | yad i̍ndra mṛ̱ḻayā̍si naḥ ||
8.93.30	tvām id vṛ̍trahantama su̱tāva̍nto havāmahe | yad i̍ndra mṛ̱ḻayā̍si naḥ ||
8.93.31	upa̍ no̱ hari̍bhiḥ su̱taṁ yā̱hi ma̍dānām pate | upa̍ no̱ hari̍bhiḥ su̱tam ||
8.93.32	dvi̱tā yo vṛ̍tra̱hanta̍mo vi̱da indra̍ḥ śa̱takra̍tuḥ | upa̍ no̱ hari̍bhiḥ su̱tam ||
8.93.33	tvaṁ hi vṛ̍trahann eṣām pā̱tā somā̍nā̱m asi̍ | upa̍ no̱ hari̍bhiḥ su̱tam ||
8.93.34	indra̍ i̱ṣe da̍dātu na ṛbhu̱kṣaṇa̍m ṛ̱bhuṁ ra̱yim | vā̱jī da̍dātu vā̱jina̍m ||
8.94.1	gaur dha̍yati ma̱rutā̍ṁ śrava̱syur mā̱tā ma̱ghonā̍m | yu̱ktā vahnī̱ rathā̍nām ||
8.94.2	yasyā̍ de̱vā u̱pasthe̍ vra̱tā viśve̍ dhā̱raya̍nte | sūryā̱māsā̍ dṛ̱śe kam ||
8.94.3	tat su no̱ viśve̍ a̱rya ā sadā̍ gṛṇanti kā̱rava̍ḥ | ma̱ruta̱ḥ soma̍pītaye ||
8.94.4	asti̱ somo̍ a̱yaṁ su̱taḥ piba̍nty asya ma̱ruta̍ḥ | u̱ta sva̱rājo̍ a̱śvinā̍ ||
8.94.5	piba̍nti mi̱tro a̍rya̱mā tanā̍ pū̱tasya̱ varu̍ṇaḥ | tri̱ṣa̱dha̱sthasya̱ jāva̍taḥ ||
8.94.6	u̱to nv a̍sya̱ joṣa̱m ām̐ indra̍ḥ su̱tasya̱ goma̍taḥ | prā̱tar hote̍va matsati ||
8.94.7	kad a̍tviṣanta sū̱raya̍s ti̱ra āpa̍ iva̱ sridha̍ḥ | arṣa̍nti pū̱tada̍kṣasaḥ ||
8.94.8	kad vo̍ a̱dya ma̱hānā̍ṁ de̱vānā̱m avo̍ vṛṇe | tmanā̍ ca da̱smava̍rcasām ||
8.94.9	ā ye viśvā̱ pārthi̍vāni pa̱pratha̍n roca̱nā di̱vaḥ | ma̱ruta̱ḥ soma̍pītaye ||
8.94.10	tyān nu pū̱tada̍kṣaso di̱vo vo̍ maruto huve | a̱sya soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.94.11	tyān nu ye vi roda̍sī tasta̱bhur ma̱ruto̍ huve | a̱sya soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.94.12	tyaṁ nu māru̍taṁ ga̱ṇaṁ gi̍ri̱ṣṭhāṁ vṛṣa̍ṇaṁ huve | a̱sya soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||
8.95.1	ā tvā̱ giro̍ ra̱thīr i̱vāsthu̍ḥ su̱teṣu̍ girvaṇaḥ | a̱bhi tvā̱ sam a̍nūṣa̱tendra̍ va̱tsaṁ na mā̱tara̍ḥ ||
8.95.2	ā tvā̍ śu̱krā a̍cucyavuḥ su̱tāsa̍ indra girvaṇaḥ | pibā̱ tv a1̱̍syāndha̍sa̱ indra̱ viśvā̍su te hi̱tam ||
8.95.3	pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kam indra̍ śye̱nābhṛ̍taṁ su̱tam | tvaṁ hi śaśva̍tīnā̱m patī̱ rājā̍ vi̱śām asi̍ ||
8.95.4	śru̱dhī hava̍ṁ tira̱ścyā indra̱ yas tvā̍ sapa̱ryati̍ | su̱vīrya̍sya̱ goma̍to rā̱yas pū̍rdhi ma̱hām̐ a̍si ||
8.95.5	indra̱ yas te̱ navī̍yasī̱ṁ gira̍m ma̱ndrām ajī̍janat | ci̱ki̱tvinma̍nasa̱ṁ dhiya̍m pra̱tnām ṛ̱tasya̍ pi̱pyuṣī̍m ||
8.95.6	tam u̍ ṣṭavāma̱ yaṁ gira̱ indra̍m u̱kthāni̍ vāvṛ̱dhuḥ | pu̱rūṇy a̍sya̱ pauṁsyā̱ siṣā̍santo vanāmahe ||
8.95.7	eto̱ nv indra̱ṁ stavā̍ma śu̱ddhaṁ śu̱ddhena̱ sāmnā̍ | śu̱ddhair u̱kthair vā̍vṛ̱dhvāṁsa̍ṁ śu̱ddha ā̱śīrvā̍n mamattu ||
8.95.8	indra̍ śu̱ddho na̱ ā ga̍hi śu̱ddhaḥ śu̱ddhābhi̍r ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | śu̱ddho ra̱yiṁ ni dhā̍raya śu̱ddho ma̍maddhi so̱myaḥ ||
8.95.9	indra̍ śu̱ddho hi no̍ ra̱yiṁ śu̱ddho ratnā̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ | śu̱ddho vṛ̱trāṇi̍ jighnase śu̱ddho vāja̍ṁ siṣāsasi ||
8.96.1	a̱smā u̱ṣāsa̱ āti̍ranta̱ yāma̱m indrā̍ya̱ nakta̱m ūrmyā̍ḥ su̱vāca̍ḥ | a̱smā āpo̍ mā̱tara̍ḥ sa̱pta ta̍sthu̱r nṛbhya̱s tarā̍ya̱ sindha̍vaḥ supā̱rāḥ ||
8.96.2	ati̍viddhā vithu̱reṇā̍ ci̱d astrā̱ triḥ sa̱pta sānu̱ saṁhi̍tā girī̱ṇām | na tad de̱vo na martya̍s tuturyā̱d yāni̱ pravṛ̍ddho vṛṣa̱bhaś ca̱kāra̍ ||
8.96.3	indra̍sya̱ vajra̍ āya̱so nimi̍śla̱ indra̍sya bā̱hvor bhūyi̍ṣṭha̱m oja̍ḥ | śī̱rṣann indra̍sya̱ krata̍vo nire̱ka ā̱sann eṣa̍nta̱ śrutyā̍ upā̱ke ||
8.96.4	manye̍ tvā ya̱jñiya̍ṁ ya̱jñiyā̍nā̱m manye̍ tvā̱ cyava̍na̱m acyu̍tānām | manye̍ tvā̱ satva̍nām indra ke̱tum manye̍ tvā vṛṣa̱bhaṁ ca̍rṣaṇī̱nām ||
8.96.5	ā yad vajra̍m bā̱hvor i̍ndra̱ dhatse̍ mada̱cyuta̱m aha̍ye̱ hanta̱vā u̍ | pra parva̍tā̱ ana̍vanta̱ pra gāva̱ḥ pra bra̱hmāṇo̍ abhi̱nakṣa̍nta̱ indra̍m ||
8.96.6	tam u̍ ṣṭavāma̱ ya i̱mā ja̱jāna̱ viśvā̍ jā̱tāny ava̍rāṇy asmāt | indre̍ṇa mi̱traṁ di̍dhiṣema gī̱rbhir upo̱ namo̍bhir vṛṣa̱bhaṁ vi̍śema ||
8.96.7	vṛ̱trasya̍ tvā śva̱sathā̱d īṣa̍māṇā̱ viśve̍ de̱vā a̍jahu̱r ye sakhā̍yaḥ | ma̱rudbhi̍r indra sa̱khyaṁ te̍ a̱stv athe̱mā viśvā̱ḥ pṛta̍nā jayāsi ||
8.96.8	triḥ ṣa̱ṣṭis tvā̍ ma̱ruto̍ vāvṛdhā̱nā u̱srā i̍va rā̱śayo̍ ya̱jñiyā̍saḥ | upa̱ tvema̍ḥ kṛ̱dhi no̍ bhāga̱dheya̱ṁ śuṣma̍ṁ ta e̱nā ha̱viṣā̍ vidhema ||
8.96.9	ti̱gmam āyu̍dham ma̱rutā̱m anī̍ka̱ṁ kas ta̍ indra̱ prati̱ vajra̍ṁ dadharṣa | a̱nā̱yu̱dhāso̱ asu̍rā ade̱vāś ca̱kreṇa̱ tām̐ apa̍ vapa ṛjīṣin ||
8.96.10	ma̱ha u̱grāya̍ ta̱vase̍ suvṛ̱ktim prera̍ya śi̱vata̍māya pa̱śvaḥ | girvā̍hase̱ gira̱ indrā̍ya pū̱rvīr dhe̱hi ta̱nve̍ ku̱vid a̱ṅga veda̍t ||
8.96.11	u̱kthavā̍hase vi̱bhve̍ manī̱ṣāṁ druṇā̱ na pā̱ram ī̍rayā na̱dīnā̍m | ni spṛ̍śa dhi̱yā ta̱nvi̍ śru̱tasya̱ juṣṭa̍tarasya ku̱vid a̱ṅga veda̍t ||
8.96.12	tad vi̍viḍḍhi̱ yat ta̱ indro̱ jujo̍ṣat stu̱hi su̍ṣṭu̱tiṁ nama̱sā vi̍vāsa | upa̍ bhūṣa jarita̱r mā ru̍vaṇyaḥ śrā̱vayā̱ vāca̍ṁ ku̱vid a̱ṅga veda̍t ||
8.96.13	ava̍ dra̱pso a̍ṁśu̱matī̍m atiṣṭhad iyā̱naḥ kṛ̱ṣṇo da̱śabhi̍ḥ sa̱hasrai̍ḥ | āva̱t tam indra̱ḥ śacyā̱ dhama̍nta̱m apa̱ snehi̍tīr nṛ̱maṇā̍ adhatta ||
8.96.14	dra̱psam a̍paśya̱ṁ viṣu̍ṇe̱ cara̍ntam upahva̱re na̱dyo̍ aṁśu̱matyā̍ḥ | nabho̱ na kṛ̱ṣṇam a̍vatasthi̱vāṁsa̱m iṣyā̍mi vo vṛṣaṇo̱ yudhya̍tā̱jau ||
8.96.15	adha̍ dra̱pso a̍ṁśu̱matyā̍ u̱pasthe 'dhā̍rayat ta̱nva̍ṁ titviṣā̱ṇaḥ | viśo̱ ade̍vīr a̱bhy ā̱3̱̍cara̍ntī̱r bṛha̱spati̍nā yu̱jendra̍ḥ sasāhe ||
8.96.16	tvaṁ ha̱ tyat sa̱ptabhyo̱ jāya̍māno 'śa̱trubhyo̍ abhava̱ḥ śatru̍r indra | gū̱ḻhe dyāvā̍pṛthi̱vī anv a̍vindo vibhu̱madbhyo̱ bhuva̍nebhyo̱ raṇa̍ṁ dhāḥ ||
8.96.17	tvaṁ ha̱ tyad a̍pratimā̱nam ojo̱ vajre̍ṇa vajrin dhṛṣi̱to ja̍ghantha | tvaṁ śuṣṇa̱syāvā̍tiro̱ vadha̍trai̱s tvaṁ gā i̍ndra̱ śacyed a̍vindaḥ ||
8.96.18	tvaṁ ha̱ tyad vṛ̍ṣabha carṣaṇī̱nāṁ gha̱no vṛ̱trāṇā̍ṁ tavi̱ṣo ba̍bhūtha | tvaṁ sindhū̍m̐r asṛjas tastabhā̱nān tvam a̱po a̍jayo dā̱sapa̍tnīḥ ||
8.96.19	sa su̱kratū̱ raṇi̍tā̱ yaḥ su̱teṣv anu̍ttamanyu̱r yo ahe̍va re̱vān | ya eka̱ in nary apā̍ṁsi̱ kartā̱ sa vṛ̍tra̱hā pratīd a̱nyam ā̍huḥ ||
8.96.20	sa vṛ̍tra̱hendra̍ś carṣaṇī̱dhṛt taṁ su̍ṣṭu̱tyā havya̍ṁ huvema | sa prā̍vi̱tā ma̱ghavā̍ no 'dhiva̱ktā sa vāja̍sya śrava̱sya̍sya dā̱tā ||
8.96.21	sa vṛ̍tra̱hendra̍ ṛbhu̱kṣāḥ sa̱dyo ja̍jñā̱no havyo̍ babhūva | kṛ̱ṇvann apā̍ṁsi̱ naryā̍ pu̱rūṇi̱ somo̱ na pī̱to havya̱ḥ sakhi̍bhyaḥ ||
8.97.1	yā i̍ndra̱ bhuja̱ ābha̍ra̱ḥ sva̍rvā̱m̐ asu̍rebhyaḥ | sto̱tāra̱m in ma̍ghavann asya vardhaya̱ ye ca̱ tve vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣaḥ ||
8.97.2	yam i̍ndra dadhi̱ṣe tvam aśva̱ṁ gām bhā̱gam avya̍yam | yaja̍māne sunva̱ti dakṣi̍ṇāvati̱ tasmi̱n taṁ dhe̍hi̱ mā pa̱ṇau ||
8.97.3	ya i̍ndra̱ sasty a̍vra̱to̍ 'nu̱ṣvāpa̱m ade̍vayuḥ | svaiḥ ṣa evai̍r mumura̱t poṣya̍ṁ ra̱yiṁ sa̍nu̱tar dhe̍hi̱ taṁ tata̍ḥ ||
8.97.4	yac cha̱krāsi̍ parā̱vati̱ yad a̍rvā̱vati̍ vṛtrahan | ata̍s tvā gī̱rbhir dyu̱gad i̍ndra ke̱śibhi̍ḥ su̱tāvā̱m̐ ā vi̍vāsati ||
8.97.5	yad vāsi̍ roca̱ne di̱vaḥ sa̍mu̱drasyādhi̍ vi̱ṣṭapi̍ | yat pārthi̍ve̱ sada̍ne vṛtrahantama̱ yad a̱ntari̍kṣa̱ ā ga̍hi ||
8.97.6	sa na̱ḥ some̍ṣu somapāḥ su̱teṣu̍ śavasas pate | mā̱daya̍sva̱ rādha̍sā sū̱nṛtā̍va̱tendra̍ rā̱yā parī̍ṇasā ||
8.97.7	mā na̍ indra̱ parā̍ vṛṇa̱g bhavā̍ naḥ sadha̱mādya̍ḥ | tvaṁ na̍ ū̱tī tvam in na̱ āpya̱m mā na̍ indra̱ parā̍ vṛṇak ||
8.97.8	a̱sme i̍ndra̱ sacā̍ su̱te ni ṣa̍dā pī̱taye̱ madhu̍ | kṛ̱dhī ja̍ri̱tre ma̍ghava̱nn avo̍ ma̱had a̱sme i̍ndra̱ sacā̍ su̱te ||
8.97.9	na tvā̍ de̱vāsa̍ āśata̱ na martyā̍so adrivaḥ | viśvā̍ jā̱tāni̱ śava̍sābhi̱bhūr a̍si̱ na tvā̍ de̱vāsa̍ āśata ||
8.97.10	viśvā̱ḥ pṛta̍nā abhi̱bhūta̍ra̱ṁ nara̍ṁ sa̱jūs ta̍takṣu̱r indra̍ṁ jaja̱nuś ca̍ rā̱jase̍ | kratvā̱ vari̍ṣṭha̱ṁ vara̍ ā̱muri̍m u̱togram oji̍ṣṭhaṁ ta̱vasa̍ṁ tara̱svina̍m ||
8.97.11	sam ī̍ṁ re̱bhāso̍ asvara̱nn indra̱ṁ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ | sva̍rpati̱ṁ yad ī̍ṁ vṛ̱dhe dhṛ̱tavra̍to̱ hy oja̍sā̱ sam ū̱tibhi̍ḥ ||
8.97.12	ne̱miṁ na̍manti̱ cakṣa̍sā me̱ṣaṁ viprā̍ abhi̱svarā̍ | su̱dī̱tayo̍ vo a̱druho 'pi̱ karṇe̍ tara̱svina̱ḥ sam ṛkva̍bhiḥ ||
8.97.13	tam indra̍ṁ johavīmi ma̱ghavā̍nam u̱graṁ sa̱trā dadhā̍na̱m apra̍tiṣkuta̱ṁ śavā̍ṁsi | maṁhi̍ṣṭho gī̱rbhir ā ca̍ ya̱jñiyo̍ va̱varta̍d rā̱ye no̱ viśvā̍ su̱pathā̍ kṛṇotu va̱jrī ||
8.97.14	tvam pura̍ indra ci̱kid e̍nā̱ vy oja̍sā śaviṣṭha śakra nāśa̱yadhyai̍ | tvad viśvā̍ni̱ bhuva̍nāni vajri̱n dyāvā̍ rejete pṛthi̱vī ca̍ bhī̱ṣā ||
8.97.15	tan ma̍ ṛ̱tam i̍ndra śūra citra pātv a̱po na va̍jrin duri̱tāti̍ parṣi̱ bhūri̍ | ka̱dā na̍ indra rā̱ya ā da̍śasyer vi̱śvapsnya̍sya spṛha̱yāyya̍sya rājan ||
8.98.1	indrā̍ya̱ sāma̍ gāyata̱ viprā̍ya bṛha̱te bṛ̱hat | dha̱rma̱kṛte̍ vipa̱ścite̍ pana̱syave̍ ||
8.98.2	tvam i̍ndrābhi̱bhūr a̍si̱ tvaṁ sūrya̍m arocayaḥ | vi̱śvaka̍rmā vi̱śvade̍vo ma̱hām̐ a̍si ||
8.98.3	vi̱bhrāja̱ñ jyoti̍ṣā̱ sva1̱̍r aga̍ccho roca̱naṁ di̱vaḥ | de̱vās ta̍ indra sa̱khyāya̍ yemire ||
8.98.4	endra̍ no gadhi pri̱yaḥ sa̍trā̱jid ago̍hyaḥ | gi̱rir na vi̱śvata̍s pṛ̱thuḥ pati̍r di̱vaḥ ||
8.98.5	a̱bhi hi sa̍tya somapā u̱bhe ba̱bhūtha̱ roda̍sī | indrāsi̍ sunva̱to vṛ̱dhaḥ pati̍r di̱vaḥ ||
8.98.6	tvaṁ hi śaśva̍tīnā̱m indra̍ da̱rtā pu̱rām asi̍ | ha̱ntā dasyo̱r mano̍r vṛ̱dhaḥ pati̍r di̱vaḥ ||
8.98.7	adhā̱ hī̍ndra girvaṇa̱ upa̍ tvā̱ kāmā̍n ma̱haḥ sa̍sṛ̱jmahe̍ | u̱deva̱ yanta̍ u̱dabhi̍ḥ ||
8.98.8	vār ṇa tvā̍ ya̱vyābhi̱r vardha̍nti śūra̱ brahmā̍ṇi | vā̱vṛ̱dhvāṁsa̍ṁ cid adrivo di̱ve-di̍ve ||
8.98.9	yu̱ñjanti̱ harī̍ iṣi̱rasya̱ gātha̍yo̱rau ratha̍ u̱ruyu̍ge | i̱ndra̱vāhā̍ vaco̱yujā̍ ||
8.98.10	tvaṁ na̍ i̱ndrā bha̍ra̱m̐ ojo̍ nṛ̱mṇaṁ śa̍takrato vicarṣaṇe | ā vī̱ram pṛ̍tanā̱ṣaha̍m ||
8.98.11	tvaṁ hi na̍ḥ pi̱tā va̍so̱ tvam mā̱tā śa̍takrato ba̱bhūvi̍tha | adhā̍ te su̱mnam ī̍mahe ||
8.98.12	tvāṁ śu̍ṣmin puruhūta vāja̱yanta̱m upa̍ bruve śatakrato | sa no̍ rāsva su̱vīrya̍m ||
8.99.1	tvām i̱dā hyo naro 'pī̍pyan vajri̱n bhūrṇa̍yaḥ | sa i̍ndra̱ stoma̍vāhasām i̱ha śru̱dhy upa̱ svasa̍ra̱m ā ga̍hi ||
8.99.2	matsvā̍ suśipra hariva̱s tad ī̍mahe̱ tve ā bhū̍ṣanti ve̱dhasa̍ḥ | tava̱ śravā̍ṁsy upa̱māny u̱kthyā̍ su̱teṣv i̍ndra girvaṇaḥ ||
8.99.3	śrāya̍nta iva̱ sūrya̱ṁ viśved indra̍sya bhakṣata | vasū̍ni jā̱te jana̍māna̱ oja̍sā̱ prati̍ bhā̱gaṁ na dī̍dhima ||
8.99.4	ana̍rśarātiṁ vasu̱dām upa̍ stuhi bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ | so a̍sya̱ kāma̍ṁ vidha̱to na ro̍ṣati̱ mano̍ dā̱nāya̍ co̱daya̍n ||
8.99.5	tvam i̍ndra̱ pratū̍rtiṣv a̱bhi viśvā̍ asi̱ spṛdha̍ḥ | a̱śa̱sti̱hā ja̍ni̱tā vi̍śva̱tūr a̍si̱ tvaṁ tū̍rya taruṣya̱taḥ ||
8.99.6	anu̍ te̱ śuṣma̍ṁ tu̱raya̍ntam īyatuḥ kṣo̱ṇī śiśu̱ṁ na mā̱tarā̍ | viśvā̍s te̱ spṛdha̍ḥ śnathayanta ma̱nyave̍ vṛ̱traṁ yad i̍ndra̱ tūrva̍si ||
8.99.7	i̱ta ū̱tī vo̍ a̱jara̍m prahe̱tāra̱m apra̍hitam | ā̱śuṁ jetā̍ra̱ṁ hetā̍raṁ ra̱thīta̍ma̱m atū̍rtaṁ tugryā̱vṛdha̍m ||
8.99.8	i̱ṣka̱rtāra̱m ani̍ṣkṛta̱ṁ saha̍skṛtaṁ śa̱tamū̍tiṁ śa̱takra̍tum | sa̱mā̱nam indra̱m ava̍se havāmahe̱ vasa̍vānaṁ vasū̱juva̍m ||
8.100.1	a̱yaṁ ta̍ emi ta̱nvā̍ pu̱rastā̱d viśve̍ de̱vā a̱bhi mā̍ yanti pa̱ścāt | ya̱dā mahya̱ṁ dīdha̍ro bhā̱gam i̱ndrād in mayā̍ kṛṇavo vī̱ryā̍ṇi ||
8.100.2	dadhā̍mi te̱ madhu̍no bha̱kṣam agre̍ hi̱tas te̍ bhā̱gaḥ su̱to a̍stu̱ soma̍ḥ | asa̍ś ca̱ tvaṁ da̍kṣiṇa̱taḥ sakhā̱ me 'dhā̍ vṛ̱trāṇi̍ jaṅghanāva̱ bhūri̍ ||
8.100.3	pra su stoma̍m bharata vāja̱yanta̱ indrā̍ya sa̱tyaṁ yadi̍ sa̱tyam asti̍ | nendro̍ a̱stīti̱ nema̍ u tva āha̱ ka ī̍ṁ dadarśa̱ kam a̱bhi ṣṭa̍vāma ||
8.100.4	a̱yam a̍smi jarita̱ḥ paśya̍ me̱ha viśvā̍ jā̱tāny a̱bhy a̍smi ma̱hnā | ṛ̱tasya̍ mā pra̱diśo̍ vardhayanty ādardi̱ro bhuva̍nā dardarīmi ||
8.100.5	ā yan mā̍ ve̱nā aru̍hann ṛ̱tasya̱m̐ eka̱m āsī̍naṁ harya̱tasya̍ pṛ̱ṣṭhe | mana̍ś cin me hṛ̱da ā praty a̍voca̱d aci̍krada̱ñ chiśu̍manta̱ḥ sakhā̍yaḥ ||
8.100.6	viśvet tā te̱ sava̍neṣu pra̱vācyā̱ yā ca̱kartha̍ maghavann indra sunva̱te | pārā̍vata̱ṁ yat pu̍rusambhṛ̱taṁ vasv a̱pāvṛ̍ṇoḥ śara̱bhāya̱ ṛṣi̍bandhave ||
8.100.7	pra nū̱naṁ dhā̍vatā̱ pṛtha̱ṅ neha yo vo̱ avā̍varīt | ni ṣī̍ṁ vṛ̱trasya̱ marma̍ṇi̱ vajra̱m indro̍ apīpatat ||
8.100.8	mano̍javā̱ aya̍māna āya̱sīm a̍tara̱t pura̍m | diva̍ṁ supa̱rṇo ga̱tvāya̱ soma̍ṁ va̱jriṇa̱ ābha̍rat ||
8.100.9	sa̱mu̱dre a̱ntaḥ śa̍yata u̱dnā vajro̍ a̱bhīvṛ̍taḥ | bhara̍nty asmai sa̱ṁyata̍ḥ pu̱raḥpra̍sravaṇā ba̱lim ||
8.100.10	yad vāg vada̍nty aviceta̱nāni̱ rāṣṭrī̍ de̱vānā̍ṁ niṣa̱sāda̍ ma̱ndrā | cata̍sra̱ ūrja̍ṁ duduhe̱ payā̍ṁsi̱ kva̍ svid asyāḥ para̱maṁ ja̍gāma ||
8.100.11	de̱vīṁ vāca̍m ajanayanta de̱vās tāṁ vi̱śvarū̍pāḥ pa̱śavo̍ vadanti | sā no̍ ma̱ndreṣa̱m ūrja̱ṁ duhā̍nā dhe̱nur vāg a̱smān upa̱ suṣṭu̱taitu̍ ||
8.100.12	sakhe̍ viṣṇo vita̱raṁ vi kra̍masva̱ dyaur de̱hi lo̱kaṁ vajrā̍ya vi̱ṣkabhe̍ | hanā̍va vṛ̱traṁ ri̱ṇacā̍va̱ sindhū̱n indra̍sya yantu prasa̱ve visṛ̍ṣṭāḥ ||
8.101.1	ṛdha̍g i̱tthā sa martya̍ḥ śaśa̱me de̱vatā̍taye | yo nū̱nam mi̱trāvaru̍ṇāv a̱bhiṣṭa̍ya āca̱kre ha̱vyadā̍taye ||
8.101.2	varṣi̍ṣṭhakṣatrā uru̱cakṣa̍sā̱ narā̱ rājā̍nā dīrgha̱śrutta̍mā | tā bā̱hutā̱ na da̱ṁsanā̍ ratharyataḥ sā̱kaṁ sūrya̍sya ra̱śmibhi̍ḥ ||
8.101.3	pra yo vā̍m mitrāvaruṇāji̱ro dū̱to adra̍vat | aya̍ḥśīrṣā̱ made̍raghuḥ ||
8.101.4	na yaḥ sa̱mpṛcche̱ na puna̱r havī̍tave̱ na sa̍ṁvā̱dāya̱ rama̍te | tasmā̍n no a̱dya samṛ̍ter uruṣyatam bā̱hubhyā̍ṁ na uruṣyatam ||
8.101.5	pra mi̱trāya̱ prārya̱mṇe sa̍ca̱thya̍m ṛtāvaso | va̱rū̱thya1̱̍ṁ varu̍ṇe̱ chandya̱ṁ vaca̍ḥ sto̱traṁ rāja̍su gāyata ||
8.101.6	te hi̍nvire aru̱ṇaṁ jenya̱ṁ vasv eka̍m pu̱traṁ ti̍sṝ̱ṇām | te dhāmā̍ny a̱mṛtā̱ martyā̍nā̱m ada̍bdhā a̱bhi ca̍kṣate ||
8.101.7	ā me̱ vacā̱ṁsy udya̍tā dyu̱matta̍māni̱ kartvā̍ | u̱bhā yā̍taṁ nāsatyā sa̱joṣa̍sā̱ prati̍ ha̱vyāni̍ vī̱taye̍ ||
8.101.8	rā̱tiṁ yad vā̍m ara̱kṣasa̱ṁ havā̍mahe yu̱vābhyā̍ṁ vājinīvasū | prācī̱ṁ hotrā̍m prati̱rantā̍v itaṁ narā gṛṇā̱nā ja̱mada̍gninā ||
8.101.9	ā no̍ ya̱jñaṁ di̍vi̱spṛśa̱ṁ vāyo̍ yā̱hi su̱manma̍bhiḥ | a̱ntaḥ pa̱vitra̍ u̱pari̍ śrīṇā̱no̱3̱̍ 'yaṁ śu̱kro a̍yāmi te ||
8.101.10	vety a̍dhva̱ryuḥ pa̱thibhī̱ raji̍ṣṭhai̱ḥ prati̍ ha̱vyāni̍ vī̱taye̍ | adhā̍ niyutva u̱bhaya̍sya naḥ piba̱ śuci̱ṁ soma̱ṁ gavā̍śiram ||
8.101.11	baṇ ma̱hām̐ a̍si sūrya̱ baḻ ā̍ditya ma̱hām̐ a̍si | ma̱has te̍ sa̱to ma̍hi̱mā pa̍nasyate̱ 'ddhā de̍va ma̱hām̐ a̍si ||
8.101.12	baṭ sū̍rya̱ śrava̍sā ma̱hām̐ a̍si sa̱trā de̍va ma̱hām̐ a̍si | ma̱hnā de̱vānā̍m asu̱rya̍ḥ pu̱rohi̍to vi̱bhu jyoti̱r adā̍bhyam ||
8.101.13	i̱yaṁ yā nīcy a̱rkiṇī̍ rū̱pā rohi̍ṇyā kṛ̱tā | ci̱treva̱ praty a̍darśy āya̱ty a1̱̍ntar da̱śasu̍ bā̱huṣu̍ ||
8.101.14	pra̱jā ha̍ ti̱sro a̱tyāya̍m īyu̱r ny a1̱̍nyā a̱rkam a̱bhito̍ viviśre | bṛ̱had dha̍ tasthau̱ bhuva̍neṣv a̱ntaḥ pava̍māno ha̱rita̱ ā vi̍veśa ||
8.101.15	mā̱tā ru̱drāṇā̍ṁ duhi̱tā vasū̍nā̱ṁ svasā̍di̱tyānā̍m a̱mṛta̍sya̱ nābhi̍ḥ | pra nu vo̍caṁ ciki̱tuṣe̱ janā̍ya̱ mā gām anā̍gā̱m adi̍tiṁ vadhiṣṭa ||
8.101.16	va̱co̱vida̱ṁ vāca̍m udī̱raya̍ntī̱ṁ viśvā̍bhir dhī̱bhir u̍pa̱tiṣṭha̍mānām | de̱vīṁ de̱vebhya̱ḥ pary e̱yuṣī̱ṁ gām ā mā̍vṛkta̱ martyo̍ da̱bhrace̍tāḥ ||
8.102.1	tvam a̍gne bṛ̱had vayo̱ dadhā̍si deva dā̱śuṣe̍ | ka̱vir gṛ̱hapa̍ti̱r yuvā̍ ||
8.102.2	sa na̱ īḻā̍nayā sa̱ha de̱vām̐ a̍gne duva̱syuvā̍ | ci̱kid vi̍bhāna̱v ā va̍ha ||
8.102.3	tvayā̍ ha svid yu̱jā va̱yaṁ codi̍ṣṭhena yaviṣṭhya | a̱bhi ṣmo̱ vāja̍sātaye ||
8.102.4	au̱rva̱bhṛ̱gu̱vac chuci̍m apnavāna̱vad ā hu̍ve | a̱gniṁ sa̍mu̱dravā̍sasam ||
8.102.5	hu̱ve vāta̍svanaṁ ka̱vim pa̱rjanya̍krandya̱ṁ saha̍ḥ | a̱gniṁ sa̍mu̱dravā̍sasam ||
8.102.6	ā sa̱vaṁ sa̍vi̱tur ya̍thā̱ bhaga̍syeva bhu̱jiṁ hu̍ve | a̱gniṁ sa̍mu̱dravā̍sasam ||
8.102.7	a̱gniṁ vo̍ vṛ̱dhanta̍m adhva̱rāṇā̍m purū̱tama̍m | acchā̱ naptre̱ saha̍svate ||
8.102.8	a̱yaṁ yathā̍ na ā̱bhuva̱t tvaṣṭā̍ rū̱peva̱ takṣyā̍ | a̱sya kratvā̱ yaśa̍svataḥ ||
8.102.9	a̱yaṁ viśvā̍ a̱bhi śriyo̱ 'gnir de̱veṣu̍ patyate | ā vājai̱r upa̍ no gamat ||
8.102.10	viśve̍ṣām i̱ha stu̍hi̱ hotṝ̍ṇāṁ ya̱śasta̍mam | a̱gniṁ ya̱jñeṣu̍ pū̱rvyam ||
8.102.11	śī̱ram pā̍va̱kaśo̍ciṣa̱ṁ jyeṣṭho̱ yo dame̱ṣv ā | dī̱dāya̍ dīrgha̱śrutta̍maḥ ||
8.102.12	tam arva̍nta̱ṁ na sā̍na̱siṁ gṛ̍ṇī̱hi vi̍pra śu̱ṣmiṇa̍m | mi̱traṁ na yā̍ta̱yajja̍nam ||
8.102.13	upa̍ tvā jā̱mayo̱ giro̱ dedi̍śatīr havi̱ṣkṛta̍ḥ | vā̱yor anī̍ke asthiran ||
8.102.14	yasya̍ tri̱dhātv avṛ̍tam ba̱rhis ta̱sthāv asa̍ṁdinam | āpa̍ś ci̱n ni da̍dhā pa̱dam ||
8.102.15	pa̱daṁ de̱vasya̍ mī̱ḻhuṣo 'nā̍dhṛṣṭābhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | bha̱drā sūrya̍ ivopa̱dṛk ||
8.102.16	agne̍ ghṛ̱tasya̍ dhī̱tibhi̍s tepā̱no de̍va śo̱ciṣā̍ | ā de̱vān va̍kṣi̱ yakṣi̍ ca ||
8.102.17	taṁ tvā̍jananta mā̱tara̍ḥ ka̱viṁ de̱vāso̍ aṅgiraḥ | ha̱vya̱vāha̱m ama̍rtyam ||
8.102.18	prace̍tasaṁ tvā ka̱ve 'gne̍ dū̱taṁ vare̍ṇyam | ha̱vya̱vāha̱ṁ ni ṣe̍dire ||
8.102.19	na̱hi me̱ asty aghnyā̱ na svadhi̍ti̱r vana̍nvati | athai̍tā̱dṛg bha̍rāmi te ||
8.102.20	yad a̍gne̱ kāni̱ kāni̍ ci̱d ā te̱ dārū̍ṇi da̱dhmasi̍ | tā ju̍ṣasva yaviṣṭhya ||
8.102.21	yad atty u̍pa̱jihvi̍kā̱ yad va̱mro a̍ti̱sarpa̍ti | sarva̱ṁ tad a̍stu te ghṛ̱tam ||
8.102.22	a̱gnim indhā̍no̱ mana̍sā̱ dhiya̍ṁ saceta̱ martya̍ḥ | a̱gnim ī̍dhe vi̱vasva̍bhiḥ ||
8.103.1	ada̍rśi gātu̱vitta̍mo̱ yasmi̍n vra̱tāny ā̍da̱dhuḥ | upo̱ ṣu jā̱tam ārya̍sya̱ vardha̍nam a̱gniṁ na̍kṣanta no̱ gira̍ḥ ||
8.103.2	pra daivo̍dāso a̱gnir de̱vām̐ acchā̱ na ma̱jmanā̍ | anu̍ mā̱tara̍m pṛthi̱vīṁ vi vā̍vṛte ta̱sthau nāka̍sya̱ sāna̍vi ||
8.103.3	yasmā̱d reja̍nta kṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ś ca̱rkṛtyā̍ni kṛṇva̱taḥ | sa̱ha̱sra̱sām me̱dhasā̍tāv iva̱ tmanā̱gniṁ dhī̱bhiḥ sa̍paryata ||
8.103.4	pra yaṁ rā̱ye ninī̍ṣasi̱ marto̱ yas te̍ vaso̱ dāśa̍t | sa vī̱raṁ dha̍tte agna ukthaśa̱ṁsina̱ṁ tmanā̍ sahasrapo̱ṣiṇa̍m ||
8.103.5	sa dṛ̱ḻhe ci̍d a̱bhi tṛ̍ṇatti̱ vāja̱m arva̍tā̱ sa dha̍tte̱ akṣi̍ti̱ śrava̍ḥ | tve de̍va̱trā sadā̍ purūvaso̱ viśvā̍ vā̱māni̍ dhīmahi ||
8.103.6	yo viśvā̱ daya̍te̱ vasu̱ hotā̍ ma̱ndro janā̍nām | madho̱r na pātrā̍ pratha̱māny a̍smai̱ pra stomā̍ yanty a̱gnaye̍ ||
8.103.7	aśva̱ṁ na gī̱rbhī ra̱thya̍ṁ su̱dāna̍vo marmṛ̱jyante̍ deva̱yava̍ḥ | u̱bhe to̱ke tana̍ye dasma viśpate̱ parṣi̱ rādho̍ ma̱ghonā̍m ||
8.103.8	pra maṁhi̍ṣṭhāya gāyata ṛ̱tāvne̍ bṛha̱te śu̱kraśo̍ciṣe | upa̍stutāso a̱gnaye̍ ||
8.103.9	ā va̍ṁsate ma̱ghavā̍ vī̱rava̱d yaśa̱ḥ sami̍ddho dyu̱mny āhu̍taḥ | ku̱vin no̍ asya suma̱tir navī̍ya̱sy acchā̱ vāje̍bhir ā̱gama̍t ||
8.103.10	preṣṭha̍m u pri̱yāṇā̍ṁ stu̱hy ā̍sā̱vāti̍thim | a̱gniṁ rathā̍nā̱ṁ yama̍m ||
8.103.11	udi̍tā̱ yo nidi̍tā̱ vedi̍tā̱ vasv ā ya̱jñiyo̍ va̱varta̍ti | du̱ṣṭarā̱ yasya̍ prava̱ṇe normayo̍ dhi̱yā vāja̱ṁ siṣā̍sataḥ ||
8.103.12	mā no̍ hṛṇītā̱m ati̍thi̱r vasu̍r a̱gniḥ pu̍rupraśa̱sta e̱ṣaḥ | yaḥ su̱hotā̍ svadhva̱raḥ ||
8.103.13	mo te ri̍ṣa̱n ye accho̍ktibhir va̱so 'gne̱ kebhi̍ś ci̱d evai̍ḥ | kī̱riś ci̱d dhi tvām īṭṭe̍ dū̱tyā̍ya rā̱taha̍vyaḥ svadhva̱raḥ ||
8.103.14	āgne̍ yāhi ma̱rutsa̍khā ru̱drebhi̱ḥ soma̍pītaye | sobha̍ryā̱ upa̍ suṣṭu̱tim mā̱daya̍sva̱ sva̍rṇare ||
